### [1] [TEMPEST: Temporal Embeddings for Scalable Driver Identification via Angular Margin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06855)
*Kyle Musgrove,Dylan B. Lewis,Sarah Powers,Emma J. Reid,Hector Santos-Villalobos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPEST 是一种基于时序卷积网络和 ArcFace 损失的可扩展驾驶员识别嵌入模型，在大规模车队与严格时序评估下显著优于传统与三元组损失基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的驾驶员识别需要嵌入模型在车队规模增长时仍保持判别性能。现有三元组损失方法在驾驶员池扩大时性能迅速下降，并在严格时序评估下过拟合到特定会话模式，因此亟需一种能实现全局类别级分离、支持动态注册且无需重训练的嵌入方案。

Method: 提出 TEMPEST：采用时序卷积网络（TCN）作为嵌入模型，并用加性角度间隔（ArcFace）损失在归一化角度空间中强化全局类别级分离。模型将 60 秒多模态驾驶窗口映射为 96 维紧凑嵌入，支持无需重训练或分类器重拟合的动态注册。

Result: 在 45 名驾驶员的严格时序评估中，TEMPEST 达到 91.71% Rank-1 准确率，比最佳传统模型高 17.9 个百分点，比最强三元组损失基线高 58.4 个百分点。驾驶员池从 10 增至 45 时性能仅下降 4.3 个百分点，而监督式与无监督三元组损失基线分别下降 22 与 32.5 个百分点。在公开 KIA Soul 数据集上，其会话内与会话间表现分别超最佳传统模型 7.3 与 14.3 个百分点。模型仅 720K 参数、2.80 MB 占用，50 个 epoch 收敛。

Conclusion: TEMPEST 为可扩展的行为驾驶员生物识别建立了严格、可复现的基线，证明 ArcFace 嵌入在规模化与跨会话场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Scalable driver identification requires embedding models that maintain discriminative performance as fleet size grows, yet existing triplet-loss formulations degrade rapidly with driver pool size and overfit to session-specific patterns under rigorous temporal evaluation. We introduce TEMPEST, a Temporal Convolutional Network embedding model trained with an additive angular margin (ArcFace) loss that enforces global class-level separation in a normalized angular space. TEMPEST maps 60-second multimodal driving windows to compact 96-dimensional embeddings, supporting truly dynamic enrollment without any retraining or classifier refitting. Under rigorous temporal evaluation on a 45-driver dataset, TEMPEST achieves 91.71% Rank-1 accuracy, outperforming the best classical model by 17.9 pp and the strongest triplet-loss baseline by 58.4 pp. TEMPEST degrades by only 4.3 pp when growing the subject pool from 10 to 45 drivers, compared to 22 pp and 32.5 pp for supervised and unsupervised triplet-loss baselines, and its cross-session advantage is corroborated on the public KIA Soul dataset, where it outperforms the best classical model by 7.3 pp within-session and 14.3 pp cross-session. With 720K parameters, a 2.80 MB footprint, and 50-epoch convergence, TEMPEST establishes a rigorous, reproducible baseline for scalable behavioral driver biometric identification.

</details>


### [2] [When Does External Guidance Help LLM Reasoning? A Bias-Variance Theory of Guidance-Augmented GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06861)
*Sofia Torres,Gabriel Almeida,Carter Adams,Camila Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GA-GRPO 统一理论框架，将外部引导建模为随机引导算子，证明收敛速率与偏差界，给出最优引导权重闭式解及下界，实验验证理论并节省 GPU 时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法（LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO）虽引入外部引导并报告经验收益，但缺乏收敛速率、偏差界与最优引导权重规则，理论空白亟待填补。

Method: 将外部引导（专家轨迹、自解释、检索思维模式）建模为随机引导算子 G，用于重写问题分布；把策略梯度估计量分析为有偏 on-policy 估计量，偏差由引导分布与策略分布间的全变差引导散度 δ_G 界定。该框架涵盖 vanilla GRPO、LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO 为不同 G 的特例。在平滑性与有界散度假设下推导收敛率、闭式 MSE 最优权重 λ* 及极小极大下界。

Result: 在平滑性与有界散度假设下，GA-GRPO 以 O(1/√T) 收敛到 GRPO 稳定点的 O(δ√T) 邻域；给出闭式最优权重 λ*(T,δ,σ₀²)=σ₀²/(σ₀²+R_max²δ²T)；证明匹配的极小极大下界，说明 Ω(δ²T) 偏差项不可避免。在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base 的九个数学及 OOD 基准上，最优权重 GA-GRPO 匹配或超越 TAPO、LUFFY、ExPO 与 vanilla GRPO，同时减少 31% GPU 时长；八项分析实验验证各理论预测。

Conclusion: GA-GRPO 为外部引导增强的 RLVR 提供了首个统一理论解释，揭示了引导偏差与收敛的权衡关系，并给出可实践的最优权重规则，实验验证了理论与效率优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models, and a recent wave of methods (LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, TAPO) further augments RL with \emph{external guidance} - expert traces, self-explanations, or retrieved thought patterns. Although each method reports empirical gains, none provides convergence rates, bias bounds, or an optimal weighting rule for the guidance signal. We close this gap with \emph{Guidance-Augmented GRPO} (GA-GRPO), a unified theoretical framework that casts external guidance as a stochastic guidance operator G re-writing the question distribution, and analyses the resulting policy-gradient estimator as a biased on-policy estimator whose bias is bounded by the total-variation guidance divergence delta\_G between the guidance-augmented sampling distribution and the policy's own distribution. The framework subsumes vanilla GRPO, LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, and TAPO as special cases obtained by particular choices of G. Under smoothness and bounded-divergence assumptions we prove that GA-GRPO converges at rate O(1/sqrt(T)) to an O(delta sqrt(T))-neighbourhood of the GRPO stationary point, derive the closed-form MSE-optimal guidance weight lambda-star(T, delta, sigma\_0 squared) = sigma\_0 squared / (sigma\_0 squared + R\_max squared delta squared T), and prove a matching minimax lower bound showing the Omega(delta squared T) bias term is unavoidable. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base across nine math and OOD benchmarks confirm that optimal-weight GA-GRPO matches or surpasses TAPO, LUFFY, ExPO, and vanilla GRPO while requiring 31\% fewer GPU-hours, and eight analysis experiments validate each theoretical prediction.

</details>


### [3] [Neutrosophic Ensemble Classification for Uncertainty-Aware Bearing Fault Detection: Evidence from Laboratory and Variable-Speed Industrial Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06880)
*Maikel Leyva-Vazquez,Dayron Rumbaut Rangel,Lorenzo Cevallos-Torres,Alexis Matheu Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将随机森林+XGBoost+逻辑回归集成模型分解为四个中智学不确定性指标（T-hat、F-hat、预测熵I1-hat、决策分歧I2-hat），在CWRU和JNU轴承数据集上评估。结果显示：CWRU校正映射错误后精度极高但误差过少无法分析不确定性；JNU跨工况泛化崩溃（40.64%）；I1-hat与误差关联稳健，但单独的逻辑回归置信度反而更优；时频域模型融合的JS散度指标在分布偏移下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轴承故障检测分类器仅输出标量置信度分数，无法区分"自信的错误"与"真正模糊的预测"；且传统真/假对（F = 1 - T）在构造上代数冗余，缺乏对不确定性来源的细粒度刻画。作者希望用中智学分解来更细致地量化集成模型的不确定性，并检验其在实际工况泛化下的有效性。

Method: 1) 构建Random Forest + XGBoost + Logistic Regression集成模型；2) 将集成输出分解为四个中智学指标：T-hat（顶类证据）、F-hat（最强竞争类证据）、预测熵I1-hat、决策分歧I2-hat；3) 在CWRU和JNU两个轴承基准数据集（600-1000 rpm）上采用留一工况协议评估；4) 额外进行时域+频域模型融合并以Jensen-Shannon散度打分；5) 在CWRU上利用文献验证的轴承故障频率经包络谱解调提取三个可解释特征。

Result: CWRU上校正文件到类映射错误后，集成在四个留出负载中的三个达到100.00%精度（第四个92.27%），误差过少无法做不确定性分析。JNU上留出1000 rpm时精度跌至40.64%，低于多数类基线；逻辑回归（57.91%）泛化远好于树集成。I1-hat对误差的关联性强于T-hat/F-hat，而I2-hat贡献甚微；单独的逻辑回归置信度优于完整分解（已如实报告此边界条件）。时频域模型融合的JS散度优于该模型自身熵（标准划分AURC 0.29 vs. 0.36；更难的单工况复现下0.54 vs. 0.73），是唯一在CWRU与JNU分布偏移对比下方向正确的指标。CWRU上基于故障频率的包络谱三个可解释特征几乎完美区分多数故障类（99.57%）。

Conclusion: 中智学分解虽提供了更细粒度的不确定性刻画，但并未一致优于简单方法：I2-hat几乎无用、完整分解不如单独逻辑回归置信度，说明该方法存在明确的边界条件。最有效的不确定性信号来自跨域（时频）模型融合的JS散度，能正确响应分布偏移。基于物理故障频率的可解释特征在单一数据集上表现接近完美。作者发布了代码、日志和图表以供独立验证，并诚实报告了不利结果。

Abstract: Machine learning classifiers for bearing fault detection produce scalar confidence scores that conflate confident errors with genuinely ambiguous predictions, and the conventional truth/falsity pair (F = 1 - T) is algebraically redundant by construction. We operationalize a refined neutrosophic decomposition of a Random Forest + XGBoost + Logistic Regression ensemble into four indicators -- T-hat (top-class evidence), F-hat (best-competitor evidence), predictive entropy I1-hat, and decision disagreement I2-hat -- evaluated on two bearing benchmarks (CWRU and JNU, 600-1000 rpm) under a leave-one-condition-out protocol. On CWRU, after correcting a file-to-class mapping error, the ensemble reaches 100.00 percent accuracy on three of four held-out loads (92.27 percent on the fourth), leaving too few errors for uncertainty analysis. On JNU, holding out 1000 rpm, accuracy collapses to 40.64 percent, below a majority-class baseline; Logistic Regression (57.91 percent) generalizes far better than the tree ensembles. I1-hat shows a robust association with error beyond T-hat/F-hat, while I2-hat contributes little; standalone Logistic Regression confidence outperforms the full decomposition, a boundary condition we report honestly. Two further results extend this: fusing a time-domain and a frequency-domain model of the same signal and scoring their Jensen-Shannon divergence beats that model own entropy (AURC 0.29 vs. 0.36 on the standard split; 0.54 vs. 0.73 under a harder single-condition reproduction), the only indicator moving correctly under a CWRU-versus-JNU distributional-shift contrast; and, on CWRU alone, literature-verified bearing fault frequencies, correctly demodulated via the envelope spectrum, separate most fault classes almost perfectly (99.57 percent) using three interpretable features. Code, logs, and figures are released for independent verification.

</details>


### [4] [Comparative review of hybrid forecasting models for short-term prediction of building thermal load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06881)
*Nikolaos A. Efkarpidis,Despoina Kothona,Georgios C. Christoforidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对建筑短期热需求预测的不同混合模型进行了对比综述与实证评估，涉及13种混合方法，发现EMD-LSTM-Markov在日前供热与生活热水功率模式预测中精度最高，但对高功率尖峰存在低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑热需求（供热与生活热水）的短期预测对能源系统调度与运行优化至关重要，而单一数据驱动模型往往精度有限，因此需要借助与其他先进技术结合的混合模型来提升预测性能，但现有文献缺乏对这些混合模型的系统对比。

Method: 研究分两步：第一步对文献中已有混合技术进行综述性分析，归纳其输入输出特征、主要优缺点，并总结出常见范式（用元启发式算法或数据驱动模型来辨识基础模型参数）；第二步利用苏格兰家庭历史热需求数据集及历史天气预报数据，对13种现有混合方法的性能进行实证评估。

Result: 在13种混合方法中，经验模态分解-长短期记忆-马尔可夫（EMD-LSTM-Markov）模型对日前供热与生活热水（DHW）需求功率模式的预测精度最高，且能较准确预测局部功率峰值，但会低估高功率涌浪与尖峰。SVM-SA与RF-ISSA-LSTM等方法则倾向于预测出平滑的需求曲线，在快速变化处低估了大部分功率峰值。

Conclusion: 混合模型总体上优于单一模型，其中EMD-LSTM-Markov在建筑短期热需求预测中表现最佳，但在极端高功率事件上仍存在低估问题；不同混合方法在平滑性与峰值捕捉能力上各有取舍，实际应用中需根据对峰值精度的需求进行选择。

Abstract: In this paper, a comparative review of different hybrid models for short-term forecasting of building thermal demand is carried out. Particularly, the assessment tackles the comparison of data-driven models enhanced with other state-of-the-art techniques. At the first step, the existing techniques reported in the literature are analysed. It is concluded that Metaheuristics or a data-driven model are used to identify the parameters of the basic model. The qualitative evaluation includes for each method the input and output features, main advantages and drawbacks. At the second step, an existing dataset of historical thermal demand from Scottish households, as well as historical weather forecasts are utilized to assess additionally the performance of existing hybrid methods. From the assessment of 13 hybrid methods, the Empirical Modal Decomposition - long short-term memory - Markov (EMD-LSTM-Markov) model can predict with the highest accuracy the day-ahead power pattern of heating and domestic hot water (DHW) demands. Though local power peaks are also accurately predicted, high power swells and spikes are underestimated. Other methods, such as Support Vector Machine - Simulated Annealing (SVM-SA) and Random Forest - Improved Sparrow Search Algorithm - LSTM (RF-ISSA-LSTM) predict a smooth pattern of heating and DHW demand profiles with rapid changes underestimating most power peaks.

</details>


### [5] [Learning When to Refine: Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning for Budgeted Neural-Operator PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06883)
*Ange Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将时变PDE神经算子自回归部署中的误差修正问题建模为预算受限的自适应求解问题，提出RV-PI策略改进方法，在浅水与Brusselator基准上取得更优轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子虽能快速替代时变PDE求解，但自回归部署存在"细化分配"难题：预测误差在时空上分布不均，而一条轨迹上可执行的局部修正数量有限。因此需要判断"在何处、何时、花多少预算"进行修正。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：全局Fourier神经算子推进整个场，局部算子提出分块残差修正，集合感知选择器选取细化位置；宏观策略决定何时以及花费剩余预算的多大比例。核心算法RV-PI通过在学习到的PDE求解器上实际展开续跑，评估可行的细化次数，把长时程优势转化为保守策略目标，且仅当留出轨迹误差改善时才接受更新。

Result: 浅水基准（32次干预预算）下，RV-PI三随机种子平均轨迹相对L2误差为0.6910，较仅即时策略改进提升5.37%，较RandomMacro提升2.41%；受力驱动的Brusselator基准（76次预算）下达到0.09954，分别提升2.31%和5.32%。

Conclusion: 在固定细化预算下，局部修正的价值取决于其对自回归轨迹的下游影响，而非仅看即时误差削减，验证了基于展开验证的策略改进的有效性。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for time-dependent PDEs, but autoregressive deployment creates a refinement-allocation problem: prediction errors vary over space and time, while only a finite number of local corrections can be committed along a trajectory. We formulate this as budgeted adaptive neural-operator solving. A global Fourier neural operator advances the full field, a local operator proposes patch-wise residual corrections, and a set-aware selector chooses where to refine. A macro policy decides when and how much of the remaining refinement budget to spend. We introduce rollout-verified policy improvement (RV-PI), which evaluates feasible refinement counts through actual continuation rollouts of the learned PDE solver, converts long-horizon advantages into conservative policy targets, and accepts an update only when held-out trajectory error improves. On the shallow-water benchmark with a 32-intervention budget, RV-PI achieves a three-seed mean trajectory relative L2 error of 0.6910, improving over immediate-only policy improvement by 5.37% and RandomMacro by 2.41%. On the forcing-driven Brusselator benchmark with a 76-intervention budget, RV-PI attains 0.09954, improving over immediate-only policy improvement by 2.31% and RandomMacro by 5.32%. These results show that, under a fixed refinement budget, the value of a local correction depends on its downstream effect on the autoregressive trajectory, not only on its immediate error reduction.

</details>


### [6] [Event-Driven ML Pipeline Orchestration for Manufacturing: An AWS Industry Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06890)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Thomas Cook,Fredaljohn Rohrbaugh,Joseph E. Hernandez,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在汽车制造业中运行三年的面向持续机器学习训练的事件驱动云基础设施的工业经验，实现了72-78%的成本降低，并通过离散事件仿真验证了准入控制与队列排空的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车制造场景下需要跨多个工厂、由制造事件触发的长时间GPU训练任务（物理预测模型与强化学习控制策略的专用模型对），而常开式GPU基础设施成本高昂，因此需要一种事件驱动、可准入控制且成本高效的持续机器学习训练架构。

Method: 系统以Amazon ECS结合EC2 GPU容量提供者、基于SQS的消息传递与死信队列、以及带准入控制的Lambda调度器（强制集群并发上限）构建；由ECS Fargate上的Conductor编排器按周计划启动依赖感知的再训练链；整套基础设施用模块化Terraform以多账户隔离方式编码实现，并辅以离散事件仿真验证调度策略。

Result: 基于40000+次生产训练任务，相较常开GPU基础设施实现72-78%成本降低；离散事件仿真表明准入控制是必要的（朴素调度会丢失65%的任务），且队列排空在延迟上与AWS Step Functions相当，同时消除了每任务的启动开销。

Conclusion: 事件驱动、准入控制与队列排空相结合的架构在汽车制造持续机器学习训练中可显著降本且保持低延迟，作者总结了经验教训并开源了仿真器与Terraform模块骨架。

Abstract: We present an industry experience report on three years of operating an event-driven cloud infrastructure for continuous machine learning training in automotive manufacturing. Our system orchestrates GPU-accelerated training of product-specialized model pairs, a physics prediction model and a reinforcement-learning control policy, across multiple plants, coordinating long-running GPU workloads triggered by manufacturing events. The architecture combines Amazon ECS with EC2 GPU capacity providers, SQS-based messaging with dead-letter queues, and an admission-controlled Lambda dispatcher that enforces cluster concurrency limits. A Conductor orchestrator on ECS Fargate initiates dependency-aware retraining chains on a weekly schedule. The entire infrastructure is codified in modular Terraform with multi-account separation. From 40000+ production training jobs we report a 72-78% cost reduction versus always-on GPU infrastructure. A discrete-event simulation confirms that admission control is necessary (naive dispatch loses 65% of jobs) and that queue-draining matches AWS Step Functions latency while eliminating per-job startup overhead. We provide lessons learned and release the simulator and Terraform module skeletons as open-source artifacts.

</details>


### [7] [Learning from Unreliable Trajectories: Adversarially-Robust Federated Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06918)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在对抗性智能体的联邦强化学习，提出 Robust Async-Fed-Q 算法，在保证协作统计收益的同时容忍对抗污染，并给出首个近乎匹配的上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习中多智能体协作可提升样本效率，但当部分智能体行为对抗、传输任意损坏信息时，能否保留协作的样本效率收益尚不清楚。作者希望理解并解决对抗污染下联邦强化学习的统计代价问题。

Method: 提出 Robust Async-Fed-Q：一种基于 epoch 的联邦学习算法，在智能体端结合 Bellman 最优算子的方差缩减估计，在服务器端采用鲁棒聚合；并扩展至单轨迹 Markov 采样与异构部分覆盖场景。

Result: 建立了高概率有限时间保证，表明该方法在容忍对抗污染的同时保留诚实智能体间的统计收益；对抗智能体影响随每个诚实智能体数据量增加而减小，在无限样本极限下消失。补充了信息论下界，刻画对抗污染不可避免的统计代价，得到首个近乎匹配的上下界。epoch 设计还显著改善了异步采样下联邦 Q-learning 的已知通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为对抗鲁棒联邦强化学习提供了首个近乎紧致的理论刻画，并在通信复杂度上取得改进，同时涵盖更一般的采样与覆盖设定。

Abstract: We study federated reinforcement learning in which multiple agents interact with a common Markov decision process and communicate through a central server to collaboratively learn the optimal state-action value function. Our goal is to understand whether the sample-efficiency benefits of collaboration can be retained when a fraction of the agents behave adversarially and transmit arbitrarily corrupted information. To address this problem, we introduce Robust Async-Fed-Q, an epoch-based federated learning algorithm that combines variance-reduced estimation of the Bellman optimality operator at the agents with robust aggregation at the server. We establish high-probability finite-time guarantees showing that the proposed method preserves the statistical gains of collaboration among the honest agents while tolerating adversarial corruption. In particular, the effect of the adversarial agents decreases as the amount of data collected by each honest agent grows and eventually vanishes in the infinite-sample limit. We complement these guarantees with information-theoretic lower bounds that characterize the unavoidable statistical cost of adversarial corruption, leading to the first nearly matching upper and lower bounds for adversarially robust federated reinforcement learning. We further extend our framework to accommodate single-trajectory Markovian sampling and heterogeneous partial coverage, where different agents may explore different regions of the state-action space and learning relies on their collective coverage. Finally, our epoch-based design substantially improves the best known communication complexity for federated Q-learning under asynchronous sampling.

</details>


### [8] [AttSVD:Prompt-Adaptive Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Attention-Guided SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06927)
*Sara Abdali,Jongwoo Ko,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AttSVD：一种免训练的可解释低秩 KV 缓存压缩方法，沿"特征"轴而非序列轴压缩，在保留全部 token 的前提下，用每个 prompt 自身注意力几何导出的在线截断 SVD 压缩 KV 缓存，最多节省 50% 内存且性能与稠密缓存相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 Transformer 的 KV 缓存随上下文长度线性增长，在长上下文场景下成为显存瓶颈。现有免训练方案多通过逐出低重要性 token 来压缩，但这是沿序列轴不可逆的丢弃操作，会造成信息永久损失。

Method: 作者转而沿"特征"轴压缩：保留所有 token，但以更廉价方式存储。提出 AttSVD——基于每个 prompt 自身注意力几何的在线逐 prompt 截断 SVD，仅保留注意力真正读取的方向，按保留秩成比例降低每个注意力头的持久 KV 内存。针对短/长生成场景分别提出 accumulating 与 streaming 两种解码期缓存策略。并加入两项自适应改进：逐矩阵能量准则，独立确定 logit 空间与注意力质量的大小；注意力感知基截断，仅在注意力真正读取的空间内截断，从而同时保留注意力 logit 与输出。此外，这些因子还能免费提供逐头的可解释性洞察（有效秩与注意力所消耗的几何结构）。

Result: 在多个模型上，于一个 agentic 基准与完整 LongBench 套件上评测，AttSVD 在使用最多 50% KV 缓存内存的情况下，性能与稠密缓存持平。

Conclusion: 沿特征轴的低秩压缩是替代序列轴 token 逐出的可行且有效的 KV 缓存压缩路径；AttSVD 在显著降低内存的同时保持性能，并附带可解释性收益。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache of autoregressive transformers grows linearly with context length and dominates memory at long context. Most training-free remedies evict low-importance tokens, an irreversible choice along the sequence axis. We instead keep every token and store it more cheaply along the "feature" axis. We therefore propose AttSVD, a new "interpretable" low-rank compression whose basis is derived from each prompt's own attention geometry: an online, per-prompt truncated SVD that keeps only the directions attention actually reads, cutting persistent per-head KV memory in proportion to the retained rank. We propose two decode-time caching strategies, accumulating and streaming, for short and long generation regimes. Furthermore, we propose two refinements that make compression adaptive. A per-matrix energy rule sizes the logit space and the attention mass independently. An attention-aware basis truncates only in the spaces attention actually reads, preserving both the attention logits and the attention output. The same factors also provide free, per-head interpretability insights into the effective rank and the geometry attention consumes. Across multiple models, on both an agentic benchmark and the full LongBench suite AttSVD stays on par with the dense cache while using up to 50% of the KV-cache memory.

</details>


### [9] [Near-Optimal Sample Complexity for Recursive Entropic Risk Reinforcement Learning with a Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06931)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了有限折扣MDP在递归熵风险偏好下的值与策略学习样本复杂度，改进基于模型的算法分析，缩小了上下界之间的指数差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决风险敏感强化学习中值与策略学习的样本复杂度问题，尤其是先前工作中上界与下界之间存在指数级差距的不足。

Method: 在可访问MDP生成模型的假设下，对基于模型的风险敏感Q值迭代（MB-RS-QVI）方法进行精细化分析，推导学习最优Q值函数与ε-最优策略的(ε,δ)-PAC保证。

Result: 所得界在有效视界1/(1-γ)上的指数依赖优于现有最优保证，且在S、A、ε、|β|以及|β|/(1-γ)的指数依赖上与已有下界匹配（至对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该分析消除了此前上下界之间的指数级差距，将有效视界上的差距缩小至多项式级别。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the sample complexities of value and policy learning in finite discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) under recursive entropic risk preferences with risk parameter \(β\neq 0\), assuming access to a generative model of the MDP. We provide a refined analysis of model-based risk-sensitive Q-value iteration (MB-RS-QVI), a plug-in model-based method introduced in prior work, and derive \((\varepsilon,δ)\)-PAC guarantees for both learning the optimal \(Q\)-value function and an \(\varepsilon\)-optimal policy. Our bounds improve the exponential dependence on the effective horizon \(1/(1-γ)\) compared with the best existing guarantees for this setting. In particular, they match the existing lower bounds in their exponential dependence on \(|β|/(1-γ)\), as well as in \(S\), \(A\), \(\varepsilon\), and \(|β|\), up to logarithmic factors. Consequently, our analysis removes the exponential gap between the previously known upper and lower bounds, leaving only a polynomial gap in the effective horizon.

</details>


### [10] [QiYao-I: A Manifold Based Foundation Model for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06936)
*Linfeng Wang,Ruitong Zhang,Kai Zhao,Yang Shu,Zhongwen Rao,Meng Wang,Yijie Li,Bin Yang,Chenjun Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QiYao-I，一种基于流形的基础模型，用于不规则多元时间序列预测，通过采样条件时序流形注意力和频率感知的动态变量交互机制，在零样本和少样本设置下取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中，观测往往是不规则采样且各变量间异步记录的，而现有时序基础模型多基于规则采样序列构建，难以泛化到不规则时间间隔和异步的跨变量依赖关系。因此，需要一种能应对不规则多元时序预测挑战的方法。

Method: 提出QiYao-I基础模型：(1) 采样条件时序流形注意力机制，将真实时间戳映射到可学习的时序流形特征空间，并向注意力层注入时序流形偏置，从而捕捉不规则时间间隔与局部采样结构；(2) 频率感知的动态变量交互机制，在异步观测下选择性地进行跨变量消息传递。

Result: 在真实世界不规则多元预测基准上的大量实验表明，QiYao-I相较时序基础模型和端到端不规则预测模型均取得更优性能，并在零样本和少样本设置下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: QiYao-I通过流形建模与频率感知的动态变量交互，有效解决了不规则多元时序预测中的异步与不规则采样问题，具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting is a challenging yet important problem in real-world applications, where observations are often irregularly sampled and asynchronously recorded across variables. Existing time series foundation models are mostly built on regularly sampled sequences, making them difficult to generalize to irregular time intervals and asynchronous cross-variable dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose QiYao-I, a manifold based foundation model for irregular multivariate time series forecasting. Specifically, we introduce a novel sampling-conditioned temporal manifold attention mechanism that maps real timestamps into a learnable temporal manifold feature space and injects temporal manifold biases into attention layers, enabling the model to capture both irregular time intervals and local sampling structures. Further, we propose a dynamic variable interaction mechanism with frequency awareness. It selectively performs cross-variable message passing under asynchronous observations. Extensive experiments on real-world irregular multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that QiYao-I achieves superior performance compared with both time series foundation models and end-to-end irregular forecasting models, showing strong generalization ability in zero-shot and few-shot settings.

</details>


### [11] [Learning to Remember: Distilling Memory Retention for Compact Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06942)
*Nilushika Udayangania,Kishor Nandakishora,Marimuthu Palaniswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemKD（记忆差异知识蒸馏）框架，通过专门的损失函数捕捉教师与学生模型在时间序列子序列上的记忆保持差异，从而训练出紧凑高效的循环神经网络，在多个基准上显著优于现有知识蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络及其变体（如LSTM）在时间序列分析中表现优异，能捕捉复杂序列模式并支持实时评估，但其高计算复杂度和大模型体积难以部署在可穿戴设备、边缘计算等资源受限环境中。知识蒸馏可将复杂教师模型的知识迁移到小型学生模型，在降低计算需求的同时保持高性能。然而，现有知识蒸馏方法多针对计算机视觉任务设计，忽略了时间序列模型特有的时间依赖性和记忆保持特性。

Method: 提出名为MemKD（Memory-Discrepancy Knowledge Distillation，记忆差异知识蒸馏）的新型知识蒸馏框架。该方法利用一种专门的损失函数，捕捉教师模型与学生模型在时间序列数据中各子序列上的记忆保持差异，确保学生模型有效模仿教师模型的行为，从而构建适合实时时间序列分析任务的紧凑高性能循环神经网络。

Result: 在扩展的时间序列基准上进行了实验并提供了深入的理论分析。实验表明，MemKD显著优于当前最先进的知识蒸馏方法，并且在多种压缩水平下均能匹配教师模型的性能，在参数量和内存占用方面实现显著降低，且精度损失不明显。

Conclusion: MemKD通过关注时间序列中特有的记忆保持差异，弥补了现有知识蒸馏方法的不足，能够有效生成紧凑、高性能的循环神经网络，适用于实时时间序列分析，并可在多种压缩水平下保持与教师模型相当的性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models, particularly recurrent neural networks and their variants, such as long short-term memory, have significantly advanced time series analysis. These models capture complex, sequential patterns in time series, enabling real-time assessments. However, their high computational complexity and large model sizes pose challenges for deployment in resource-constrained environments, such as wearable devices and edge computing platforms. Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a solution by transferring knowledge from a large, complex model (teacher) to a smaller, more efficient model (student), thereby retaining high performance while reducing computational demands. Current KD methods, originally designed for computer vision tasks, neglect the unique temporal dependencies and memory retention characteristics of time series models. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel KD framework termed Memory-Discrepancy Knowledge Distillation (MemKD). MemKD leverages a specialized loss function to capture memory retention discrepancies between the teacher and student models across subsequences within time series data, ensuring that the student model effectively mimics the teacher's behaviour. This approach facilitates the development of compact, high-performing recurrent neural networks suitable for real-time, time series analysis tasks. We provide additional experiments, in-depth theoretical analysis, and insights into the proposed framework across extended time series benchmarks. Our experiments demonstrate that MemKD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art KD methods. Additionally, we demonstrate that it can match the teacher model's performance across a wide range of compression levels, achieving notable reductions in parameter count and memory usage without a significant loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [12] [Learning to Decide, Not to Reason: Parameter-Efficient Decision Operators via Low-Rank Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06950)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DecisionSteer，一种通过行为克隆训练的 System-1 决策算子，以约 1/100 的参数成本匹配甚至超越强化学习训练的百万级技能算子，并将长链思考压缩为少 token 决策且不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向冻结语言模型注入技能通常需要百万级参数和强化学习流程，成本高昂、训练困难。作者希望找到更便宜、更易训练的技能注入方式，并探究先前算子难以训练的根源。

Method: 使用行为克隆（behavior cloning）训练 System-1 决策算子：默认算子仅 330K 参数，另有 rank-4 的 23K 参数变体；架构采用共享低秩主干加零初始化输出投影，使梯度在第一步即可流动。通过梯度流探针、logit-lens 探针和跨任务/跨主干迁移实验进行验证。

Result: 330K 算子匹配 1.33M 强化学习算子，将 3,685-token 推理压缩为 6-token 决策而无精度损失；23K rank-4 变体为最强公开算子的 1/58，足以应对 SearchQA 并接近满足 LiveMath。方法在 5 个任务、3 个主干上迁移，OOD 增益在训练后数月发布的 LiveMath 上仍保持；57 个 LiveMath 点中 23 个优于基模型 best-of-8；增益与基模型 headroom 相关，技能可近似线性组合、插值与热插拔。

Conclusion: 与先前工作的差距主要在于可训练性，由初始化与架构共同决定：先前算子的初始化在第一步使两个大因子矩阵梯度为零，而本文零初始化输出投影可立即获得梯度。增益并非链式思考压缩，而是放大模型既有的后层答案通路。

Abstract: Injecting skills into a frozen language model currently costs a million parameters and a reinforcement-learning pipeline. We introduce \method{}, a System-1 decision operator trained by behavior cloning that lowers this cost by roughly two orders of magnitude. The default operator uses 330K parameters to match a 1.33M-parameter operator trained with reinforcement learning, exceeds or achieve comparable performance, while collapsing 3,685-token deliberation into a 6-token decision with no loss in accuracy. A rank-4 variant with 23K parameters, 1/58 of the strongest published skill operator, suffices for SearchQA and near-suffices for LiveMath, where higher rank still helps; the same recipe transfers across five tasks and three backbones, with out-of-distribution gains persisting on LiveMath problems released months after training. The gap to prior work is trainability, and it is set jointly by initialization and architecture: the initialization of prior operators zeroes the gradient of both large factor matrices at the first optimization step, whereas our zero-initialized output projection inside a shared low-rank backbone receives a gradient immediately, which a gradient-flow probe confirms directly. The gain isn't chain-of-thought compression: 23 of 57 LiveMath points beat the base model's best-of-8 sampling, and a logit-lens probe shows the operator amplifies the answer along the model's existing late-layer pathway, not writing it earlier. Gains track the base model's headroom across 13 base--task pairs, and skills compose as approximately linear operators that can be added, interpolated, and hot-swapped at inference time. Code on https://github.com/rlisml/decisionsteer.

</details>


### [13] [Do Neural PDE Solvers Learn the Right Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06952)
*Haonan Li,Yue Song,Bin Yang,Kaihong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种评估框架，通过考察误差形成、集合几何与极端事件三个方面，揭示神经PDE求解器在预测分数之外的动力学保真度局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE求解器虽能实现低预测误差，但预测分数仅衡量与参考解的一致性，无法反映误差如何累积、邻近状态如何发散以及极端事件如何产生，因此需要新的评估方法来检验模型是否真正再现系统动力学。

Method: 提出评估框架，针对确定性与随机神经求解器，通过演化邻近初始状态的集合，并与直接数值模拟对比，从误差形成、集合几何和极端事件三个互补方面评估所学动力学。

Result: 在二维Kolmogorov流上的实验显示：较小的轨迹误差可能反映较弱的误差放大而非更准确的局部更新；模型可匹配集合整体扩散与有效维度，却无法捕捉邻近状态发散的空间方向；匹配总体事件频率可能掩盖对持续性极端事件的预测失败。

Conclusion: 预测精度的提升不必然意味着动力学保真度的提升；该框架使这一区别可测量，为评估神经PDE求解器是否更好捕捉底层动力学提供了具体标准。

Abstract: Neural PDE solvers can achieve low prediction errors, but do they reproduce the dynamics of the systems they model? Prediction scores alone offer an incomplete answer: they measure agreement with reference solutions but provide limited insight into how errors accumulate, nearby states diverge, or extreme events arise. We propose an evaluation framework that directly examines these behaviors in deterministic and stochastic neural solvers. By evolving ensembles of nearby initial states and comparing them with direct numerical simulation, we assess three complementary aspects of learned dynamics: error formation, ensemble geometry, and extreme events. Experiments on two-dimensional Kolmogorov flow reveal limitations that conventional scores can obscure. Smaller trajectory errors can reflect weaker error amplification despite less accurate local updates. Models can match an ensemble's overall spread and effective dimension while failing to capture the spatial directions where nearby states diverge. Similarly, matching overall event frequencies can conceal failures to predict persistent extreme events. These findings show that improved prediction accuracy does not necessarily imply greater dynamical fidelity. Our framework makes this distinction measurable, providing concrete criteria for evaluating whether advances in neural PDE solvers better capture the underlying dynamics.

</details>


### [14] [EVFormer: An Egocentric Vision-EMG Bidirectional Attention Model for Bimanual Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06970)
*JiaCheng Ge,SiYu Zhang,ShengJie Li,XinTong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVFormer是一个融合RGB图像与200ms双边腕部sEMG的多模态框架，用于估计44个手指和腕关节角度，在单被试可行性研究中取得11.482度MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心双手姿态估计对虚拟交互、可穿戴控制和康复很重要，但视觉观测常因自遮挡、手-手接触和物体操作而退化，单一视觉模态难以应对，因此需要引入额外模态信息。

Method: EVFormer分别编码视觉空间特征和sEMG时间特征，通过顺序双向交叉注意力实现跨模态信息交换，并采用特征级门控融合整合两种模态；使用一段同步公开EgoEMG记录，按时间顺序划分训练、验证和测试集。

Result: 在296个测试样本上，EVFormer平均绝对误差为11.482度，优于仅视觉、仅sEMG、晚期融合和训练均值基线（13.228-13.610度），相对仅视觉降低13.20%，相对晚期融合降低14.23%，并在五类手势中的四类取得最低误差。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明自我中心视觉与sEMG的特征级交互可改善双手姿态估计，但跨被试、跨记录会话、传感器位置和真实交互条件下的泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Egocentric bimanual hand pose estimation is important for virtual interaction, wearable control, and rehabilitation, but visual observations are often degraded by self-occlusion, hand-hand contact, and object manipulation. We propose EVFormer, a multimodal framework that combines the current RGB frame with the preceding 200 ms of bilateral wrist surface electromyography (sEMG) to estimate 44 finger and wrist joint angles. EVFormer separately encodes visual spatial features and sEMG temporal features, enables cross-modal information exchange through sequential bidirectional cross-attention, and integrates the two modalities using feature-wise gated fusion. We evaluate EVFormer in a single-participant feasibility study using one synchronized public EgoEMG recording with chronologically separated training, validation, and test splits. On 296 test samples, EVFormer achieves a mean absolute error of 11.482 degrees, compared with 13.228-13.610 degrees for vision-only, sEMG-only, late-fusion, and training-mean baselines. This corresponds to relative error reductions of 13.20% compared with the vision-only model and 14.23% compared with late fusion. EVFormer also achieves the lowest error in four of the five evaluated gesture classes. These results provide preliminary evidence that feature-level interaction between egocentric vision and sEMG can improve bimanual hand pose estimation. Further evaluation across participants, recording sessions, sensor placements, and real-world interaction conditions is required to establish the generalizability of the approach.

</details>


### [15] [Uncertainty in Representation Learning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06974)
*Yuqicheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究知识图谱嵌入（KGE）中的三类不确定性（知识、算法、预测），提出模型无关方法以实现可靠、具备不确定性意识的知识图谱推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGE方法虽在基准测试上表现优异，但其预测常缺乏可靠性与不确定性保证，限制了在高风险场景中的应用；不确定性贯穿从输入知识到训练与预测的整个流程。

Method: 针对算法不确定性，展示相同设置下模型预测差异显著，并提出基于投票的聚合框架；针对预测不确定性，将保形预测（conformal prediction）适配到KGE，构建具分布无关注覆盖保证的答案集，并扩展至谓词条件可靠性保证；针对知识不确定性，开发具统计有效性的预测区间及基于嵌入的方法，以在形式化可靠性保证下近似统计本体上的概率推理。

Result: 实现具备分布无关覆盖保证的答案集与谓词条件可靠性保证；提供统计有效的置信三元组预测区间；以形式化可靠性保证近似概率推理；通过投票聚合缓解训练不稳定性带来的预测差异。

Conclusion: 这些互补且模型无关的方法为KGE中的不确定性提供了实用且有理论基础的解决方案，推动知识图谱推理从单纯预测精度迈向可靠性与不确定性意识。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) methods represent entities and predicates in continuous vector spaces to infer missing knowledge. Despite strong benchmark performance, their predictions often lack principled reliability guarantees, limiting their use in high-stakes applications. Moreover, uncertainty arises throughout the KGE pipeline, from incomplete or probabilistic input knowledge to stochastic training and prediction. This thesis systematically investigates three sources of uncertainty in KGE: knowledge uncertainty, arising from incomplete, noisy, or probabilistic input knowledge; algorithmic uncertainty, induced by randomness in model training; and predictive uncertainty, concerning the reliability of model outputs. To address algorithmic uncertainty, the thesis demonstrates that models trained under identical settings can produce substantially different predictions and introduces a voting-based aggregation framework to mitigate this instability. To quantify predictive uncertainty, it adapts conformal prediction to KGE, constructing answer sets with distribution-free coverage guarantees and extending them to provide predicate-conditional reliability guarantees. To support reasoning under knowledge uncertainty, it develops statistically valid prediction intervals for confidence-scored triples and an embedding-based approach to approximate probabilistic reasoning over statistical ontologies with formal soundness guarantees. Together, these complementary, model-agnostic methods provide a practical and theoretically grounded approach to uncertainty in KGE, advancing beyond predictive accuracy toward reliable and uncertainty-aware knowledge graph reasoning.

</details>


### [16] [FC-SWE: Failure-Conditioned RL for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07898)
*Jia Liufu,Bin Hu,Linglin Jing,Terry Kong,Yuki Huang,Ashwath Aithal,Wenming Yang,Jun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FC-SWE：一种失败条件化的强化学习框架，利用失败补丁与验证反馈作为恢复轨迹的上下文，改进仓库级SWE智能体的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO等RL方法对同一issue独立采样多条轨迹并比较终端奖励，但不复用失败补丁的验证反馈作为后续尝试的上下文，浪费了有价值的诊断信息。

Method: 在补丁验证失败后，将仓库恢复到原始任务状态，以失败补丁和验证反馈为上下文生成恢复轨迹；通过轨迹局部奖励（保留每次尝试的验证结果）和活跃集优势估计（将实际执行的初始与恢复轨迹组成比较组）来改进GRPO。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench Verified任务上，Qwen3.5-4B与SWE-agent组合下，FC-SWE达到41.7% Resolved@1和52.8% Resolved@2，优于GRPO的38.9%和48.5%；仅用最多两次尝试训练，在十一次测试预算下达到70.7% Resolved@11。

Conclusion: 将失败恢复轨迹纳入策略训练可显著提升仓库级SWE智能体的性能，且训练的效率可外推到更大的测试时预算。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering (SWE) is a challenging long-horizon setting: agents must reason over extended interactions, use tools, and adapt to stateful environments. Recent work trains SWE agents with reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which independently sample multiple trajectories per issue, test the resulting patches, and compare terminal rewards within a fixed group. However, this training setup does not reuse verifier feedback from failed patches as context for subsequent attempts, even though this feedback contains valuable diagnostic information about what went wrong. Training on recovery trajectories is challenging because the preceding outcome determines whether the next trajectory is generated, while the failed execution determines its conditioning context. We introduce FC-SWE, a failure-conditioned RL framework that incorporates recovery attempts into policy training. After a patch fails verification, FC-SWE restores the repository to its original task state and uses the failed patch and verifier feedback as context for a recovery trajectory. FC-SWE adapts GRPO to these chains of complete, multi-turn tool-use trajectories through two mechanisms. Trajectory-local rewards preserve each attempt's verifier outcome, preventing recovery success from rewarding an earlier failed patch. Active-set advantage estimation forms a comparison group from all initial and recovery trajectories actually executed for the same issue, so failed attempts remain in the group while unexecuted attempts are excluded. On all 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks under a verifier-assisted protocol, FC-SWE with Qwen3.5-4B and SWE-agent achieves 41.7% Resolved@1 and 52.8% Resolved@2, compared with 38.9% and 48.5% for GRPO. Although trained with at most two attempts per chain, FC-SWE reaches 70.7% Resolved@11 under an eleven-attempt test-time budget.

</details>


### [17] [A Data-Driven Framework for Unsupervised Monitoring of Transmission Systems Using End-of-Line Testing Data: A Case Study at Ford Motor Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06980)
*Mohammad N. Bisheh,Mehrdad Moradi,Parinaz Farajiparvar,Colin Brady,Rajesh Gupta,Xueling Li,Javad Navaei,Milad Parvaneh,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向高维数据的多变量监测框架，两阶段处理（预处理+非线性降维与Phase I控制图异常检测），支持无监督/监督模式，在福特汽车真实生产数据上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感技术在各行业快速发展，产生具有复杂非线性模式和强时间依赖性的高维数据。传统统计监测方法难以捕捉非线性结构；终端测试中常用的分析方法依赖预设阈值和启发式规则，限制了其发现高维时序数据中有价值异常特征的能力；而深度学习和生成式AI虽预测能力强，但在数据采集成本高、且系统需保持可解释、低延迟、计算高效、供非技术人员使用的场景中并不适用。

Method: 提出一个面向高维数据的先进多变量监测框架，分两阶段：第一阶段对数据进行预处理，去除不完整和非信息样本，并对时间序列数据进行时间对齐；第二阶段执行非线性降维，随后通过控制图的Phase I监测过程进行异常检测。该框架可根据训练时是否有真实标签，在无监督与监督两种设置下使用；其灵活模块化结构允许从业者按不同领域和运行需求调整各组件。

Result: 在福特汽车公司真实汽车制造生产数据上评估。所提方法在准确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于公司现有模型，分别从0.50、0.30、0.429提升至0.625、1.00、0.769。

Conclusion: 该两阶段多变量监测框架能够有效处理高维非线性时序数据的异常检测任务，兼顾可解释性与计算效率，并在真实工业场景中显著优于现有方法，适合非技术从业者使用。

Abstract: Sensing technologies have advanced rapidly across industries ranging from energy to automotive manufacturing. These systems generate high-dimensional (HD) data characterized by complex nonlinear patterns and strong temporal dependencies. Traditional statistical monitoring methods are often limited in their ability to capture such nonlinear structure. Likewise, many analytical approaches used in End-of-Line testing rely on predefined thresholds and heuristic rules, which restrict their ability to detect informative anomaly signatures in HD temporal data. In contrast, while modern deep learning and generative AI models offer strong predictive capabilities, they are often unsuitable in applications where data are costly to collect and where the monitoring system must remain interpretable, low-latency, computationally efficient, and usable by non-technical practitioners. To overcome these limitations, we propose an advanced multivariate monitoring framework for HD data. The framework operates in two stages. In the first stage, the data are preprocessed to remove incomplete and non-informative samples and to temporally align time series data. In the second stage, nonlinear dimensionality reduction is performed, followed by anomaly detection through a control chart based phase I monitoring procedure. The framework can be used in both unsupervised and supervised settings, depending on the availability of ground truth labels during training. Moreover, its flexible and modular structure allows practitioners to adapt its components to different domains and operational requirements. We evaluate the proposed framework on real production data from an automotive manufacturing environment at Ford Motor Company. The proposed method achieves higher accuracy, recall, and F1 score than the company's existing model, improving these metrics from 0.50, 0.30, and 0.429 to 0.625, 1.00, and 0.769, respectively.

</details>


### [18] [An Information-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Benchmark and Model Diagnosis in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06988)
*Houru Jiang,Zixi Wang,Tengteng Cheng,Xueyi Li,Mingliang Hou,Jiaqi Zheng,Renqiang Luo,Teng Guo,Zitao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于信息论的KT基准诊断框架，用CTW作为操作性的因果不确定性坐标，将预测投影到共享不确定性维度上，按熵带评估模型表现，揭示模型改进的非均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪（KT）模型主要用AUC、准确率等聚合指标评估，这些全局分数掩盖了剩余错误的来源，也无法判断基准是否接近饱和。全局理论性能极限难以估计，但局部可预测性可以量化。

Method: 提出信息论评估框架：在项目-反应历史和当前项目查询上使用Context Tree Weighting（CTW）作为操作性的因果不确定性坐标，并将其与完整KT信息集下不可观测的Local Irreducible Uncertainty（LIU）区分开；将预测投影到这一共享不确定性坐标上，在不同熵带下评估模型性能增益，而非仅看全局水平。

Result: 在NIPS Task 3/4和Algebra 2005上的评估显示，模型改进高度非均匀：现代KT模型在高熵区域有显著增益；额外的项目感知参考、log-loss和等频分析支持这种局部化结论；框架还标出了一些表观增益需要检验噪声敏感行为的区域。

Conclusion: 通过揭示局部建模失败与真实增益，该框架为研究残差预测结构及当前KT基准和模型的局限性提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models are predominantly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as area under the curve (AUC) and accuracy. However, these global scores obscure where the remaining errors originate and fail to indicate whether a benchmark is approaching saturation. While estimating a global theoretical performance limit is challenging in realistic KT settings, it is possible to quantify local predictability. To address this, we propose an information-theoretic evaluation framework for KT benchmark diagnosis. We use Context Tree Weighting (CTW) on item-response histories and current-item queries as an operational causal uncertainty coordinate, while distinguishing it from the unobserved Local Irreducible Uncertainty (LIU) under the full KT information set. By projecting predictions onto this shared uncertainty coordinate, we evaluate model performance gains across distinct entropy bands rather than only at the global level. Comprehensive evaluations on NIPS Task 3/4 and Algebra 2005 reveal that model improvements are highly non-uniform. Modern KT models show substantial gains in high-entropy regions, and additional item-aware references, log-loss, and equal-frequency analyses support this localization. The framework also flags regions where apparent gains require checks for noise-sensitive behavior. By surfacing these local modeling failures alongside genuine gains, this approach provides a diagnostic tool for studying both residual predictive structure and the limitations of current KT benchmarks and models.

</details>


### [19] [Repair Lot Skyline: A Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Approach to Pavement Repair Optimization from Geospatial Hazard Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06989)
*Takato Yasuno,Keita Kobayashi,Ryuta Sakaguchi,Takuya Okamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将路面病害清单的修复决策形式化为沿里程定义的加权约束满足问题（WCSP）——Repair Lot Skyline问题，并进一步对最小修复段长度与历史上下文半径做联合四目标超参搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路面管理机构需要把空间分布的病害清单转化为有界、可执行的修复批次方案：坑洞等事故级缺陷必须修复，裂缝等低风险缺陷仅在收益可抵成本时才纳入，历史修补位置仅作为再退化风险的信号而不直接触发修复。这一决策需要在仅有一次病害调查、不对未来劣化做任何假设的条件下完成。

Method: 将问题形式化为定义在里程（而非时间）上的WCSP：坑洞为硬约束、裂缝为软约束、历史修补位置作为上下文信息。识别143个候选危险簇（61个硬、82个软），构建skyline前沿以权衡收益与修复长度，并将最小修复段长度L_min与历史上下文半径κ作为联合四目标超参数进行搜索。

Result: 最终方案合并106个候选簇，总长1,997.4 m，仅为纳入全部软候选所需2,385.9 m的83.7%；覆盖100%观测坑洞（138/138）与91.6%观测裂缝（404/441），捕获全部候选集合93.6%（542/579）的危害收益。被排除的37个软候选仅贡献6.8%额外收益，却需增加19.4%修复长度，呈现明显边际递减。推荐配置（L_min=14.7 m，κ=10 m）相较未调优默认值减少7.1%的修复班组出动次数，代价是预算增加12.9%、裂缝覆盖率下降1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 沿里程定义的WCSP框架无需预测未来劣化即可生成兼顾安全与成本的修复批次方案，skyline前沿刻画了收益与长度的权衡结构，而L_min与κ的联合调优可在出动次数、预算与覆盖率之间实现可控折衷。

Abstract: Pavement agencies must translate a spatially distributed distress inventory into a bounded, actionable repair-lot plan: accident-critical defects (potholes) must always be addressed, lower-risk defects (cracks) should be included only when their benefit justifies the repair cost, and historical patch locations signal re-degradation risk without themselves triggering repair. We formalize this as a Repair Lot Skyline problem: a Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Problem (WCSP) defined over chainage (distance along the road) rather than over time, so that it requires only a single-epoch distress survey and makes no claim about future deterioration. The WCSP identifies 143 candidate hazard clusters (61 hard, 82 soft), of which 106 are merged into a final repair plan totaling 1,997.4 m---83.7% of the 2,385.9 m that would be required if every soft candidate were included regardless of cost. This plan covers 100% of observed potholes (138/138) and 91.6% of observed cracks (404/441), capturing 93.6% (542/579) of the total hazard benefit available in the full candidate set. The skyline frontier shows pronounced diminishing returns beyond this point: the remaining 37 excluded soft candidates would add only 6.8% additional benefit for a 19.4% increase in repair length. We further formalize the minimum-lot-length $L_{\min}$ and historical-context radius $κ$ as a joint, four-objective hyperparameter search over this WCSP; on the same case study, the recommended configuration ($L_{\min} = 14.7$ m, $κ= 10$ m) reduces repair-crew mobilizations by 7.1% relative to an untuned default, at the cost of a 12.9% larger budget and a 1.1-percentage-point lower crack coverage.

</details>


### [20] [Tram-FL: Reducing Communication and Computation Costs through Sequential Model Circulation in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07859)
*Kota Maejima,Takayuki Nishio,Asato Yamazaki,Yuko Hara-Azumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tram-FL机制，通过让单个模型在节点间循环训练，并配合量化动量，实现低计算与通信开销的去中心化联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统去中心化联邦学习以客户端为中心，每个客户端各自维护模型副本并独立更新与交换，在non-IID数据下容易造成大量计算与通信浪费，难以在保证精度的同时降低资源消耗，因此需要转向以模型为中心的思路。

Method: Tram-FL以单个模型在节点间循环训练为核心，解决模型循环训练中的调度问题，即确定哪些节点应更新模型以及更新次数，通过设计模型的循环路线与更新迭代分配来实现；同时引入量化动量，在控制单次传输通信负载的情况下减少模型循环次数。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使在non-IID数据条件下，所提算法也能收敛到全局模型，并降低通信与计算开销。

Conclusion: 以模型为中心的去中心化联邦学习机制Tram-FL，结合调度与量化动量，可在non-IID数据下以更少的通信与计算达到全局模型收敛。

Abstract: Conventional decentralized federated learning (DFL) often focuses on clients, with each client maintaining a model copy, performing updates individually, and undertaking model exchange and integration. While fully leveraging computational resources can shorten training times, it can also lead to significant computational and communication waste. This is especially pronounced with non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data, where achieving high model accuracy demands extra resources. This research shifts focus to the model itself, aiming to realize DFL with minimal computation and communication costs. To this end, we propose Tram-FL (Traveling Model Training Mechanism for Decentralized Federated Learning), a mechanism designed to efficiently address these challenges. It sequentially trains a single model by circulating it among nodes. We address the training scheduling problem in model circulation-based training, specifically determining which nodes should update the model and the number of updates to perform. This is approached by considering the model's circulation route and update iteration allocation, for which we propose simple yet effective methods. Additionally, with quantized momentum, Tram-FL achieves high accuracy with fewer model circulations while controlling communication load per transmission. Experimental results show that the proposed algorithm, even with non-IID data, converges to a global model with reduced communication and computation.

</details>


### [21] [DART-ES: Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Fine-Tuning LLMs with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06993)
*Zhishen Sun,Hongzhan Wang,Sizhe Dang,Guang Dai,Haishan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART-ES通过难度感知重加权与稀有可解样本回放，改进ES微调，性能超ES且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ES统一平均奖励并把问题级种群反馈压缩为单一标量，难以捕捉各问题学习价值随模型能力的变化。

Method: 用扰动种群通过率估计局部可解性，聚合历史观测构建动态难度状态，共同引导连续难度重加权和稀有可解样本回放，无需额外难度模型或反向传播。

Result: 五个基座模型上均超ES，平均准确率72.07%升至73.53%，超GRPO的73.26%；五个数学推理基准平均49.20%（ES为48.34%）；单步运行时间降低15.2%–50.2%，峰值显存降低21.1%–51.1%，优于GRPO及GRPO+LoRA。

Conclusion: DART-ES在指令微调、代码生成和Countdown任务上具泛化性，可扩展至14B模型，兼顾性能与系统效率。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) enable memory efficient full parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward computation. However, standard ES uniformly averages rewards across problems and compresses problem level population feedback into a single scalar, making it difficult to capture how the learning value of each problem changes with model capability. To address this limitation, we propose Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Evolution Strategies (DART-ES). DART-ES estimates the local solvability of each problem from its pass rate across the perturbation population and aggregates historical observations to construct a dynamic difficulty state. This shared state jointly guides continuous difficulty reweighting and rare solvable sample replay, thereby improving perturbation direction evaluation and training data allocation without introducing an additional difficulty model or backpropagation. Extensive experiments show that DART-ES achieves good fine-tuning performance. DART-ES outperforms ES on all five base models and improves the average accuracy from 72.07\% to 73.53\%, exceeding the 73.26\% achieved by GRPO on GSM8K. Across five challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DART-ES achieves an average accuracy of 49.20\%, compared with 48.34\% for ES and remains competitive with strong 7B models trained with RL. Further experiments show consistent gains in instruction tuning, code generation and the Countdown task with a 14B model, demonstrating strong generalization across tasks and scalability to larger models. Beyond performance gains, DART-ES also shows clear advantages in system efficiency. It reduces runtime per step by 15.2\%--50.2\% and peak memory usage per GPU by 21.1\%--51.1\% compared with GRPO. Despite performing full parameter fine-tuning, DART-ES also requires less runtime and GPU memory than GRPO+LoRA.

</details>


### [22] [Mask-Guided KV Cache Eviction in Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06996)
*Gleb Molodtsov,Ekaterina Alimaskina,Evgeny Uskov,Artur Zagitov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaskAhead：一种免训练方法，用单一掩码查询排序机制同时解决块扩散语言模型的KV选择与淘汰问题，大幅降低KV内存并提升生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型在生成过程中保留大型KV缓存并在每个去噪步骤attend，限制了内存容量与生成速度。要降低这些开销，需决定哪些过去token用于当前块去噪（选择）、哪些保留在内存供未来块使用（淘汰）。

Method: 提出MaskAhead，通过单一掩码查询排序机制同时解决选择与淘汰：当前块掩码指导选择，未来掩码块的探测指导淘汰；两者均按对attention输出的估计贡献对KV条目排序。其量化变体Q-MaskAhead直接从低比特KV计算选择与attention，基本保留所选条目。

Result: 在Fast-dLLM-v2、DreamReasoner、LLaDA2.0-mini上实验，覆盖长生成推理、长提示QA和针检索。长提示QA中，MaskAhead平均减少KV内存9.5倍，F1平均损失1.2点；Q-MaskAhead减少20.1倍，F1平均损失2.3点。batch-32系统测试中，MaskAhead相比稠密推理实现1.23倍端到端和1.68倍解码阶段加速。

Conclusion: MaskAhead以免训练方式统一处理KV选择与淘汰，显著降低KV内存并加速生成，量化变体进一步放大内存收益，适用于多种长上下文任务。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models keep a large key-value (KV) cache throughout generation and attend to it at every denoising step, limiting both memory capacity and generation speed. Reducing these costs requires deciding which past tokens to use for denoising the current block (selection) and which to keep in memory for future blocks (eviction). We propose MaskAhead, a training-free method that solves both tasks with a single mask-query-based ranking mechanism. Current-block masks guide selection, while probes of upcoming masked blocks guide eviction. Both rank KV entries by their estimated contribution to the attention output. Our quantized variant, Q-MaskAhead, computes selection and attention directly from low-bit KV, largely preserving the selected entries. Experiments on Fast-dLLM-v2, DreamReasoner, and LLaDA2.0-mini cover long-generation reasoning, long-prompt question answering, and needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. On long-prompt QA, MaskAhead reduces KV memory by $9.5\times$ on average with a 1.2-point mean F1 loss relative to dense inference. Q-MaskAhead increases the reduction to $20.1\times$ with a 2.3-point mean F1 loss. In a batch-32 systems profile, MaskAhead achieves $1.23\times$ end-to-end and $1.68\times$ decode-stage speedups over dense inference.

</details>


### [23] [Should We Skip Diffusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07002)
*Yiping Ji,James Martens,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DDT-RFE，通过移除条件编码器中Self-Attention和MLP的残差连接提升表示抽象层次，并融合多深度特征供解码器使用，在多项视觉理解任务与图像生成上均优于DDT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成图像的同时学习语义表示。DDT中条件编码器为速度解码器提供去噪所需特征，这些特征需同时包含高层抽象结构与低层细节。但编码器中的skip/residual连接使浅层特征绕过后续变换，可能限制渐进式抽象，或至少难以区分不同抽象层次。

Method: 提出DDT-RFE：移除每个编码器块中Self-Attention与MLP操作周围的残差连接，同时保持训练稳定；为保留被抽象丢弃但解码器仍需的信息，将输入patch embedding与中间及最终编码器特征融合，构成编码器输出，使解码器可获取多个编码深度的信息，而每个编码器块能学习更抽象的表示。

Result: DDT-RFE在图像分类、语义分割、目标发现、语义对应等视觉理解任务上整体优于DDT，且使用更少的编码器块；在ImageNet图像生成上取得更低的FID。

Conclusion: 移除编码器残差连接并融合多深度特征，可在减少编码器块的同时提升表示抽象能力与下游任务性能，兼顾理解与生成。

Abstract: Diffusion models learn semantic representations while generating images. In the Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (DDT), a condition encoder provides features that guide a velocity decoder in denoising. To enable effective denoising at all noise levels, these features must capture both high-level abstract structures and low-level details. However, skip/residual connections in the encoder allow shallow features to bypass successive transformations, which may limit progressive abstraction, or at least make it difficult to disentangle different levels of abstraction. We propose DDT-RFE, which removes the residual connections around the Self-Attention and MLP operations in each encoder block while maintaining stable training. To retain the information that abstraction discards but that the decoder still needs, we fuse the input patch embedding with intermediate and final encoder features to form the encoder output. The decoder thus has access to information from multiple encoder depths, while each encoder block is able to learn more abstract representations. DDT-RFE achieves overall improvements over DDT across visual understanding tasks, including image classification, semantic segmentation, object discovery, and semantic correspondence, while using fewer encoder blocks. It also achieves a lower FID for image generation on ImageNet.

</details>


### [24] [STOCK-JEPA: Prior-Anchored Latent Revision Representation Learning in Equity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07006)
*Yizhi Luo,Jiahe Yi,Jianhui Zhang,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stock-JEPA，一种联合嵌入预测框架，学习相对于时点金融先验的可预测增量修正，结合低复杂度金融模型与深度表征学习的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据信噪比低，表征学习困难。黑盒深度模型能捕捉复杂模式，但易过拟合噪声且缺乏显式经济结构；经典线性金融模型具备可解释性，但其过度简化的假设无法捕捉非线性信号。需要一种方法结合两者优势。

Method: 框架分三步：1）用低复杂度金融模型生成固定的多期收益与风险统计量，先验投影器将其映射到目标编码器隐空间作为锚点；2）设计上下文条件修正预测器，估计未来表征相对锚点的可预测位移，两分支分别用独立损失更新——锚点学习先验统计量，修正分支从历史上下文中捕捉额外可预测信息；3）冻结所有表征模块，训练下游读出层，通过横截面排序与组合表现评估预测。

Result: 理论上证明最优修正可恰好降低先验锚点对未来同一表征的期望平方误差，降低量为 E[‖Δ‖²]，即历史上下文中可预测额外信号的期望平方幅度。实验上，Stock-JEPA 在中国和美国大规模股票池、5 项关键评估指标上优于 13 个强基线。消融研究与表征分析进一步验证所学修正的价值。

Conclusion: Stock-JEPA 通过可预测增量修正将先验金融统计与深度表征学习结合，理论与实验均表明该修正对股票市场表征学习具有显著价值。

Abstract: Learning effective representations helps characterize the structure and dynamics of equity markets from financial data with a low signal-to-noise ratio. Black-box deep models can capture complex patterns but may overfit sample noise and lack explicit economic structure. Meanwhile, classic linear financial models provide interpretable references, but their oversimplified assumptions leave non-linear signals uncaptured. To combine the strengths of these two directions, we propose Stock-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive framework that learns predictable incremental revisions relative to a point-in-time financial prior. First, we leverage a low-complexity financial model to produce fixed statistics summarizing multi-horizon return and risk. A prior projector then maps these statistics into the target encoder's latent space as an anchor. Second, we design a context-conditioned revision predictor to estimate the future representation's predictable displacement from the anchor. Separate losses update the two branches: the anchor learns from prior statistics, while the revision captures additional predictable information from historical context. Third, we freeze all representation modules and train a downstream readout, evaluating its forecasts through cross-sectional ranking and portfolio performance. Theoretically, we prove that optimal revision reduces the prior anchor's expected squared error for the same future representation by exactly $\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbolΔ\|_2^2]$. This non-negative gain is the expected squared magnitude of the additional signal predictable from historical context. Experimentally, Stock-JEPA outperforms 13 strong baselines across large-scale China and U.S. equity universes on 5 key evaluation metrics. Ablation studies and representation analysis further demonstrate the value of the learned revisions for representation learning in equity markets.

</details>


### [25] [Identifiable World Models from Pretrained Diffusion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07028)
*Ruchi Sandilya,Conor Liston,Logan Grosenick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，无需重新训练扩散模型主干，仅通过一个轻量级对齐映射（ConDA），即可让冻结的预训练扩散世界模型获得可辨识、可解释且适用于干预分析的动力系统坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式世界模型虽能预测高维动力系统的轨迹，但预测准确并不代表其潜在坐标能恢复真实的状态变量或因果交互。作者希望探究：能否在不重新训练生成主干的前提下，为冻结的预训练扩散模型赋予可辨识的坐标。

Method: 提出对比扩散对齐（ConDA）：在冻结的扩散潜变量之上，仅学习一个轻量级对齐映射。理论上证明辅助变量非线性ICA的可辨识性保证可迁移至ConDA，在标准TCL/GCL假设下，对齐表示能辨识潜在动力状态（至排列与逐分量可逆变换），保留潜在动态结构因果模型，并将滞后图恢复化简为转移雅可比矩阵的稀疏性问题。实验对比TCL、GCL、CEBRA-based ConDA与TDRL、CaRiNG、IDOL、temporal SuaVE、iVAE。

Result: 在物理与机器人视频系统上，TCL和GCL实现近乎完美的分块状态恢复与有竞争力的滞后图恢复，在模拟落体系统中达到精确恢复。在模拟双足机器人中，所学动力学能恢复对留出控制扰动的响应的符号与时间结构。

Conclusion: 冻结的生成式扩散模型可以获得可辨识、结构可解释且有助于分析干预相关动力学的坐标，为世界模型的可解释性与因果分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based world models can generate and predict trajectories in high-dimensional dynamical systems, but predictive accuracy does not imply that their latent coordinates recover the underlying state variables or causal interactions. We ask whether a frozen pretrained diffusion model can be equipped with identifiable coordinates without retraining its generative backbone. We show that auxiliary-variable nonlinear ICA guarantees can be transferred to Contrastive Diffusion Alignment (ConDA), which learns only a lightweight alignment map on top of frozen diffusion latents. Under standard TCL/GCL assumptions, the aligned representation identifies latent dynamical states up to permutation and componentwise invertible transformations, preserves the latent dynamic structural causal model, and reduces lagged graph recovery to transition-Jacobian sparsity. We evaluate TCL-, GCL-, and CEBRA-based ConDA against TDRL, CaRiNG, IDOL, temporal SuaVE, and iVAE across physical and robotic video systems. TCL and GCL achieve near-perfect blockwise state recovery and competitive lagged graph recovery, including exact recovery in a simulated falling-body system. In a simulated bipedal robot, learned dynamics recover the sign and temporal structure of responses to held-out control perturbations. These results show that a frozen generative diffusion model can be equipped with coordinates that are identifiable, structurally interpretable, and useful for analyzing intervention-relevant dynamics.

</details>


### [26] [Shaping the Wind: Nested Potentials for Kinematically Admissible Urban Wind Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07033)
*Yidi Wang,Yunhe Zhang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sculpt嵌套势框架，将几何相关的无散度与壁面不可穿透约束直接内嵌于神经网络参数化中，并发布UrbanWindFlow数据集，同时评估精度与运动学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测瞬态城市风场对理解城市微气候和设计气候韧性城市至关重要。建筑解析大涡模拟虽能给出详细不可压风场，但每种布局计算成本高昂。神经代理模型可加速预测速度场演化，但最小化速度预测误差无法保证局部质量守恒与壁面不可穿透性（二者共同定义运动学可行性）。其根源在于无约束的输出表示：几何条件只引导预测而不将其限制在可行速度场中；而事后修正边界违规又会改变相邻流体单元的通量平衡，可能破坏局部质量守恒。

Method: 提出Sculpt——一种嵌套势框架，把耦合的、依赖几何的约束直接内建到参数化中。该嵌套参数化在原生三维交错网格上通过体矢量势的离散旋度生成无散度速度更新；一个共享标量势约束矢量势的边界值，使同一算子同时强制不可穿透性，无需逐步压力投影。由于旋度反向传播会衰减大尺度梯度信号，作者在多个分辨率上参数化体势以更好捕捉大尺度流动结构。

Result: 引入UrbanWindFlow数据集，涵盖多种城市形态与入流条件，用于同时评估预测精度与运动学可行性，验证了所提框架在两者上的表现。

Conclusion: 通过将无散度与不可穿透约束内嵌于网络参数化，Sculpt在不依赖每步压力投影的情况下生成满足运动学可行性的城市风场预测，为快速、物理可靠的瞬态城市风场建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Predicting transient urban winds is fundamental to understanding urban microclimates and designing climate-resilient cities. Building-resolving large-eddy simulation produces detailed incompressible urban wind fields at substantial computational cost for each layout. Neural surrogates offer a faster alternative by learning to predict the evolution of velocity fields. However, minimizing velocity prediction error does not guarantee local mass conservation and wall impermeability, which together define kinematic admissibility. This limitation stems from an unconstrained output representation: geometry conditioning guides predictions but does not restrict them to admissible velocity fields. Correcting boundary violations in these outputs changes the flux balance in adjacent fluid cells and may consequently compromise local mass conservation. To address the challenge, we propose Sculpt, a nested potential framework that builds the coupled, geometry-dependent constraints directly into its parameterization. This nested parameterization generates divergence-free velocity updates through the discrete curl of a volume vector potential on the native three-dimensional staggered grid. A shared scalar potential constrains the vector potential's boundary values so that the same operator also enforces impermeability, without a per-step pressure projection. Because backpropagation through this curl attenuates large-scale gradient signals, we parameterize the volume potential at multiple resolutions to better capture large-scale flow structures. We introduce UrbanWindFlow, an LES dataset spanning urban morphologies and inflow conditions, to evaluate accuracy and kinematic admissibility together.

</details>


### [27] [The Premise Is the Problem: Exchangeability Failure in Self-Monitored Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07038)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu,Zhenda Li,Liying Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多步时间序列预测中"自监控自适应"的可靠性问题：当监控与自适应使用同一预测误差反馈时，监控的统计保证可能失效，导致误报、保护性响应反而损害预测，并掩盖真实变化。作者主张部署时需检验监控假设、与冻结模型对比并限制保护响应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预测模型常在部署后更新以应对数据变化，但更新有时会令预测变差，因此实际系统需要可靠的监控来检测有害变化并触发保护。一个自然的做法是监控那些驱动更新的同一批预测误差。本文质疑：当监控与自适应共用同一反馈时，监控背后的统计保证是否仍然成立。

Method: 作者在多步时间序列预测的设定下进行理论研究，分析监控与自适应共享同一预测误差反馈时的统计性质，考察重叠目标（overlapping targets）与预测误差中的依赖性对监控保证所需关键假设的影响，并观察自适应过程对监控信号的作用。

Result: 重叠目标与误差依赖会破坏监控保证所依赖的关键假设，使监控在并未发生有害变化时也发出警报，且其响应会进一步损害预测质量。此外，自适应可能把持续性的变化从自身监控中隐藏起来，而原本的冻结模型反而保留更清晰的信号。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了自监控自适应存在一个基本失效模式。可靠的部署需要检验监控的假设、在真实反馈下将自适应与冻结模型进行对比，并限制每一次保护性响应所造成的影响。

Abstract: Modern forecasting models are often updated after deployment so they can respond to changing data. These updates can also make predictions worse, so practical systems need a reliable monitor that can detect harmful changes and trigger protection. A natural design is to monitor the same prediction errors that guide the updates. This paper asks whether the statistical guarantee behind such a monitor remains valid when monitoring and adaptation use the same feedback. We study this question in multi-step time-series forecasting. We show that overlapping targets and dependence in forecast errors can break a key assumption required by the guarantee. The monitor may then raise alarms even when no harmful change has occurred, and its response can further damage prediction quality. We also find that adaptation can hide sustained changes from its own monitor, while the original frozen model retains a clearer signal. These results expose a basic failure mode in self-monitored adaptation. They show why reliable deployment requires checking the monitor's assumptions, comparing adaptation with the frozen model under realistic feedback, and limiting the effect of every protective response.

</details>


### [28] [TRIAGE: Direction-Aware Mismatch Stabilization of Native NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07043)
*Zhen Li,Shuai Zhang,Yanggan Gu,Yiming Zhang,Yang Yu,Mingfa Feng,Congkai Xie,Shuang Yu,Junjie Lai,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出方向感知的稳定化方法TRIAGE，用于低精度（NVFP4）强化学习训练大语言模型，通过分段诊断选择性重平衡策略梯度更新，实现稳定优化并达到全精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度执行能大幅加速大语言模型的强化学习，但学习者与采样器执行之间的差异会导致策略优化不稳定。仅用失配幅度无法识别哪些更新贡献具有局部放大或收缩作用，因此需要刻画失配与策略梯度方向之间的交互。

Method: 论文刻画了失配与策略梯度方向的交互，区分局部放大与收缩的更新贡献；在原生NVFP4运行中发现两类放大区域之间的早期失衡，倾向于负优势、负差距更新，其尾部token集中于少数响应片段。基于此提出TRIAGE：利用分段级诊断选择性重平衡策略梯度更新，并对残余严重失配施加有界修复，修改优化目标同时保留采样器与学习者上的原生NVFP4 W4A4前向执行。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-30B-A3B上的实验中，TRIAGE在整个评估训练周期内保持稳定优化，在五个数学推理基准上达到全精度水平性能，原生NVFP4配合TRIAGE相比BF16提供最高2.3倍的rollout吞吐量。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过方向感知的分段诊断与有界修复，解决了低精度强化学习中失配导致的优化不稳定问题，在保持原生NVFP4 W4A4执行的同时兼顾了稳定性、精度与吞吐效率。

Abstract: Low-precision execution can substantially accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models, but discrepancies between learner and sampler execution can destabilize policy optimization. In this paper, we characterize the interaction between mismatch and the policy-gradient direction, distinguishing locally amplifying from contracting update contributions that mismatch magnitude alone cannot identify. In native NVFP4 runs, we observe an early imbalance between the two amplifying regions, favoring negative-advantage, negative-gap updates. Their tail tokens become concentrated in a small fraction of response segments before mismatch spreads globally. Motivated by these findings, we introduce TRIAGE, a direction-aware stabilization method that uses segment-level diagnosis to selectively rebalance policy-gradient updates and applies bounded repair to residual severe mismatch. TRIAGE modifies the optimization objective while retaining native NVFP4 weight-and activation 4-bit (W4A4) forward execution on both the sampler and learner. Experiments on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B show stable optimization throughout the evaluated training horizon and achieve full precision level performance across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, while native NVFP4 with TRIAGE provides up to 2.3x higher rollout throughput than BF16.

</details>


### [29] [Skillful Data-Driven Subseasonal Soil Moisture Forecasting: Prospects and Limits for Flash Drought Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07060)
*Noelia Otero,Atahan Özer,Miguel-Ángel Fernández-Torres,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用基于Vision Transformer的双路径时空注意力架构进行欧洲S2S土壤湿度预测，发现预测技巧不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于问题设定方式（残差学习、物理单位目标、概率化分位数微调），在2021-2022年基准测试中取得最优确定性及概率性技巧，但快速干旱爆发预测仍是所有S2S系统的共同难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管中短期天气预报取得显著进展，但快速干旱（flash drought）等高影响事件的预测仍是预警业务和基于物理的次季节到季节（S2S）预测系统的关键挑战。作者希望探索S2S土壤湿度预测技巧的来源，并推进数据驱动的S2S预测能力。

Method: 采用基于Vision Transformer的双路径时间与空间注意力架构；引入残差学习以超越持续性基准；在物理单位下（而非标准化异常）预测根区土壤湿度；通过分位数（quantile-head）微调实现概率化预测分布；并与深度学习及ECMWF业务S2S基线在2021-2022年进行对比。

Result: 相比深度学习和ECMWF业务S2S基线，该模型在所有预见期上取得最高的确定性技巧和概率性技巧，并能可靠检测异常的根区干燥状态（低于第20百分位）。然而，按多候（multi-pentad）增强标准定义的快速干旱爆发，仍是当前所有S2S系统共同面临的重大挑战。

Conclusion: 预测技巧在很大程度上取决于预测问题的表述方式（残差学习、物理单位目标表示、概率化建模），这些因素甚至与模型本身同等重要。研究推进了数据驱动的S2S土壤湿度预测，但快速干旱发展的预测仍是尚未解决的核心难题。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in short-to-medium-range weather forecasting, predicting high-impact events such as flash droughts remains a key challenge for both early warning operations and physically-based subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) prediction systems. Here we demonstrate that, for S2S soil-moisture forecasting over Europe, forecast skill depends as much on how the prediction problem is formulated as on the forecasting model itself. Using a Vision Transformer-based architecture with dual-pathway temporal and spatial attention, we show that residual learning is essential to outperform persistence. This advantage is realized only when forecasting root-zone soil moisture in physical units rather than standardized anomalies, revealing that the target representation itself constrains predictability. A probabilistic extension via quantile-head fine-tuning further provides well-calibrated predictive distributions. Benchmarked against deep-learning and operational ECMWF S2S baselines over 2021-2022, our model achieves the highest deterministic and probabilistic skill at all lead times and reliably detects anomalously dry root-zone states (below the 20th percentile). Yet flash drought onset, defined by multi-pentad intensification criteria, remains a fundamental challenge shared across all current S2S systems. These findings advance data-driven S2S soil-moisture forecasting while highlighting the remaining challenge of predicting rapid drought development.

</details>


### [30] [Learning to Simulate Individuals from Macro Social Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07062)
*Yining Zhao,Bushi Liu,Haofei Yu,Zhengyang Qi,Shanyong Wang,Chuyue Li,Yuxiang Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 macro2mind，利用预测市场的价格轨迹来学习"行为推理"，通过 GRPO 训练语言模型进行社会行为分解式预测，从而获得可迁移的用户模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于模拟个体响应的大语言模型，其行为推理要么继承自预训练，要么学习自个体级标注；这些来源的行为多样性有限，且对推理过程本身缺乏监督。

Method: 以预测市场（价格轨迹记录大规模人群对真实事件的响应）作为行为推理的学习信号，用 GRPO 训练语言模型。核心是"社会行为分解"，把行为推理变成预测的显式步骤：推断代表性市场参与者群体、预测各群体如何解读新闻并更新信念、推理群体间互动，并聚合为价格。此外设计了"事后懊悔课程"配合难度感知采样，聚焦于事后识别的群体能显著改进预测的转换样本，并优先保留对当前策略仍可学习的样本。

Result: 在 SWM-Bench 上，macro2mind 在 Polymarket 的方向准确率和相关性达到最优。仅用市场数据训练，可零样本迁移到四个用户模拟基准（Humanual、OvertonBench、PRISM、CAD），在零样本方法中具竞争力。作为数据生成器，可在未见用户上把下游模拟器准确率提升 15.5 个百分点，比其骨干模型生成的数据高出 13.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 预测市场可作为一种可扩展的行为推理监督来源；学到的推理无需额外训练即可用于用户模拟，并能迁移与提升下游任务表现。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate how individuals respond to new situations, yet the behavioral reasoning behind these responses is either inherited from pretraining or learned from individual-level annotations, which offer limited behavioral diversity and little supervision of the reasoning itself. We propose to learn behavioral reasoning from prediction markets, whose price trajectories record how populations respond to real-world events at scale. We introduce macro2mind, which trains a language model with GRPO using market signals. A social behavioral decomposition makes behavioral reasoning an explicit step of forecasting: the model infers representative groups of market participants, predicts how each interprets the news and updates its beliefs, reasons about their interactions, and aggregates these responses into a price. A hindsight-regret curriculum with difficulty-aware sampling focuses training on transitions where hindsight-identified groups substantially improve the forecast while prioritizing examples that remain learnable for the current policy. The learned reasoning applies to user simulation without further training. On SWM-Bench, macro2mind achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy and correlation on Polymarket. Trained on market data, it transfers zero-shot to four user-simulation benchmarks (Humanual, OvertonBench, PRISM, and CAD) and has competitive performance among zero-shot methods. Used as a data generator, macro2mind also raises a downstream simulator's accuracy on unseen users by 15.5 points, outperforming data generated by its backbone by 13.2 points.

</details>


### [31] [What Must Replay Preserve? Separating Correctable Bias from Class Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07077)
*BoRen Deng,Xiangyue Ma,Chenglong Li,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个诊断框架，将缓存预测视为时间异质监督，以区分Logit replay中存储分数与其对应关系的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Logit replay方法（如DER、DER++）通过匹配存储样本上的历史预测来缓解遗忘，但删除匹配的性能代价无法说明存储分数本身是否必要，也无法判断该代价能否通过纠正分类器对近期类别的偏移来消除。

Method: 提出诊断框架，将缓存预测分为存储时已知类别与之后学习的类别，分别编辑并评估；在任务级偏移（不改变任务内预测）前后对每个模型进行比较。在CIFAR-100上以DER++为对象，用固定常数替换未刷新的后续类别存储分数。

Result: 在1个百分点等价范围内，固定常数可替代后续学习类别的旧存储分数；任务级偏移将删除后续类别匹配的代价从14.9降至1.8点。替换存储时已知类别的非gold分数（保持数值与任务目标概率）在偏移前代价4.3点、偏移后4.0点，图像蒸馏中也有类似代价。

Conclusion: 在测试的固定头设定下，删除后续类别匹配的大代价主要可通过该偏移纠正，而破坏类别对应关系的小代价则持续存在。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning must recognize all classes seen so far without task labels. Logit replay methods such as DER and DER++ mitigate forgetting by matching the model's past predictions on stored examples. Deleting this matching reveals its benefit, but the resulting accuracy cost cannot show whether the stored scores themselves are needed, or whether the cost survives correction of the classifier's bias toward recent classes. We propose a diagnostic framework that treats a cached prediction as temporally heterogeneous supervision: it separates classes known when an example was stored from classes learned afterward, edits each group, and evaluates every model before and after a task-level offset that leaves within-task predictions unchanged. On CIFAR-100 with DER++, suitable fixed constants replace the unrefreshed stored scores of later-learned classes within an equivalence margin of 1 percentage point, and the offset reduces the cost of deleting their matching from 14.9 to 1.8 points. Reassigning the non-gold scores of classes known at storage, which preserves their values and each task's target probability, costs 4.3 points before and 4.0 after the offset, and a parallel cost persists in image distillation. In the tested fixed-head setting, the large cost of deleting later-class matching is thus mostly correctable by this offset, whereas the smaller cost of disrupting class correspondence persists. Code and data are available at anonymous.4open.science/r/replay-preserve-E22B.

</details>


### [32] [Few-Shot Bioactivity Prediction with Meta-Learning under Assay Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07079)
*Michal Kmicikiewicz,Tommy Rochussen,Vincent Fortuin,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MetaHeta框架，通过利用相关实验的辅助数据来缓解实验异质性对元学习的影响，从而提升少样本生物活性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中的生物活性预测面临单个实验数据过少、难以独立训练可靠模型的挑战。元学习为这种少样本场景提供了可行方案，但实验之间的异质性可能限制其效果。作者希望验证这一假设并加以解决。

Method: 作者首先验证元学习性能随元训练任务异质性增加而下降。为此提出MetaHeta框架，通过在与相关实验的辅助数据条件下进行预测来考虑实验异质性，相关性可灵活定义。其架构结合了对大规模辅助数据的线性注意力与对稀缺任务特定上下文的精确注意力，从而在高效扩展辅助数据的同时保留对任务特定上下文的精确注意力。

Result: 在来自ChEMBL和BindingDB的实验数据上，该方法改善了少样本生物活性预测，并在回顾性贝叶斯优化中提升了后续化合物优先级排序的效果。

Conclusion: 实验异质性会削弱元学习效果，而通过条件化于相关实验的辅助数据可有效缓解该问题，MetaHeta在少样本生物活性预测及下游化合物筛选中表现更优。

Abstract: Accurate bioactivity prediction is a central challenge in early-stage drug discovery, as individual assays often contain too few measurements to train reliable models independently. Meta-learning offers a principled approach to this few-shot setting, but assay heterogeneity may limit its effectiveness. Here, we test this hypothesis and show that meta-learning performance degrades as meta-training tasks become more heterogeneous. To address this, we introduce MetaHeta, a meta-learning framework that accounts for assay heterogeneity by conditioning predictions on auxiliary data from related assays, with relatedness defined flexibly from available assay information. The architecture of MetaHeta combines linear attention over large auxiliary datasets with exact attention over scarce task-specific context, enabling efficient scaling to the former without compromising exact attention over the latter. We demonstrate the benefits of our approach on assays from ChEMBL and BindingDB, improving few-shot bioactivity prediction and downstream compound prioritization in retrospective Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [33] [When Attention Does Not Explain the Peak: Temporal Reference vs. Forecast Output in Attention-Based Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07080)
*Yuji Akamatsu,Takao Yamanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在负荷预测模型中，尽管跨注意力分布具有结构化、输入敏感和可复现的特点，但其注意力权重并不能有效解释预测峰值的出现时间；注意力更可能扮演的是整合未来外生信息的“时间参考”角色，而非峰值选择性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力图常被当作预测模型决策依据的证据。本文针对一个负荷预测模型，检验“高度被关注的未来时间步可解释预测峰值时间”这一直觉性的时间解释是否成立。

Method: 使用 horizon 级注意力描述子 Ψ_out；在巴拿马负荷数据集上，对 31 个按天对齐的时间窗比较预测峰值时间与 Ψ_out 的 argmax；并通过 ±1 h 范围内的注意力相对均匀基线的倍数来检验注意力集中性；同时进行未来天气特征替换为训练集均值的消融实验，以及三个随机种子重复。

Result: 预测峰值中位时间误差为 0 h，精确匹配率 51.6%；而 Ψ_out 的 argmax 中位误差为 5 h，精确匹配率 0%。预测峰值在 31 个窗口中有 27 个更接近观测峰值。±1 h 内注意力仅为均匀基线的 1.16×、1.11×、1.14×，表明集中性弱且非选择性。注意力分布具有跨窗口结构性，一致性 0.83；替换 12 个未来天气特征为训练集均值后分布接近均匀，说明其受未来天气变化影响而非固定时间位置；结果在三个随机种子下可复现。

Conclusion: 结构化、输入敏感且可复现的 horizon 级跨注意力并不必然提供有效的峰值选择性解释。该行为更符合内部“horizon 参考”角色，用于整合未来外生信息，但其功能作用尚未被因果证实。

Abstract: Attention maps are often interpreted as evidence of what a forecasting model uses when making predictions. In our load-forecasting model, a CLS representation of historical demand queries 24 future exogenous horizon tokens through cross-attention, inviting a temporal interpretation in which highly attended horizons may appear to explain forecast peak timing. We test this interpretation using a horizon-level attention descriptor, $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$. Across 31 day-aligned windows of the Panama load dataset, the forecast achieves a median peak-time error of 0 h and a 51.6% exact-match rate, whereas the argmax of $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$ has a median error of 5 h and 0% exact match. The forecast peak is closer to the observed peak in 27 of 31 windows. This dissociation is not merely an argmax artifact: within $\pm1$ h, attention reaches only $1.16\times$, $1.11\times$, and $1.14\times$ the uniform baseline around observed, predicted, and weekly-naive peaks, respectively, indicating weak and non-selective concentration. Yet the attention profile is structured, with cross-window consistency of 0.83. Replacing 12 future weather features with their training-set means makes the profile nearly uniform, showing sensitivity to future weather variation rather than fixed horizon position alone. The dissociation is also reproduced across three random-seed runs. These results show that structured, input-sensitive, and reproducible horizon-level cross-attention need not provide a valid peak-selective explanation of forecast behavior.
