The observed behavior is instead consistent with an internal horizon-reference role for integrating future exogenous information, although this functional role is not causally established.

</details>


### [34] [SchemaFill: Efficient LLM Tool Calling via Slot-Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07086)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SchemaFill框架，通过槽位并行推测解码加速LLM工具调用生成，在Glaive和BFCL上实现最高4.05倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过结构化工具调用与外部系统交互，标准自回归解码逐token生成多个调用及大量参数字段，延迟高。虽然参数结构提供了并行生成机会，但后续参数值可能依赖前面字段和调用，独立生成的值会偏离目标模型输出。

Method: 槽位并行推测解码框架。并发生成未来槽位值作为候选，无需预先知道实际调用序列或参数值；将跨多字段、多调用的候选拼接，在真实输出前缀下由目标模型验证；仅提交通过验证的token，候选不一致时由目标模型提供修正。

Result: 在Glaive和BFCL数据集上，相比自回归解码，端到端吞吐量最高提升4.05倍。

Conclusion: SchemaFill在应用目标验证的同时，利用槽位与调用间的并行性，有效加速LLM工具调用生成，兼顾效率与输出一致性。

Abstract: LLM agents interact with external systems by generating structured tool calls. Given a user request, conversational context, and a catalog of tool schemas, a tool-calling model must select tools and generate their arguments, potentially producing multiple calls in a single response. Standard autoregressive decoding generates these calls token by token, incurring substantial latency for requests involving multiple calls or many argument fields. The explicit argument structure offers opportunities for parallel generation, but later argument values may depend on preceding fields and calls, so independently generated values can differ from the target model's output. We present SchemaFill, a framework for efficient LLM tool calling through slot-parallel speculative decoding. SchemaFill generates future slot values concurrently as candidates, without requiring advance knowledge of the actual call sequence or argument values. Candidates spanning multiple fields and calls are concatenated for verification by the target model under the actual output prefix. Only verified tokens are committed, and the target supplies corrections when candidates disagree. This applies target verification while exploiting parallelism across slots and calls. On Glaive and BFCL, SchemaFill achieves up to a 4.05$\times$ improvement in end-to-end throughput over autoregressive decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/Czzzk/SchemaFill.

</details>


### [35] [Muon Is Theoretically Wrong For Convolutions, But Empirically Effective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07103)
*Thibaut Boissin,Thomas Massena,Mathieu Serrurier,Franck Mamalet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对Muon优化器在卷积核上被展平为矩阵、破坏其理论解释的问题，直接在卷积算子几何中形式化优化目标，提出Conv-NS来近似该几何下的极因子，实验发现其与展平版Muon性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器对矩阵更新有清晰解释，但卷积核以四维张量存储，标准实现将其展平为矩阵，这一捷径破坏了背后的理论理解。作者希望探究在卷积算子几何中直接定义的优化目标会发生什么。

Method: 在卷积算子几何中直接形式化优化目标，并提出Convolutional Newton-Schulz (Conv-NS)，在保持核支撑的前提下近似该几何下的极因子，并在快速训练实验中与基于展平的Muon对比。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与ImageNet分类任务上，Conv-NS与展平版Muon计算效率相近且精度相当；理论上更契合的Conv-NS并未超过展平版Muon。

Conclusion: 作者假设精确的卷积正交化可能过度约束更新，从而解释实践与理论理解之间的落差。结果凸显Muon的强实践性能，也为卷积场景下的进一步改进开辟方向。

Abstract: Muon, an optimizer known for its efficiency, has a clear interpretation for matrix-valued updates, but convolutional kernels are stored as four-dimensional tensors. Standard implementations reshape these tensors into matrices, a shortcut which breaks the theoretical understanding behind Muon. To investigate this, we formalize the corresponding optimization objective directly in convolutional operator geometry and introduce Convolutional Newton-Schulz (Conv-NS), which approximates the polar factor in this geometry while preserving kernel support. When applied in fast training experiments, Conv-NS and reshape-based Muon are both computationally efficient and achieve comparable accuracy on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet classification tasks. However, as one could expect a theoretically aligned Conv-NS to outperform reshape-based Muon, we investigate this mismatch between practice and theoretical understanding, with the hypothesis that exact convolutional orthogonalization may overconstrain updates. These findings highlight Muon's strong practical performance while opening directions for its further development on convolutions. Our code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/thib-s/muonconv-cifar10-airbench}{github conv-muon}.

</details>


### [36] [LiLib: Lifelong Air-to-Ground Path-Loss Prediction on UAVs via a Drift-Triggered Model Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07111)
*Minh Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiLib 是一种轻量级持续学习方案，让无人机维护一个小型递归最小二乘专家库，通过窗口残差检测漂移并在探测后复用或新建专家，以应对空对地路径损耗预测中环境的反复变化。在四类城市化剖面仿真中，其 RMSE 从最佳滑窗基线的 5.89 dB 降至 4.03 dB，重返已知环境的误差从 12.3 dB 降至 5.7 dB，速率自适应吞吐恢复达独立已知机制 oracle 的 99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机作为中继或基站需要准确的空对地路径损耗预测以支持速率自适应和部署，但无人机在郊区、城区和高层建筑区之间移动时传播条件不断变化，且相同区域常被反复访问。在线回归器靠遗忘来适应环境，每次都要从头重学；而用全部数据训练单一模型又会平均掉互不兼容的机制。

Method: 提出 LiLib：无人机维护一个小型递归最小二乘（RLS）专家库。用带窗口的残差检验检测漂移，随后经过一个短探测阶段，选择复用库中最优的既有专家，或创建新专家。基于四种标准城市化剖面进行仿真评估。

Result: 相比最佳滑窗基线，预测 RMSE 从 5.89 dB 降至 4.03 dB（p < 0.001）；重返已知环境后不久的误差从 12.3 dB 降至 5.7 dB；速率自适应中恢复独立机制 oracle 吞吐量的 99%。专家库以不足 0.5 KB 存储四个专家，无标签条件下机制辨识纯度为 92%。当第二架无人机用同伴的库初始化时，环境变化后的误差减半。

Conclusion: LiLib 未达到 oracle 水平，且当阴影效应较强时相似机制可能被合并。结果表明，对于反复出现的漂移，记忆比重新适应更有效。

Abstract: UAVs that act as relays or base stations need accurate air-to-ground path-loss predictions for rate adaptation and placement, but propagation conditions change as a UAV moves between suburban, urban and high-rise areas, and the same areas are often revisited. Online regressors that adapt by forgetting must relearn each environment from scratch, whereas a single model trained on all data averages incompatible regimes. We propose LiLib, a lightweight continual-learning scheme in which a UAV maintains a small library of recursive-least-squares experts. A windowed residual test detects drift; a short probe phase then either reuses the best stored expert or creates a new one. In simulations based on four standard urbanization profiles, LiLib reduces prediction RMSE from 5.89 dB (best sliding-window baseline) to 4.03 dB (p < 0.001), lowers the error shortly after a return to a known environment from 12.3 dB to 5.7 dB, and recovers 99% of the throughput of a regime-aware oracle in rate adaptation. The library stores four experts in under 0.5 KB, and identifies regimes with 92% purity without labels. When a second UAV is initialized with the library of a peer, its error after environment changes halves. LiLib does not reach the oracle, and similar regimes may be merged when shadowing is strong. The results indicate that, for recurring drift, remembering is more effective than re-adapting.

</details>


### [37] [Sample-Optimal Estimation of the Fréchet Inception Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07114)
*Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Kevin Tian,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究已知一个分布时，两个d维高斯分布间FID估计的样本复杂度，给出经验插值估计器的紧致偏差与方差界，提出任意阶外推去偏框架与RTD算法，并证明RTD达到最优的O(d/ε²)样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID被广泛用于评估生成模型，但其经验插值估计器存在有限样本偏差，缺乏对样本复杂度的严格理论刻画。

Method: 在均值距离与协方差有界、一个分布已知的高斯设定下，建立经验插值估计器的紧致有限样本偏差与方差界；将FID_∞估计器推广到任意阶k的外推方法；提出受U统计量启发的相对泰勒去偏（RTD）算法。

Result: 经验插值估计器偏差为Θ(d²/n)、方差为Θ(d/n + d²/n²)，样本复杂度约为d²；任意阶k外推偏差为Θ(d^{k+2}/n^{k+1})、方差为Θ(d/n + d²/n²)；RTD达到O(d/ε²)样本复杂度并证明其最优。合成高斯实验验证了预测的残余偏差与界的紧致性；ImageNet上RTD在50K样本预算下平均估计误差最低，VALE₂仅用10K样本即达到FID_∞在50K样本的精度。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上刻画了FID估计的样本复杂度并证明RTD最优，同时通过实验验证理论并展示RTD与VALE₂在实际生成模型评估中的高效性。

Abstract: The Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate generative models, but its empirical plug-in estimator suffers from finite-sample bias [BSAG18, CF20]. We study the sample complexity $n$ of estimating FID to error $ε$ between $d$-dimensional Gaussians with bounded mean distance and covariances, when one distribution is known. Our contributions are threefold. (1) We establish tight finite-sample $Θ(\frac{d^2}{n})$ bias and $Θ(\frac{d}{n} + \frac {d^2} {n^2})$ variance bounds for the empirical plug-in estimator, establishing a $\gtrsim d^2$ sample complexity. (2) To debias the empirical plug-in estimator, we generalize the ${\rm FID}_\infty$ estimator of [CF20] to extrapolation methods of arbitrary order $k$. (3) We introduce Relative Taylor Debiasing (RTD), a new, computationally efficient FID estimation algorithm using debiasing techniques inspired by U-statistics. We show that RTD achieves an $O(\frac d {ε^2})$ sample complexity, and prove that this is optimal. We provide a complementary empirical evaluation of our new estimators. Our experiments on synthetic Gaussians validate the predicted residual bias and support the tightness of our bounds. On ImageNet with Inception embeddings, RTD achieves the lowest mean estimation error at the standard 50K sample budget, while our second-order variance-aware extrapolation estimator (VALE$_2$) uses only 10K samples to achieve accuracy comparable to FID$_\infty$ at 50K samples.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选回复的呈现顺序会改变LLM评判者的裁决（位置翻转）。常规检测需对每对样本正反两种顺序各评判一次，成本翻倍，因此希望寻找一种低成本的事前预测方法。

Result: 线性探针达到.621-.850的AUROC，比组合基线高.062-.113 AUROC；在未做MT-Bench拟合或重校准的情况下，跨数据集AUROC为.685-.853。

Abstract: The order in which candidate responses are presented can change an LLM judge's verdict. Detecting such a position flip ordinarily requires judging each pair in both orders, which doubles the number of judgments. We investigate whether residual stream activations recorded immediately before the initial verdict can predict a flip. We use nested grouped cross-validation to evaluate regularized linear probes on 534 JudgeBench pairs for three Qwen3 judges and Llama-3.1-8B. The linear probes achieve AUROCs of .621-.850 and outperform a combined baseline that uses verbalized confidence, verdict-label logits, response lengths, and the judge's initial choice by .062-.113 AUROC. Linear probes trained on JudgeBench and then frozen achieve AUROCs of .685-.853 on 1,802 MT-Bench comparisons without MT-Bench fitting or recalibration. These results show that pre-verdict activations support prediction of susceptibility to candidate order and outperform the non-activation predictors evaluated here.

</details>


### [39] [SoloQ: Calibration-Free Quantization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07121)
*Donghyun Lee,Arkapravo Ghosh,Varun Manjunath,Bumjoon Kyle Rhee,Hyunho Kook,Shiting Xiao,Youngeun Kim,Priyadarshini Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoloQ：面向扩散大语言模型的无校准量化框架，通过旋转基变换实现数据无关量化，支持4-bit与NVFP4，显著降低内存并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）因模型规模增大和推理成本高而难以高效部署：全序列去噪需反复前向计算，块扩散模型还引入内存密集的KV-cache。低比特权重-激活量化虽有吸引力，但现有后训练量化方法依赖校准数据，而激活分布在掩码状态和去噪步之间会发生变化，导致校准不可靠。

Method: 提出SoloQ无校准量化框架，将权重和激活映射到具有可预测边缘分布的归一化旋转基，实现数据无关量化。方法结合结构化K-RPBH旋转与轻量重缩放校正；旋转后可预测分布支持分布匹配码本与硬件原生NVFP4。针对块扩散模型，进一步引入提交时KV-cache量化，在压缩持久状态的同时不干扰当前正在去噪的块。

Result: 在完整的序列扩散模型（LLaDA、Dream）和块扩散模型（Fast-dLLM v2、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion）上，SoloQ在4-bit量化下保持精度，并在知识密集和推理密集型基准上优于基于校准的基线。使用NVFP4时，峰值内存最高降低2.61倍，端到端推理最高加速2.24倍。

Conclusion: SoloQ无需校准数据即可实现有效的低比特量化，兼容硬件原生NVFP4，在多种扩散语言模型上兼顾精度与效率，为dLLM的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models through bidirectional diffusion-based token generation. However, their growing model sizes and high inference costs make efficient deployment challenging: full-sequence denoising repeatedly invokes compute-intensive forward passes, while block-diffusion models additionally introduce a memory-intensive KV-cache. Low-bit weight-activation quantization is therefore attractive, yet existing dLLM post-training quantization methods rely on calibration data despite activation distributions shifting across masking states and denoising steps. We present SoloQ, a calibration-free quantization framework that maps weights and activations into a normalized rotated basis with a predictable marginal distribution, enabling data-independent quantization. SoloQ combines a structured K-RPBH rotation with a lightweight rescaling correction for calibration-free quantization. Its predictable post-rotation distribution supports both distribution-matched codebooks and hardware-native NVFP4. For block-diffusion models, SoloQ further applies commit-time KV-cache quantization to compress persistent states without perturbing the actively denoised block. Across full-sequence dLLMs (LLaDA and Dream) and block-diffusion dLLMs(Fast-dLLM v2 and Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion), SoloQ retains accuracy under 4-bit quantization and outperforms calibration-based baselines on knowledge- and reasoning-intensive benchmarks. With NVFP4, SoloQ reduces peak memory by up to 2.61X and accelerates end-to-end inference by up to 2.24X.

</details>


### [40] [The Implicit Bias of Hyperbolic Representation Learning for Multiclass Data: A Busemann Risk Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07131)
*Xingrun Li,Sho Kuno,Yusuke Mukuta,Xin Yang,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲多类分类中黎曼梯度流的隐式偏置，在固定类原型设定下，通过将大半径处的原型距离分解为径向项与Buseman函数描述的方向项，证明了径向二分现象（漂移系数μ的符号决定半径趋向边界还是返回内部）以及边界方向收敛到Buseman风险的临界点，为双曲表示学习中的边界饱和与近边界聚类提供了渐近理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间因能高效表示层次结构而被广泛用于表示学习，其中"边界饱和"和"近边界聚类"是常见现象，但缺乏严格的渐近理论分析。作者希望从隐式偏置角度理解黎曼梯度流为何及何时将表示推向理想边界。

Method: 针对双曲空间𝔥ⁿ中固定类原型的多类分类，采用一般排列不变相对间隔（PERM）损失（含交叉熵等标准损失）。利用大半径处距离分解：径向项 + 由Buseman函数刻画的方向依赖项，进而分析黎曼梯度流的渐近行为。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：(1) 径向二分：漂移系数μ的符号决定半径被推向理想边界还是拉回内部；若正漂移持续，则r(t)=½log t+O(1)；若负漂移持续，轨迹在有限时间内返回大半径阈值。(2) 边界方向收敛到∂𝔥ⁿ上Buseman风险的临界点。

Conclusion: 该工作为双曲表示学习中的边界饱和与近边界聚类现象提供了严格的渐近视角，揭示了梯度流隐式偏置由漂移系数符号和Buseman风险临界点共同决定。

Abstract: We study the implicit bias of Riemannian gradient flow for hyperbolic multiclass classification with fixed class prototypes in hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^n$. Our framework accommodates general permutation invariant relative margin (PERM) losses, a class that includes cross entropy and other standard multiclass losses. Our analysis is based on a decomposition: at large radius, the distance to each prototype splits into a radial term and a direction-dependent term described by the Busemann function. This yields two main results. First, we prove a radial dichotomy: the sign of a drift coefficient $μ$ determines whether the radius is pushed toward the ideal boundary or back toward the interior; if the positive drift persists, then $r(t)=\frac{1}{2}\log t+O(1)$, while persistent negative drift returns the trajectory to the large-radius threshold in finite time. Second, we show that the boundary direction converges to a critical point of the Busemann risk on $\partial\mathbb{H}^n$. These results provide a rigorous asymptotic perspective on two phenomena we refer to as boundary saturation and near-boundary clustering in hyperbolic representation learning.

</details>


### [41] [Adversarial Training for Deep Hedging in Nonstationary Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07162)
*Philipp J. Schneider,Lukas Looser,Antoine Garin,Shuhan Liu,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WRAP框架，基于双预算DRO公式，通过重加权与路径扰动两种对抗手段提升深度对冲在非平稳市场下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度对冲从历史或模拟轨迹学习交易策略，但在非平稳条件下，训练路径可能无法代表未来市场状况，导致策略泛化性不足。

Method: 提出WRAP（Wasserstein-Reweighting Adversarial Perturbation），源于双预算分布鲁棒优化（DRO）。以加权经验参考分布为锚，基线权重平衡采样不确定性与时间漂移；模糊集允许对抗者施加φ-散度约束重加权轨迹，并以最优传输（OT）约束扰动路径。推导联合一阶展开，将分布内上确界近似为有限维对抗攻击。

Result: 联合一阶展开将名义期望损失的超额增量分解为重加权贡献（由对冲损失跨轨迹的离散度决定）与传输贡献（由损失对路径扰动的敏感性决定）。在平稳与非平稳Heston动力学及广义仿射扩散（GAD）实验中，重加权与传输带来互补收益，联合对抗训练在非平稳下增益最大。

Conclusion: WRAP通过可处理的一阶对抗攻击统一了重加权与路径扰动，在非平稳市场下显著提升深度对冲鲁棒性，两种机制具有互补性。

Abstract: Deep hedging learns trading policies from historical or simulated market trajectories, yet under nonstationarity these training paths may not represent future market conditions. We propose WRAP (Wasserstein-Reweighting Adversarial Perturbation), a drift-aware adversarial training framework derived from a two-budget distributionally robust optimization (DRO) formulation. The formulation is anchored to a weighted empirical reference distribution whose fixed baseline weights are chosen to balance sampling uncertainty against temporal drift. Around this reference distribution, the ambiguity set addresses two complementary forms of distributional misspecification by allowing an adversary to reweight the observed trajectories subject to a $φ$-divergence constraint and perturb their paths subject to an optimal-transport (OT) constraint. We derive a joint first-order expansion in which the leading-order increase over the nominal expected loss decomposes into a reweighting contribution determined by the dispersion of hedging losses across trajectories and a transport contribution determined by the sensitivity of the loss to path perturbations. This expansion yields an explicit finite-dimensional adversarial attack that replaces the distributional inner supremum with a tractable first-order approximation. Across stationary and nonstationary Heston dynamics and a generalized affine diffusion (GAD), the experiments show complementary benefits from reweighting and transport, with joint adversarial training providing the largest gains under nonstationarity.

</details>


### [42] [A theory of platonic representations in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07168)
*Darshil Doshi,Wenjie Zhou,Corinna Elena Wegner,Daniel J. Korchinski,Santiago Acevedo,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于数据具有跨语言共享抽象层级、语言特定表层结构的假设，用概率上下文无关文法生成合成语言，证明贝叶斯最优预测器为信念传播，从而理论解释多语言模型中间层跨语言相似性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型内层中翻译句子的表示相似，这一现象与"柏拉图表示假说"相关，但缺乏理论解释。作者希望从数据层级结构和预测机制出发，给出可验证的理论框架。

Method: 假设数据具有隐藏层级结构，抽象层跨语言共享而表层为语言或模态特有。用概率上下文无关文法生成合成语言，上层产生式规则共享、下层不共享。在此设定下，贝叶斯最优下一词预测为信念传播，将消息编码到连续层中得到解析预测，并与相同数据训练的Transformer对比验证。

Result: 解析预测与Transformer训练结果吻合良好。框架解释了跨语言相似性为何在中间层达到峰值、可与语言特定结构共存，并随语言接近度、模型质量和数据暴露增加而增强。区分了"相似性"（共享邻域几何）与"对齐"（共享坐标），显示后者在混合语言句子数据充足时出现。并预测从每层减去前一层线性可预测成分可提升跨语言相似性，在预训练大模型中验证成立。

Conclusion: 该工作从数据层级结构和信念传播角度，为多语言模型跨语言表示相似性提供了理论解释，区分了相似性与对齐，并提出可实证检验的层间去线性成分预测，得到预训练大模型验证。

Abstract: Representations of translated sentences are similar in the inner layers of multilingual language models -- an observation connected to the platonic representation hypothesis, yet unexplained theoretically. We provide an explanation based on the assumption that data have a hidden hierarchical structure whose abstract levels are shared across languages while surface levels are modality- or language-specific. Concretely, we generate synthetic languages from probabilistic context-free grammars sharing upper-level but not lower-level production rules. In this setting the Bayes-optimal next-token predictor is belief propagation (BP); encoding its messages in successive layers yields analytical predictions that agree well with transformers trained on the same data. The framework explains why cross-lingual similarity peaks in middle layers, coexists with language-specific structure, and strengthens with language proximity, model quality and data exposure. It distinguishes similarity (shared neighborhood geometry) from alignment (shared coordinates), showing that the latter occurs when code-switched data, i.e. mixed-language sentences, are abundant enough. It further predicts that subtracting from each layer the component linearly predictable from the preceding one increases cross-lingual similarity, which we confirm in pretrained LLMs.

</details>

*Gowthamkumar Nandakishore*

TL;DR: 该论文评估了一个开放对比决策模型（CLM-v0.1-8B）作为评判者的能力，发现其在多个公开基准上接近随机水平，且存在明显的过度自信问题；尽管温度校准能显著改善置信度质量，但校准后的置信度几乎无法用于级联筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验一个开放的对比决策模型在困难公开基准上作为评判者（judge）的实际能力，并与同等参数量的生成式评判者和奖励模型进行公平、预注册的比较，同时考察其置信度是否可用于可靠的决策级联。

Method: 在五个公开偏好基准和一个带真实标签的幻觉基准上进行评测，采用预先冻结的预注册方案，对比生成式评判者、奖励模型和简单平凡基线，并发布逐项预测；同时分析置信度校准（池化温度拟合）与决策顺序翻转率、长度偏好偏移等性质。

Result: CLM得分仅0.351（四选一，随机0.250）至0.593（成对，随机0.500），在RM-Bench和JudgeBench上与抛硬币无统计差异，在HaluEval上对所有样本输出同一常量标签（0.581，等同always-first基线）；同参数量奖励模型达0.764–0.976，生成式评判者达0.611–0.778，所有差距经Benjamini-Hochberg校正后显著。原始置信度过度自信最高达+0.401，单一池化温度拟合将ECE修复至≤0.062，修复后的置信度在六个基准中的三个能高于随机地区分自身错误。顺序翻转率为0.0002（生成式评判者为0.2188），长度偏好偏移为-0.023（生成式评判者为-0.217）。置信度门控级联在预注册0.97保留阈值下需将0.923–1.000的样本升级给强评判者。

Conclusion: 该开放对比决策模型在困难公开基准上接近随机，远逊于同等参数量的奖励模型和生成式评判者；虽然温度校准能有效修复置信度，但对一个接近随机的评判者而言，校准后的置信度几乎没有可保留的判别价值，导致级联几乎全部升级，实用性有限。

Abstract: An open contrastive decision model is near chance as a judge on the hard public benchmarks: Contrastive-LM/CLM-v0.1-8B scores between 0.351 (best- of-four, chance 0.250) and 0.593 (pairwise, chance 0.500), is statistically indistinguishable from coin flipping on RM-Bench and JudgeBench, and answers every HaluEval item with one constant label, matching the trivial always-first baseline at 0.581. Judges with the same parameter count score far higher everywhere: a reward model reaches 0.764 to 0.976 and a generative judge 0.611 to 0.778, and every gap to CLM is significant after Benjamini-Hochberg correction. Two properties do work. Raw confidences are overconfident by up to +0.401, yet one pooled temperature fit on held-out calibration items repairs expected calibration error to at most 0.062, and the repaired confidence ranks the model's own errors above chance on three of six benchmarks. The decision order-flip rate is 0.0002 against 0.2188 for the generative judge, and the length-preference shift is -0.023 against -0.217. The confidence-gated cascade, however, escalates between 0.923 and 1.000 of items to the strong judge at the preregistered 0.97 retention bar: calibrated confidence about a near-chance judge has almost nothing to keep. The design: five public preference benchmarks and one hallucination benchmark with real labels, scored under a preregistration frozen before any test item was seen, against generative, reward-model, and trivial baselines, with per-item predictions released.

</details>


### [44] [Learning Scientific Exploration from Human Research Decision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07184)
*Xuchen Gong,Shane Gu,Haokun Liu,Dixi Yao,Chenhao Tan,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ResearchTrails，一个从Git仓库的commit历史中自动抽取人类科研探索轨迹的数据集，将commit作为科研探索过程的代理信号，并验证其在科研经验检索与模型训练中的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建科研AI系统的关键挑战在于实现"科学探索"——即通过一系列研究决策与行动来系统性地探究未知现象、获取新知识的过程。然而现有科学语料（如研究论文）主要记录最终成果，而非产生这些成果的探索轨迹，这一过程信息严重缺失。

Method: 作者开发了一套自动化、可扩展的流水线，从Git仓库的commit历史中提取结构化的科研轨迹，捕捉方法、实验和消融研究的连续变更。随后对数据集进行特征刻画，并设计多个用例进行验证：将人类科研经验作为外部技能在测试时检索，以及在科研轨迹上训练模型。

Result: 分析表明，这些轨迹包含关于中间研究决策的有意义信号，其信息超出最终论文所揭示的内容。在检索人类科研经验作为测试时外部技能、以及用科研轨迹训练模型以提升对新研究决策泛化能力等用例中，ResearchTrails均展现出实用价值。

Conclusion: 研究表明，构建不仅从科学成果中学习、也从发现过程本身学习的AI系统是可行的，为科研AI提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: A key challenge in building AI systems for scientific research is enabling $\textit{scientific exploration}$: the systematic process of investigating unknown phenomena or ideas to gain new knowledge through sequences of research decisions and actions. Yet this process is largely missing from existing scientific corpora; for example, research papers primarily record final outcomes rather than the trajectories that produced them. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{ResearchTrails}$, a dataset of $\textbf{human research trajectories constructed from Git repositories}$, where $\textbf{commit histories}$ serve as proxies for research exploration. We develop an automated and scalable pipeline that extracts structured research trajectories from repository commits, capturing successive changes to methods, experiments, and ablations. We characterize the resulting dataset and show that these trajectories contain meaningful signals about intermediate research decisions beyond what final papers reveal. We further demonstrate utilities of ResearchTrails in multiple use cases, including retrieving human research experience as external skills at test time and training models on research trajectories to improve generalization to new research decisions. Our results suggest a path toward AI systems that learn not only from the products of science, but from the evolving process of discovery itself.

</details>


### [45] [Exact Unlearning via Quantized Sufficient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07197)
*Ami Tavory,Shripad Gade,Tal Sarig,Noam Touitou,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量化充分统计量（QSS），将冻结模式与可加性内容分离，实现删除即精确减法，无需每次请求都重建模型，在多数任务上接近SISA精度且大幅降低删除延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用精确遗忘方法依赖不相交分片来局部化重训练，但每个删除请求仍会使整个模型失效，且分片越小各子模型可用数据越少。需要一种既能保证精确删除、又避免频繁全量重建的方法。

Method: QSS将模型分为小型冻结模式（学习全局结构）与可变、和可分解的内容（以量化区域为索引存储局部预测修正的加性统计量）。删除内容即为精确减法而非优化。区分两种保证：QSS-L精确移除标签但保留无标签输入；QSS-E通过在不含可删除样本的情况下学习模式，精确移除输入与标签两者。删除以概率1-ρ走算术快速路径，以概率ρ触发全量重建，报告延迟包含两种事件。

Result: 在15个视觉、文本和表格数据集、ρ=0.5%条件下，QSS-L在11个任务上与SISA精度差距在2个百分点以内，并在紧凑模式有效的小类别数任务上实现4--483倍的更低期望删除延迟。QSS-E量化了彻底移除输入所有痕迹所带来的额外精度代价。

Conclusion: QSS通过模式与内容分离及量化加性统计，在保证精确遗忘的同时显著降低删除延迟，多数任务精度接近SISA，QSS-E进一步揭示了完全移除输入痕迹的精度成本。

Abstract: Exact unlearning requires a deployed predictor to match one rebuilt without the information named by a deletion request. Existing general-purpose exact methods localize retraining through disjoint shards, but every request still invalidates a model, and smaller shards reduce the data available to each constituent predictor. We introduce Quantized Sufficient Statistics (QSS), which separates a small frozen schema from mutable, sum-decomposable content. The schema learns global structure; the content stores local prediction corrections as additive statistics indexed by quantized regions. Deleting content is therefore exact subtraction rather than optimization. We distinguish two guarantees: QSS-L exactly removes a label while retaining the unlabelled input, whereas QSS-E exactly removes both input and label by learning the schema without deletable examples. A deletion takes the arithmetic fast path with probability $1-ρ$ and triggers a full rebuild with probability $ρ$; all reported expected latencies include both events. Across 15 vision, text, and tabular datasets at $ρ=0.5\%$, QSS-L is within 2 percentage points of SISA on 11 tasks and provides 4--483$\times$ lower expected deletion latency on the low-class-count tasks where a compact schema is effective. QSS-E quantifies the additional accuracy cost of removing every trace of an input.

</details>


### [46] [Distributionally Robust Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07207)
*Xin Teng,Muxiao Li,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRMoET 提出一种分布鲁棒 MoE 训练目标，将每层专家视为鲁棒性分组，优化高损失路由结果而非仅均衡流量，从而提升稀疏 MoE 的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 通过每 token 仅激活少量专家来扩展容量，但路由不完美时，负载均衡模型可能把 token 送入训练不足的专家，形成隐藏的可靠性问题。

Method: DRMoET 作为一种即插即用目标，把逐层专家视为内生鲁棒性分组；基于 EMA 平滑、激活加权的专家损失，用熵正则化 softmax 规则更新每层专家分布，强化合理的非 top 路由路径，同时保持标准 MoE 计算。

Result: 在 FLAME-MoE 配方下（746M 与 10.3B 总参数规模），DRMoET 优于标准 FLAME-MoE 与无辅助损失均衡方法。在 10.3B 总参数、67B 训练 token 下，七任务平均分从 0.6625 提升至 0.6767，无辅助损失基线为 0.6431。机制分析显示专家损失方差更低、均值损失几乎不变，强制 mid-k 误路由下过量损失降低 4.3%，域-专家特化更优。

Conclusion: 结果表明，路由鲁棒性不仅是利用率均衡，可作为可靠稀疏 MoE 扩展的实用目标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) transformers scale capacity by activating only a few experts per token, but this sparsity creates a hidden reliability problem: when routing is imperfect, load-balanced models may send tokens to experts that are insufficiently trained for the assigned inputs. We propose Distributionally Robust MoE Training (DRMoET), a drop-in objective that treats layer-wise experts as endogenous robustness groups and optimizes high-loss routing outcomes rather than merely equalizing traffic. DRMoET updates a per-layer expert distribution by an entropy-regularized softmax rule on EMA-smoothed, activation-weighted expert losses, strengthening plausible non-top routing paths while preserving standard MoE computation. Under the FLAME-MoE recipe at 746M-total and 10.3B-total scales, DRMoET improves downstream averages over both standard FLAME-MoE and auxiliary-loss-free balancing. At 10.3B total parameters and 67B training tokens, DRMoET improves the seven-task average from 0.6625 to 0.6767, while the auxiliary-loss-free baseline achieves 0.6431. Mechanistic analyses show lower expert-loss variance with nearly unchanged mean loss, 4.3% lower excess loss under forced mid-$k$ misrouting, and improved domain-expert specialization. These results position routing robustness-not only utilization balance-as a practical objective for reliable sparse MoE scaling. Project page and code are available at: https://drmoet.github.io/.

</details>


### [47] [Can LLM-assisted regularization increase forecast accuracy for migration flows in low data regimes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07208)
*Nathaniel T. Hindman,Fabricio Murai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索用大语言模型从新闻中提取迁移"推拉"信号，并通过特征特定正则化融入加权Lasso预测框架，预测多条迁移走廊的迁移流，结果显示效果因走廊而异，无统一最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于引力模型的迁移预测主要依赖结构化社会经济指标（经济差距、政治稳定性、地理距离），难以捕捉新闻等非结构化情境信息，预测能力受限。作者希望验证LLM能否从新闻文章中提取与迁移相关的上下文信号来改进预测。

Method: 提出一个LLM增强的预测框架：使用分层LLM推理流水线从新闻数据中分类识别迁移的"推—拉"信号；将这些信号作为特征，通过特征特定的正则化惩罚项融入加权Lasso预测框架；在2021年11月至2022年11月间的多条迁移走廊（墨西哥—美国、乌克兰—波兰、叙利亚—土耳其）上评估预测性能，并对比多种建模策略（如引力模型增强、Direct LLM-Lasso、LLM辅助正则化AR）。

Result: 各走廊与建模策略表现不一，没有单一正则化方法在所有实验中持续占优。墨西哥最佳配置为引力模型加上推拉比率，MAPE为17.15%；叙利亚最佳配置为Direct LLM-Lasso，MAPE为29.29%；乌克兰最佳配置为LLM辅助正则化（AR），MAPE为41.05%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，文章衍生的情境特征与LLM引导的正则化在特定条件下可改善迁移预测，但性能强烈受迁移走廊特征、文章数量及超参数配置影响，LLM方法并非普遍优于传统方法。

Abstract: Predicting migration flows remains a significant challenge for traditional gravity-based forecasting models, which primarily rely on structured socio-economic indicators such as economic disparity, political stability, and geographic distance. This work investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can improve migration forecasting by extracting contextual migration-related signals from news articles and incorporating them into a weighted Lasso forecasting framework through feature-specific regularization penalties. The proposed framework uses hierarchical LLM inference pipelines to classify migration-related push--pull signals from news data and evaluates the resulting forecasting performance across multiple migration corridors between November 2021 and November 2022, including Mexico--United States, Ukraine--Poland, and Syria--Turkey. Experimental results showed mixed performance across migration corridors and modeling strategies, and no single regularization approach consistently outperformed the others across all experiments. The best-performing Mexico configuration, which consisted of a gravity-based model augmented with the proposed push--pull ratios, achieved a Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 17.15%, while the strongest Syria configuration achieved a MAPE of 29.29% using Direct LLM-Lasso. For Ukraine, the best-performing configuration used LLM-Assisted Regularization (AR) and achieved a MAPE of 41.05%. Overall, the results suggest that contextual article-derived features and LLM-guided regularization can improve migration forecasting under certain conditions, although migration corridor characteristics, article volume, and hyperparameter configuration strongly influenced performance.

</details>


### [48] [Reward-Driven Learning under Prompt-Level Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07212)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练提供提示级差分隐私保证：将同一提示的所有回答视为一个隐私记录，聚合其梯度、只裁剪一次、加高斯噪声并跨更新组合隐私损失，使预算不依赖于每题回答数或裁剪范数。在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 上以 LoRA、ε=8 训练，验证奖励信号在隐私条件下仍有效提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 在可能涉密的问题上训练语言模型，训练后的模型会泄露它见过哪些问题，因此需要隐私保护；但此前没有针对 RLVR 训练的差分隐私保证。

Method: 在提示级差分隐私下训练 RLVR：把同一提示的多条回答归为一个隐私记录，聚合其梯度，对该提示的贡献只裁剪一次，加入高斯噪声，并跨更新步组合隐私损失；预算与每题回答数量及裁剪范数无关。以 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 加 LoRA、每轮 ε=8 进行训练。

Result: 在相同提示与相同预算下，与仅去掉奖励信号的对照组及两种私有监督微调方案相比：奖励信号在 MATH 上提升 2.65 分、GSM8K 上提升 3.24 分，且在每个随机种子下均成立；该提升在格式稳健评分器下仍存在（MATH 上 1.3 分），且不能由回答长度解释。同预算下私有模型在 MATH 与 GSM8K 上优于两种监督方案 2.3 至 3.8 分，保留非私有 GRPO 增益的 85–90%；在 MATH 上，预算收紧八倍带来的噪声最多损失 1.2 分。奖励效应还延伸到非数学任务 CommonsenseQA。

Conclusion: 在提示级隐私约束下，验证器反馈仍是可用的学习信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains a language model on problems that may themselves be confidential, and the trained model can reveal which problems it saw. We study RLVR under prompt-level differential privacy: the released weights must be (ε,δ)-differentially private with respect to the presence of any one training problem. Taking the group of responses to one prompt as the privacy record, our method aggregates their gradients, clips the prompt's contribution once, adds Gaussian noise, and composes the privacy loss across updates, so the budget depends on neither the number of responses per prompt nor the clipping norm; to our knowledge this is the first differential privacy guarantee for RLVR training. We train Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct with LoRA at a per-run budget of ε=8 and compare, on the same prompts and at the same budget, a control that removes only the reward signal and two private supervised fine-tuning recipes. The reward signal improves accuracy over the control by 2.65 points on MATH and 3.24 on GSM8K, in every seed; the improvement survives a format-robust scorer, at 1.3 points on MATH, and is not explained by response length. At the same budget the private model outperforms both supervised recipes on MATH and GSM8K by 2.3 to 3.8 points, retains 85--90% of the gain of non-private GRPO on these tasks, and on MATH the noise of an eightfold tighter budget costs at most 1.2 points. The reward effect also carries to CommonsenseQA, an exploratory non-mathematical task. Verifier feedback thus remains a usable learning signal under prompt-level privacy.

</details>


### [49] [Constant-Curvature Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein for Heterogeneous Cross-Curvature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07218)
*Shanglin Li,Wenjing Lu,Muyang Li,Nicu Sebe,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出常曲率切片Gromov-Wasserstein（CCSGW）散度，用于对齐异构常曲率空间上的概率分布，并应用于多种混合曲率学习任务中取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合曲率模型整合了多种常曲率组件，但不同曲率空间本质异构、缺乏统一度量，因此通常被独立学习，缺少跨空间强制几何一致性的显式机制；同时跨混合曲率空间比较概率分布的问题尚未被探索。

Method: 利用Gromov-Wasserstein距离通过对齐空间内部几何来比较异构分布的原理，提出CCSGW散度。首先为球面空间引入缺失的基于测地线的一维投影，然后将切片GW扩展到常曲率空间，实现对不同曲率流形间高效且有原则的比较，保留内在几何关系并避免高计算成本。

Result: 理论分析表明CCSGW能够控制异构空间间的内在几何差异，促进分布层面的几何一致性。将CCSGW整合到图异常检测、图节点分类和多模态学习等混合曲率学习任务中，在多种设置下均获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: CCSGW为异构常曲率空间上的分布比较提供了有原则且高效的框架，并能有效提升现有混合曲率学习任务的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in representation learning have highlighted the utility of constant-curvature models, such as hyperbolic and spherical spaces, for modeling complex data. Mixed-curvature models further enhance this by integrating multiple constant-curvature components. However, these models typically learn each component space independently because spaces with different curvatures are inherently heterogeneous and lack a unified metric. Consequently, they lack explicit mechanisms to enforce geometric consistency across various spaces. Moreover, the problem of comparing probability distributions across mixed-curvature spaces remains unexplored. To compare distributions on heterogeneous spaces, Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distances provide a principled framework by aligning their intra-space geometries. Building on this, we propose constant-curvature sliced Gromov-Wasserstein (CCSGW), a novel divergence for aligning distributions supported on heterogeneous constant-curvature spaces. We first introduce the missing geodesic-based one-dimensional projections for spherical spaces, and then extend sliced GW to constant-curvature spaces, enabling efficient and principled comparison across manifolds with different curvatures. This formulation preserves intrinsic geometric relationships while avoiding the high computational cost. We provide theoretical analysis showing that CCSGW controls intrinsic geometric discrepancy across heterogeneous spaces, promoting distribution-level geometric consistency. By integrating CCSGW into existing mixed-curvature learning tasks, including graph anomaly detection, graph node classification, and multimodal learning, we observe consistent performance gains across diverse settings.

</details>


### [50] [Data, Numbers, and Geometry: Three Tutorials on Numerical Methods, Machine Learning, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07220)
*Jessica N. Howard,Yidi Qi,Tomás S. R. Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了三个用于数学研究的数值计算与机器学习实践教程，涵盖外微积分的数值方法、结构化神经网络设计以及机器学习结果的评估与展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数学研究者提供可复现的数值计算与机器学习教程，帮助区分数值一致性、预测精度、结构保证与严格界等不同类型的证据。

Method: 教程一：基于微分形式的逐点求值，采用外微分的通量公式进行数值外微积分；教程二：结合椭圆曲线、箭图与边值问题探讨数学结构如何指导神经网络设计，并用区间算术界定网络残差；教程三：讨论性能指标、统计不确定性、分类阈值、ROC曲线与图表设计。

Result: 通过欧几里得空间与球面等例子展示了几何恒等式、拓扑特征以及近似与有限精度的影响；揭示了架构选择对学习的影响，并实现了对边值问题全区间残差的严格界定。

Conclusion: 三个教程可独立阅读，配有笔记本和练习，便于读者复现示例并将方法推广至其他问题。

Abstract: We present three practical tutorials on numerical computation and machine learning for mathematical research, developed for the DANGER: Data, Numbers, and Geometry workshop held at the Banff International Research Station in April 2026. The first develops a numerical approach to exterior calculus from pointwise evaluations of differential forms, using a flux formulation of the exterior derivative. Examples in Euclidean space and on the sphere illustrate geometric identities, topological features, and the effects of approximation and finite precision. The second examines how mathematical structure guides neural network design through examples involving elliptic curves, quivers, and a boundary value problem. It explores how architectural choices affect learning and uses interval arithmetic to bound the residual of a trained network over the full interval of the boundary value problem. The third addresses the evaluation and presentation of machine learning results, covering performance metrics, statistical uncertainty, classification thresholds, receiver operating characteristic curves, and accessible figure design. Throughout, the tutorials distinguish numerical agreement, predictive accuracy, structural guarantees, and rigorous bounds as different forms of evidence. Each contribution can be read independently, with accompanying notebooks and exercises that allow readers to reproduce the examples and adapt the methods to other problems.

</details>


### [51] [Minimal Witness Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07226)
*T. Y. Tsui,Zihao Ye,Pengxiang Cai,Yanchao Li,Yuqiang Li,Zhehong Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"寻找充分且不可约的见证"形式化为最小见证识别问题，并提出MWRL方法从单一黑盒验证器反馈中学习出完整的极小见证族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算与科学中，"产生某结果所需的不可约充分条件是什么"是反复出现的核心问题，其答案即最小充分见证，也是我们所说的解释、机制与理由。这类问题通常要求给出多个最小见证，但标准强化学习方法往往只能揭示一个解或给出冗余解。

Method: 论文将问题形式化为最小见证识别，并提出最小见证强化学习（MWRL）。MWRL取策略采样中成功提案所认证集合的并集，并根据去掉某提案后该组并集所损失的覆盖度为其分配信用。该信用分配直接由问题定义导出，仅凭一个黑盒验证器比特即可统一最小性与多样解恢复的需求。基于此原理，作者推导出可恢复整个见证族的值迭代规划器，以及可扩展到大语言模型的策略梯度方法。

Result: 在不同实验设置下，MWRL能恢复大多数最小见证，而其他方法只能返回冗余超集或单一见证。

Conclusion: MWRL使见证族能够从验证器反馈中学习，将强化学习的适用范围从单解优化拓展到多解（见证族）学习。代码已开源。

Abstract: ``What are the irreducible conditions that are sufficient to produce an outcome?'' is one of the most common questions that recur across computation and science. Its answers, the minimal sufficient witnesses, are what we mean by explanations, mechanisms and reasons. These problems usually ask for multiple minimal witnesses, yet standard RL methods may reveal only one solution or redundant ones. We formalize this problem as minimal-witness identification and introduce Minimal-Witness Reinforcement Learning (MWRL). MWRL takes the union of the sets certified by successful proposals sampled from the policy and credits each proposal for the coverage the group union would lose without that proposal. This credit assignment, derived directly from the problem definition, unifies the demands for minimality and recovery of alternatives from a single black-box verifier bit. Under this principle, we derive a value iteration planner that recovers the entire family of witnesses and a policy gradient method that can scale to large language models. Across different experimental settings, MWRL recovers most minimal witnesses, while other methods return redundant supersets or a single witness. By making witness families learnable from verifier feedback, MWRL expands the scope of reinforcement learning beyond single-solution optimization. Our code is available at https://github.com/TSUITUENYUE/MWRL.

</details>


### [52] [Conditional Flow Matching for Transport Between Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07229)
*Syamantak Kumar,Dheeraj Nagaraj,Saptarshi Roy,Purnamrita Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫过程轨迹之间的输运问题，提出基于流匹配的算法学习从源分布到目标分布的轨迹输运映射，同时保持马尔可夫结构，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受时间序列域适应中序列到序列输运问题的启发，需要在仅有有限数量源与目标轨迹的情况下学习轨迹分布的输运映射，并保持马尔可夫结构。

Method: 提出基于流匹配的算法，学习从源到目标轨迹分布的输运映射并保留马尔可夫结构；在混合时间假设下进行理论分析，推导有限样本误差界，并构造下界证明混合时间相关的样本复杂度不可避免。

Result: 证明算法在总体极限下的一致性并给出有限样本误差界；下界构造表明即使具有规则高斯条件转移，混合时间相关的样本复杂度也无法避免。在合成与真实数据上评估，在THINGS-EEG2的EEG图像检索任务中，将ENIGMA解码器平均top-5检索准确率从43.87%提升至49.05%，相对提升11.82%。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论与实验上均验证了马尔可夫轨迹输运的有效性，并在EEG图像检索任务中取得显著的准确率提升。

Abstract: Motivated by sequence-to-sequence transport in the context time-series domain adaptation, we study the problem of transportation between trajectories of Markov processes. Given a limited number of trajectories from source distribution and the target distribution, we formulate a flow matching based algorithm which learns a transport map from the source to target trajectory distribution, while preserving the Markov structure. We show that this is consistent in the population limit and derive finite-sample error bounds under mixing time assumptions, following the analysis of classical statistical problems including regression (Nagaraj et al., 2020), principal component analysis (Kumar and Sarkar, 2023), and matrix concentration (Neeman et al., 2024) in the Markov setting. We complement that with a lower-bound construction showing that a mixing-time dependent sample complexity is unavoidable even with regular Gaussian conditional transitions. We evaluate on synthetic and real-world data. For image retrieval from electroencephalography (EEG) on THINGS-EEG2 (Gifford et al., 2022), the task is to identify the viewed image from EEG signals captured from human subjects, which suffers from high inter subject variability. We augment the ENIGMA decoder (Kneeland et al., 2026) with a conditional flow before its subject-specific temporal map. This improves mean top-5 retrieval accuracy from 43.87% to 49.05%, an 11.82% relative improvement.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking Time Series Foundation Models for Load Forecasting Under Covariate Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07232)
*Tomas Kaljevic,Ivan Arzola,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对4个从零训练模型和4个时间序列基础模型在3个真实负荷数据集、不同未来协变量可用性与质量条件下进行基准测试，发现Chronos-2在协变量可用或准确时表现最优，但协变量噪声增大时性能下降，而TimesNet在严重协变量不确定性下更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的短期负荷预测（STLF）对现代电力系统可靠高效运行至关重要。时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）虽在多种预测任务中表现出色，但其在真实运行条件下的STLF效果尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建综合基准，对比4个从零训练（TFS）模型与4个TSFM，在3个真实负荷预测数据集上，设置未来协变量信息可用性与质量不同的运行场景进行评测。

Result: Chronos-2在零样本与微调设置下，当未来协变量可用或预测准确时均持续达到最优性能；但随着协变量预测噪声增加，其性能下降，而TimesNet在严重协变量不确定性下表现出更强的稳健性。

Conclusion: 结果证明了协变量信息增强的TSFM在STLF中的有效性，同时凸显了真实预测应用中稳健协变量建模的关键作用。

Abstract: Accurate short-term load forecasting (STLF) is essential for the reliable and efficient operation of modern power systems. While time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable performance across a wide range of forecasting tasks, their effectiveness for STLF under realistic operational conditions remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a comprehensive benchmark of four trained-from-scratch (TFS) models and four TSFMs across three real-world load forecasting datasets under operational scenarios that differ in the availability and quality of future covariate information. Our results show that Chronos-2 consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings when future covariates are available or accurately forecast. However, its performance degrades as covariate forecasts become increasingly noisy, whereas TimesNet exhibits greater robustness under severe covariate uncertainty. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of covariate-informed TSFMs for STLF while highlighting the critical role of robust covariate modeling in real-world forecasting applications.

</details>


### [54] [Learning What to Distill: Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07247)
*Heng Liang,Xinwen Zhang,Hongchang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BiToK-SD，用双层优化自适应学习自蒸馏中应施加蒸馏的token位置，在数学推理基准上取得最优平均性能且额外计算轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力强但推理成本高，知识蒸馏可将其能力迁移到紧凑模型。On-policy自蒸馏进一步减少对外部大教师模型的依赖并提升小模型推理能力。然而现有方法要么对所有token位置均匀蒸馏，要么用固定启发式准则选token，且对选中位置赋予相同蒸馏强度，无法自适应学习哪些token最有利于蒸馏。

Method: 提出BiToK-SD（Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation），基于双层优化的token选择方法，在on-policy自蒸馏过程中学习蒸馏应施加的位置。将Top-K token选择建模为可微的阈值松弛形式，作为下层问题，使选中位置随学生策略演化而自适应调整；上层问题则在选中位置上执行知识蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，BiToK-SD在所有对比方法中取得最佳平均性能，且仅需轻量的额外计算。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化自适应选择蒸馏token位置，可有效提升on-policy自蒸馏的效果，为紧凑模型推理能力迁移提供高效方案。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong reasoning capabilities, but their high inference costs make knowledge distillation an important approach for transferring such capabilities to compact models in resource-constrained scenarios. On-policy self-distillation further reduces the reliance on external large teacher models while improving the reasoning ability of compact language models. However, existing methods typically either distill all token positions uniformly or select tokens using fixed heuristic criteria, assigning the same distillation strength to the selected positions rather than adaptively learning which tokens are most beneficial for distillation. To address these limitations, we propose BiToK-SD (Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation), a bilevel-optimization-based token selection method that learns where distillation should be applied during on-policy self-distillation. Specifically, BiToK-SD is formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the lower-level problem models Top-K token selection as a differentiable threshold-based relaxation, allowing the selected positions to adapt as the student policy evolves, while the upper-level problem performs knowledge distillation on the selected positions. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that BiToK-SD achieves the best average performance among all compared methods while requiring only lightweight additional computation.

</details>

*Prateik Sinha,Stefania Druga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Neural fields are usually evaluated by how well they reconstruct an observation. We show that this misses two useful properties of a fitted network: how easily it can adapt to new observations, and what its weights retain from earlier ones. We study these properties as adaptation geometry. For images, we meta-learn class-specific initializations, adapt each one to a new image, and measure how much the network must change to fit it. A simple local linear model closely predicts this adaptation cost, while replacing one network's tangent kernel with another's substantially worsens the prediction. Adaptation thus depends on the local geometry of the fitted network, not only on its current reconstruction. For physical fields, we repeatedly fit the same network to observations from a sequence. Its weights then retain information about that history. When two wave histories end at exactly the same observation, the final weights recover the sign of the wave velocity with 68.6% accuracy, whereas the current observation alone contains no such information and gives 50%. These two phenomena are quantitatively linked: tangent-kernel eigenvalues predict both which changes are easy to learn and how quickly they are overwritten by later fitting. Together, these results show that neural fields contain useful information beyond what they currently reconstruct: in how they can change and in how they got there.

</details>


### [56] [Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Saddle Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07255)
*Samantha Chen,Jesse He,Coleman Clougherty,Gal Mishne,Chester Holtz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphPDHG，一种基于 Chambolle-Pock 原始-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法的图神经网络消息传递框架，用于求解一般图鞍点问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合神经算法推理与算法范式，以求解多项式时间内可解甚至计算更难的组合优化问题，尤其是图上的鞍点问题。

Method: 提出 GraphPDHG 消息传递框架，通过模拟 PDHG 迭代来对齐神经网络与算法；理论证明其能高效求解一类图鞍点问题，并可学习加速的 PDHG 算法。实验上评估其作为二阶优化方法（SSNAL）学习热启动的性能，并与非对齐 GNN 基线比较尺寸泛化能力。

Result: 实验支持了加速 PDHG 的学习效果；与 PDHG 对齐带来比非对齐 GNN 基线更强的尺寸泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种求解图上一般优化问题族的新架构，展示了神经算法推理在优化问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Neural algorithmic reasoning, or aligning a neural network with an algorithmic paradigm, has emerged as an approach to solving polynomial-time-solvable and computationally harder combinatorial optimization problems. We propose a new message-passing framework based on the Chambolle-Pock Primal--Dual Hybrid Gradient (PDHG) method called \textsc{GraphPDHG} for solving general graph saddle-point problems. Theoretically, we show that \textsc{GraphPDHG} can efficiently solve a family of graph saddle-point problems by simulating PDHG. We also show that our network can learn an accelerated PDHG algorithm. Experimentally, we support our results on accelerated PDHG by evaluating the performance of our model as a learned warm start for second-order optimization techniques (SSNAL). We also show that alignment with PDHG leads to stronger size generalization than non-aligned graph neural network (GNN) baselines. Overall, we propose a novel architecture for solving a general family of optimization problems on graphs.

</details>


### [57] [Algorithmically Aligned Neural Agglomerative Tree Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07271)
*Robert R Nerem,Pranav Singh,Cheyenne Ward,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NN-linkage，一种可与 Lance-Williams 递推算法对齐的神经网络模型，能够学习任务特定且局部依赖的层次聚类合并规则，同时保持经典链接算法的递归结构与高效推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次聚类的链接算法虽高效，但难以判断哪种合并规则适合特定数据集或任务；而神经方法虽能从数据中学习，却往往失去经典算法的高效性与规模泛化能力。因此需要一种既有学习能力又保留经典算法效率与结构的框架。

Method: 提出 NN-linkage，与 Lance-Williams（LW）递推算法对齐，LW 是定义凝聚层次聚类链接规则连续族的参数化框架，单链接、全链接、平均链接均为其离散特例。模型可学习任务特定、局部依赖的合并规则，并可结合 transformer 编码以逼近全局依赖规则（如鲁棒单链接）。

Result: 理论上证明 NN-linkage 是连续链接函数（含 LW 递推）的通用逼近器，结合 transformer 可逼近全局依赖规则，且能精确实现任意对称常系数 LW 递推（跨所有输入规模）。实验上在时钟树布线与系统发育重建任务中，使用合成与真实数据集，效果优于经典算法与其他神经方法。

Conclusion: 通过直接从目标树学习合并规则，NN-linkage 将高效层次聚类扩展到现有人工设计链接规则无法充分刻画的科学与工程目标中。

Abstract: Linkage algorithms for hierarchical clustering (HC) are a powerful and efficient framework for constructing clustering trees, yet it is often unclear which merge rule best suits a given dataset or task. In contrast, neural approaches can learn from data, but often fail to retain the efficiency and size generalization of classical algorithms. We introduce NN-linkage, a neural network (NN) model that can learn task-specific and locally dependent merge rules while retaining the recursive structure and efficient inference of classical linkage algorithms. In particular, our model is algorithmically aligned with the Lance-Williams (LW) recurrence, a parameterized framework for defining a broad, continuous family of linkage rules for agglomerative HC. Classical methods such as single linkage (SL), complete linkage (CL), and average linkage arise as discrete choices within this broader family. We show that NN-linkage is a universal approximator for continuous linkage functions, including LW recurrences, and, when paired with a transformer encoding, can also approximate globally dependent rules such as robust single-linkage. We further show that NN-linkage can exactly implement any symmetric constant-coefficient LW recurrence across all input sizes. On the empirical front, we evaluate NN-linkage in real-world applications, clock-tree routing and phylogenetic reconstruction, using both synthetic and real datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness over both classical algorithms and other neural approaches. By learning merge rules directly from target trees, NN-linkage extends efficient HC to scientific and engineering objectives not adequately captured by existing hand-designed linkage rules.

</details>


### [58] [Lock-in EP: An In-Situ Training Algorithm for Oscillatory Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07283)
*Sowjanya Tammali,Wilkie Olin-Ammentorp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了锁相平衡传播（LIEP）训练方法，为振荡模拟硬件提供无需独立前向/反向扫描的局部梯度信息，支持原位学习与扰动后性能恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟硬件平台有望比数字架构降低能耗，但要成功应用，大规模模拟系统必须能够应对或从元件变异性中恢复。因此需要一种适用于模拟振荡硬件的训练方法。

Method: 推导并演示锁相平衡传播（LIEP）训练方法；该方法为振荡网络中每个元件提供局部梯度信息，无需独立的前向和后向扫描；并将其表述为三因子更新规则。

Result: LIEP既可用于从头训练（ab-initio training），也可在预训练参数受到扰动时恢复性能；目前该方法仅在浅层网络上得到验证。

Conclusion: LIEP有潜力支持模拟振荡硬件平台的原位学习能力；尽管当前仅验证于浅层网络，但通过替代架构有望扩展到深层和大规模网络，以处理复杂任务。

Abstract: Analog hardware platforms offer the potential to reduce energy consumption over digital architectures, but in order to succeed, large-scale analog systems must also be able to operate with or recover from the variability of their components. Towards this goal, we derive and demonstrate the lock-in equilibrium propagation (LIEP) training method. LIEP provides local gradient information for each component in an oscillatory network without separate forward and backward sweeps, potentially allowing for in-situ learning capabilities on analog oscillatory hardware platforms. We demonstrate that LIEP can be used both for ab-initio training as well as recovering performance when pre-trained parameters are perturbed. We show that LIEP can be formulated as a three-factor update rule, and suggest that although the method is currently only validated on shallow networks, alternate architectures may allow it to extend to deep and large-scale networks addressing complex tasks.

</details>


### [59] [FlexiFlow: Bandit-based Model Switching in ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07286)
*Abhilash Jindal,Todd Nief,Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Shankaradithyaa V,Tvisha Malik,Anshik Sahu,Aaron Schein,Amitabh Chaudhary,Tanu Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FlexiFlow 系统，通过多臂老虎机方法在运行时动态切换模型，在复用中间结果的同时提升准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型难以在所有数据批次上都达到最优准确率；某些数据子集上备用模型表现更好，实验显示切换模型可提升准确率高达 23%。但现有系统缺乏基于性能的自适应模型切换能力，用户只能手动顺序测试模型。

Method: 设计 FlexiFlow 数据流系统，在当前模型准确率低时动态切换到备用模型。系统采用一种新颖的多臂老虎机方法学习模型排序，综合考虑模型运行时间、通过用户自定义断言的概率以及 ML 工作流的计算结构，并指出标准 Thompson 采样在此场景下不足。

Result: 所提方法有效且可扩展到复杂真实 ML 工作流；运行时切换模型并复用中间结果可获得更高准确率，同时相比顺序工作流运行带来 48% 的效率提升。

Conclusion: 在 ML 工作流中动态切换模型并结合中间结果复用，能同时改善准确率与执行效率，为自适应模型选择提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Model optimizations help improve inference performance and accuracy of ML workflows. However, relying on a single model to perform inference across all data batches often fails to maximize accuracy and thus overall performance. In many cases, alternate models could perform better on specific subsets of data where a primary model underperforms. Our experiments with real ML workflows indeed show that switching models improves workflow accuracy by up to 23%. Yet, current systems lack the ability to adaptively switch between models based on performance, forcing users to manually test models in sequence. We present FlexiFlow, a dataflow system that dynamically switches between alternate models when the current model exhibits low accuracy. FlexiFlow learns to rank models using a novel multi-armed bandit approach that accounts for model runtimes, probability of passing user-defined assertions, and the computational structure of the ML workflow. We show that the standard Thompson sampling approach is insufficient for switching models in ML workflows. In contrast, our proposed approaches are effective and scales to complex real-world ML workflows. Experiments show that switching models at runtime while reusing intermediate results provides higher accuracy, but also 48% efficiency gain compared to sequential workflow runs.

</details>


### [60] [ATLAS-AL: Adaptive Trust-Region for Latent Adversarial Searches via Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07323)
*Marsalis Gibson,Claire Tomlin,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ATLAS 是一个基于查询的黑盒对抗攻击框架，将攻击生成建模为主动学习式的水平集估计问题，通过标定近似与局部-全局采样在有限查询预算下发现并采样对抗区域，生成更具代表性的对抗样本集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型系统的安全评估不能仅依赖固定攻击集合的测试，而需要能高效发现"诱导模型失败输入集合"的自适应机制。现有黑盒攻击往往只找到单个对抗样本，难以刻画模型鲁棒性的整体状态。

Method: ATLAS（Adaptive Trust-Regions for Latent Adversarial Searches）将攻击生成转化为主动学习中的水平集估计问题，结合标定近似（calibrated approximations）与局部-全局采样架构定位包含对抗样本的输入区域，并在已发现的对抗区域内采样以构建对抗集合。

Result: 在玩具实验中，ATLAS 在有限查询预算下能比先前工作恢复更多对抗区域；在标准训练与对抗训练的 MNIST、CIFAR、ImageNet 模型上，其生成的对抗样本比 NES、SignHunter、BayesOpt 等查询式黑盒攻击更具代表性。

Conclusion: ATLAS 提供了一个自动化红队框架，可用于开发阶段的学习系统鲁棒性分析，也可用于持续审计以观察系统鲁棒性随时间的变化。

Abstract: Security evaluation of learning-based systems requires more than just testing the system against a fixed collection of attacks. It requires adaptive mechanisms that can efficiently discover \textit{sets} of inputs that induce model failure. We introduce ATLAS (Adaptive Trust-Regions for Latent Adversarial Searches), which is a query-based framework that discovers adversarial input sets for black-box learning systems. ATLAS casts attack generation as an active learning level set estimation problem then combines calibrated approximations with a local-global sampling architecture to find regions of the input space that contain adversarial examples. Once discovered, ATLAS is designed to sample points within these adversarial regions to build adversarial sets that accurately represent the state of robustness of the target model. When applied on toy experiments, we find that ATLAS is able to recover more of the adversarial region under a limited query budget than does previous work. When applied to standard and adversarially trained MNIST, CIFAR, and ImageNet model targets, ATLAS produces better representative attacks than other query-based black-box attacks (NES, SignHunter, BayesOpt). ATLAS represents an automated red-teaming framework that can be used for both analyzing the robustness of learning-based systems under development and continuous auditing to see how the robustness of a system changes over time.

</details>


### [61] [Scale-Invariant Training for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07324)
*Ignacy Stepka,Willa Potosnak,Kin G. Olivares,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了时间序列基础模型训练中仿射缩放方法（如ReVIN）反变换导致的"尺度污染训练"问题，并提出在缩放后目标上计算损失的"尺度不变训练"方案，仅需一行代码改动即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在大规模、多领域数据集上训练，序列幅度差异巨大。ReVIN等仿射缩放方法在计算损失前会反变换，使得每个序列的梯度被乘以b^p，导致序列尺度成为重要性权重，高尺度序列主导训练。现有TSFM对训练损失的计算方式缺乏一致约定，训练目标不统一。

Method: 作者将这一问题形式化为"尺度污染训练"（ScaleCon）。证明对于尺度等变缩放器和p次齐次的残差损失（含MSE、MAE、分位数损失），在缩放后目标上计算损失可使每个mini-batch梯度乃至整个优化轨迹对序列的任意独立重缩放置换不变，从而得到"尺度不变训练"（ScaleIn）。并在合成与真实数据上做受控实验，在四种TSFM架构上预训练验证。

Result: 在四种TSFM架构的24个架构-基准对比中，ScaleIn全部降低MASE，GIFT-Eval平均降低18.8%，M竞赛平均降低21.9%。在有监督神经预测中，20个匹配设置中有16个降低MASE。大多数现有时间序列预测流程仅需一行代码即可采用ScaleIn。

Conclusion: 反变换计算损失引入的尺度污染会扭曲训练梯度与优化轨迹。改用缩放后目标计算损失可实现尺度不变训练，显著提升预测精度且实现成本极低，建议作为通用训练约定。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are trained on large collections of time series datasets that span various morphologies and domains. This setting exposes models to series whose scales -- typical magnitudes of their values -- can differ substantially. Affine scaling methods such as Reversible Instance Normalization (ReVIN) scale model inputs and reverse the transform before computing the loss. We show that this inversion multiplies each series' gradient by $b^p$ relative to loss on scaled targets, where $b$ is the scaling denominator (e.g., standard deviation) and $p$ is the loss degree. We call this scale-contaminated training (ScaleCon), because the scale of each series consequently becomes an importance weight, causing high-scale series to dominate training. For any scale-equivariant scaler and residual loss that is homogeneous of degree $p$, including MSE, MAE, and Quantile Loss, we prove that computing loss on scaled targets makes every mini-batch gradient and, consequently, the full optimization trajectory invariant to arbitrary independent rescaling of the training series, yielding scale-invariant training (ScaleIn). Notably, existing TSFMs use both objectives, with neither consistent reporting nor a common convention on how to compute training loss. We isolate the convergence disparity induced by ScaleCon and its correction under ScaleIn in controlled studies on synthetic and real data. In pretraining across four TSFM architectures, ScaleIn lowers MASE in all 24 architecture-benchmark comparisons, with average reductions across TSFMs of 18.8% on GIFT-Eval and 21.9% on the M-competitions. The gains extend to supervised neural forecasting, where it lowers MASE in 16 of 20 matched settings. Most existing time series forecasting pipelines can adopt ScaleIn with a one-line code change.

</details>


### [62] [Structuring MoE Expert Selection for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07332)
*Bolian Li,Ting-Yao Hu,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Oncel Tuzel,Raviteja Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体后训练与MoE专家选择之间的关系，发现专家路由天然与智能体操作轨迹对齐，并提出分层路由控制框架以提升任务性能和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体常用稀疏MoE模型实现，但智能体行为与MoE结构的协同设计尚未被充分探索。标准RL算法忽略专家选择的专业化结构，导致路由在训练中失控，限制任务性能与推理效率。

Method: 提出分层路由控制框架：在轮次层面鼓励专家选择与智能体操作（如READ、UPDATE）对齐，在token层面正则化以保持局部一致性；并引入熵门控控制机制以解决后训练中的稳定性问题。

Result: 在所有评估基准上，路由控制框架将成功率提升超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 智能体轨迹结构为RL后训练中优化MoE容量提供了有效信号。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are frequently implemented using sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, yet the co-design of agentic behavior and MoE structures remains underexplored. In this work, we comprehensively study the connections between agentic post-training and MoE expert selection. In off-the-shelf MoE models, we observe expert selection exhibits a specialized structure that naturally aligns with agentic trajectories. Specifically, expert routing overlaps more between turns where the agent performs semantically similar operations (e.g., READ, UPDATE) than between turns with differing operations. However, standard RL algorithms ignore this specialization, allowing the MoE routing to go uncontrolled during training, which empirically limit task performance and inference efficiency. To address this, we introduce a hierarchical routing control framework for agentic tasks. We explicitly encourage turn-level expert selections to align with agentic operations while regularizing token-level expert selections to maintain local consistency. To resolve stability issues that arise during post-training with the proposed methods, we further introduce an entropy-gated control mechanism. Overall, our routing control framework achieves over 10-point improvements in success rate on all evaluated benchmarks. These results demonstrate that agentic trajectory structure provides an effective signal for optimizing MoE capacity during RL post-training.

</details>


### [63] [Weight Oracles: Reading Neural Network Weights with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07334)
*Krishna Kabra,Constantin Venhoff,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"权重神谕"(Weight Oracles)：微调语言模型直接读取目标网络原始权重来诊断其属性，无需行为测试。Phase I 证明可行性（模拟小Transformer前向传播，未见目标上99%准确率）；Phase II 用于安全审计，零样本检测后门，AUROC 0.93(注意力路由后门)、0.81(多样化威胁)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络可解释性方法多为反应式：需已知输入并针对特定前向传播的激活进行分析，才能发现后门等隐藏能力。作者希望实现一种主动式范式——直接读取权重即可诊断网络属性，摆脱行为测试的依赖。

Method: 提出权重神谕：微调后的语言模型直接读取目标网络原始权重。Phase I 通过分阶段课程学习和外部"计算链"(将无参数操作委派给确定性代码)训练解释器LLM模拟小Transformer的前向传播。Phase II 复用该基础设施，仅用良性病理作为训练信号，训练神谕回答关于权重异常的自然语言诊断问题，并在训练中未出现的后门上做零样本评估。

Result: Phase I 在未见目标上达到99%留出准确率。Phase II 神谕在注意力路由后门上AUROC 0.93，在包含隐蔽与对抗正则化变体的多样化威胁分布上达0.81。手工统计检测器在其隐含针对的威胁模型上表现锐利，但威胁模型偏移时崩溃，神谕则在各攻击类型上表现一致。

Conclusion: 直接读取权重进行诊断的范式可行且具安全审计价值，比手工统计检测器更具对威胁模型偏移的鲁棒性。扩展至现实模型规模仍是主要未解挑战。

Abstract: Interpretability methods for neural networks are predominantly reactive: they analyse activations produced during specific forward passes, requiring known inputs to find hidden capabilities such as backdoors. We propose Weight Oracles, fine-tuned language models that diagnose properties of a target network by reading its raw weights directly, without behavioural testing. We investigate this paradigm in two phases. Phase I establishes feasibility: through a staged curriculum and an external chain-of-computation that delegates parameter-free operations to deterministic code, an explainer LLM learns to simulate the forward pass of small transformers from their weights, achieving 99% holdout accuracy on unseen targets. Phase II repurposes this infrastructure for safety auditing. We train an oracle on natural language diagnostic questions about weight anomalies using only benign pathologies as training signal, and evaluate it zero-shot on backdoors absent from training. The oracle achieves AUROC 0.93 on attention-routed backdoors and 0.81 across a diversified threat distribution including stealth and adversarially regularized variants. Hand-crafted statistical detectors are sharp on the threat models they implicitly target but collapse on threat-model shift, while the oracle remains uniformly competent across attack types. Scaling to realistic model sizes remains the principal open challenge.

</details>


### [64] [Selective Critique for Cost-Aware LLM Agents in Long-Horizon Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07335)
*Heewon Park,Somin Im,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAG是一个成本感知的框架，将外部批评的调用建模为长程交互中的逐步决策问题，通过轻量级免训练门控机制选择性地仅在收益超过成本时调用批评，从而在显著提升任务成功率的同时保持低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在长程决策中可靠性不足：早期错误会在轨迹中传播并导致级联失败。现有方法通过引入外部批评或审议机制来提升可靠性，但每一步都调用这些机制会大幅增加token消耗和延迟，限制了实际部署。因此需要一种能按需分配昂贵反馈、平衡性能与成本的方案。

Method: 提出SAG框架，将批评调用形式化为长程交互中的逐步决策问题。其核心是一个轻量级、免训练的门控机制，利用动作层面的歧义信号（全局熵和局部top-2间距）在可行动作集上估计批评的效用。从决策理论角度，该机制近似了批评的"信息价值"（VoI），使智能体仅在预期收益超过成本时调用反馈。此外引入在线自举自我改进，让执行者内化批评辅助行为，逐步减少对批评的依赖。

Result: 在三个长程交互基准和多个骨干模型上，SAG相比"无批评"和"始终开启批评"的智能体显著改善了性能-成本权衡。在ALFWorld上，任务成功率从24.6%提升到78.4%，同时token预算与ReAct相当，归一化token效率提升3.1倍。此外，7B执行者搭配轻量级3B批评者即可达到无批评14B执行者的性能水平。

Conclusion: 选择性批评可以在大幅降低推理成本的同时，恢复审议机制的大部分可靠性收益，证明了按需、成本感知的批评调用策略在长程智能体决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Improving the reliability of large language model (LLM) agents in long-horizon decision-making remains a key challenge. When deployed as autonomous agents interacting with complex environments, early mistakes can propagate through trajectories and cause cascading failures. Recent approaches improve reliability by incorporating external critique or deliberation, but invoking these mechanisms at every step substantially increases token consumption and latency, limiting practical deployment. We propose SAG (Self-improving Agent with Gated critique), a cost-aware framework that formulates critique invocation as a step-wise decision problem during long-horizon interaction. SAG introduces a lightweight, training-free gating mechanism that estimates the utility of critique using action-level ambiguity signals--global entropy and local top-2 margin--computed over admissible actions. From a decision-theoretic perspective, this mechanism approximates the Value of Information (VoI) of critique, enabling the agent to selectively allocate expensive feedback only when its expected benefit justifies the cost. SAG further incorporates online bootstrapped self-improvement, allowing the actor to internalize critic-assisted behaviors and progressively reduce reliance on critique. Across three long-horizon interactive benchmarks and multiple backbone models, SAG substantially improves the performance-cost trade-off compared with both no-critique and always-on critique agents. On ALFWorld, SAG increases task success from 24.6% to 78.4% while maintaining a token budget comparable to ReAct, yielding a $3.1\times$ improvement in normalized token efficiency. Moreover, a 7B actor with a lightweight 3B critic achieves performance comparable to a 14B actor without critique, showing that selective critique can recover most of the reliability benefits of deliberation while dramatically reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [65] [CausalBind: Causal Modeling and Learning for Protein-Molecule Virtual Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07340)
*Loka Li,Jin Tian,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalBind，通过V-structure因果模型与Heckman选择建模，利用稀疏交互模式实现蛋白质-分子虚拟筛选的组件级可识别性与跨靶点泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质-分子虚拟筛选方法依赖稠密整体对齐，将不变的结合决定因素与冗余相关混淆，限制了对新靶点的迁移。而真实结合由少量接触界面与关键局部相互作用（氢键、疏水接触、盐桥）主导，属于稀疏跨模态交互。

Method: 作者假设挖掘稀疏交互模式对泛化至关重要。由于训练数据仅含观测结合对，采用Heckman式选择下的V-structure因果模型形式化该先验，并给出三个理论结果：(i) 无适度稀疏约束时潜在概念不可识别；(ii) 结构稀疏条件下概念及其稀疏交互组件级可识别；(iii) 低秩松弛给子空间可识别性。据此提出CausalBind及三个实现变体。

Result: 在DUD-E与LIT-PCBA基准上，所有变体一致优于强检索基线，在LIT-PCBA早期富集上增益最大，并进一步泛化到靶点级与骨架级分布外划分。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 稀疏交互模式的显式建模与可识别性理论为虚拟筛选提供了可泛化的表示学习框架，验证了稀疏交互先验在跨靶点与跨骨架场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Protein-molecule virtual screening is increasingly cast as a problem of representation learning in a shared embedding space. Existing methods rely on dense holistic alignment, entangling invariant binding determinants with nuisance correlations and limiting transfer to new targets. It has been noted that binding in protein-molecule systems involves sparse cross-modality interactions: binding is governed by a small contact interface and a few decisive local interactions (e.g., hydrogen bonds, hydrophobic contacts, and salt bridges) rather than the global structures of the protein and molecule. We hypothesize that uncovering and leveraging sparse interaction patterns is critical for generalization beyond the training data, as these patterns are reusable and expected to improve performance across different scenarios. In this paper, we aim to identify and leverage sparse interaction patterns, and verify our hypothesis. Since the training data contain only observed binding pairs, we formalize this prior via a V-structure causal model under Heckman-style selection, and establish three theoretical results: (i) the latent concepts of interacting proteins and molecules are not identifiable without appropriate sparsity constraints; (ii) these concepts and their sparse interactions are component-wise identifiable under structural sparsity conditions; and (iii) a low-rank relaxation of these conditions yields subspace identifiability of the concepts and interactions. Inspired by these principles, we propose CausalBind with three implementation variants. Extensive experiments on DUD-E and LIT-PCBA benchmarks show that all variants consistently outperform strong retrieval baselines, with the largest gains on LIT-PCBA early enrichment, and further generalize to target- and scaffold-level out-of-distribution splits. Code is available at https://github.com/lokali/CausalBind.

</details>


### [66] [Stepped MoE: Segment-Level Routing with Configurable Inference Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07348)
*Arnav Kundu,Zhaoyang Xu,Bairu Hou,Chang Gao,Reed Li,Tao Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，将弹性结构与稀疏门控架构结合，使单一模型可同时适配部署约束与任务需求，实现1-4B参数弹性跨度、更高精度且延迟与稠密模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练资源消耗大，且需适配计算约束各异的部署场景（尤其边缘设备的内存与算力限制）。现有弹性架构与稀疏激活模型各自独立处理这些维度，缺乏统一方案。

Method: 构建以上下文和目标效率规格为条件的主干网络，在弹性嵌套子网络内学习激活任务相关参数，使单一模型覆盖多个容量点，同时保持输入自适应路由，从而在推理时细粒度控制精度-效率权衡。

Result: 模型可在1、2、3、4B参数间灵活切换，在知识密集型基准上比稠密对应模型高2-5%，与静态版本持平，延迟与稠密模型相似。

Conclusion: 通过共享模型参数节省设备磁盘空间，支持依据DRAM与可用算力灵活服务，同时给出更准确的结​​果。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) is resource-intensive, and adapting them for diverse deployment scenarios with varying computational constraints remains challenging. While elastic architectures enable flexible model deployment and sparsely activated models allow input-adaptive computation, existing approaches treat these dimensions independently. Moreover, models catered towards on-device edge inference need to conform to the memory and compute limitations of the serving devices. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework that combines elastic structures with sparsely gated architectures to create models that adapt simultaneously to both deployment constraints and task requirements. Our approach employs a model backbone that conditions on both the context and target efficiency specifications, enabling fine-grained control over the accuracy-efficiency trade-off at inference time. The model learns to activate task-relevant parameters within elastically-nested sub-networks, allowing a single model to span multiple capacity points while maintaining input-adaptive routing. Through experiments we demonstrate that we can create a model that allows the flexibility to use 1,2,3,4 billion parameters while being more accurate than their dense counter-parts (2-5\% on knowledge-intensive benchmarks) and at par with their static versions while delivering similar latency metrics as dense models. Overall, we save on device disk space by sharing the model parameters, allow flexibility of serving based on DRAM and compute available while delivering more accurate results.

</details>


### [67] [RELACE: retrospective likelihood-based action credit estimation for long-horizon language agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07349)
*Sayak Chakrabarti,Sathish Reddy Indurthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RELACE 是一个无需 critic 的框架，通过回顾性动作评估与状态条件优势估计相结合，为多轮智能体提供细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 避免了独立 critic，但多轮场景下轨迹级监督信用粗粒度且噪声大：终端奖励无法定位错误，会同时惩罚有用动作与错误动作。GiGPO 等后续方法虽通过状态条件比较细化监督，但其信用估计仍对下游决策和结果敏感。

Method: RELACE 在原始上下文与结果增强上下文下，用 teacher-forced 似然打分评估已执行动作，比较二者似然得到轨迹归一化的回顾性因子，捕捉动作合理性的结果依赖变化；用该因子对折扣任务回报重加权，并通过比较同一任务中等价状态的动作加权回报构建局部优势，将回顾相关性同观测奖励耦合；再用时间平滑与成功保护掩码稳定局部信号。无需辅助价值模型、奖励模型或额外自回归 rollout。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上，使用 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 取得显著提升。1.5B 模型下 ALFWorld 成功率 96.35%、WebShop 79.43%，分别超过 GiGPO 5.47 和 5.60 个百分点。

Conclusion: RELACE 将回顾性相关性同观测奖励耦合，产生细粒度信用，补充轨迹级 GRPO 监督，在多个基准上显著超越 GRPO、GiGPO 与 HCAPO。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) avoids a separate critic by estimating advantages from rollout groups. For multi-turn agents, however, trajectory-level supervision provides coarse, noisy credit: terminal rewards do not locate errors and can penalize useful actions alongside mistakes. Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO) and subsequent methods refine supervision through state-conditioned comparisons, but their credit estimates remain sensitive to downstream decisions and outcomes. We introduce RELACE, Retrospective Likelihood-based Action, a critic-free framework that integrates retrospective action assessment with state-conditioned advantage estimation. RELACE evaluates executed actions through teacher-forced likelihood scoring under both their original contexts and outcome-augmented contexts. Comparing these likelihoods yields a trajectory-normalized retrospective factor that captures outcome-dependent changes in action plausibility, rather than hindsight plausibility alone. We use this factor to reweight discounted task returns and construct local advantages by comparing weighted returns among actions from equivalent states within a task. This couples retrospective relevance with observed reward, producing fine-grained credit that complements trajectory-level GRPO supervision. Temporal smoothing and success-protecting masking further stabilize the local signal. RELACE requires neither auxiliary value nor reward models nor additional autoregressive rollouts for credit estimation. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct demonstrate substantial improvements over GRPO, GiGPO, and HCAPO. With the 1.5B model, RELACE achieves $96.35\%$ success on ALFWorld and $79.43\%$ on WebShop, surpassing GiGPO by $5.47$ and $5.60$ percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Explainable Benchmarking for Data-driven Post-Wildfire Debris Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07358)
*Zhisheng Qi,Li Zhu,Utkarsh Sahu,Douglas Tommey,Josh Roering,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的数据驱动野火后泥石流（PFDF）预测基准，并提出基于强化学习的特征选择框架以识别关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PFDF数据驱动预测研究在特征空间、模型架构和评估协议上高度碎片化，难以进行严格比较与科学洞察；同时缺乏对气象、地形、土壤、火烧严重度等异质因素相对重要性的系统研究。

Method: 1）构建统一的PFDF预测基准，支持跨多种模型与特征配置的公平全面评估；2）提出基于强化学习的特征选择框架，通过识别使正负样本变得不可区分的扰动因素，揭示PFDF发生的区域性内在机制。

Result: 实现跨模型、跨特征配置的公平全面评估；通过强化学习特征选择发现不同区域PFDF发生的关键驱动因素与区域机制；代码与基准已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作为数据驱动PFDF预测提供统一评估标准，并通过强化学习特征选择深化了对PFDF形成驱动机制的理解，代码与基准开源。

Abstract: Post-wildfire debris flows (PFDFs) are destructive sediment-laden hazards triggered when intense rainfall strikes recently burned terrain, destabilizing hillslopes and threatening infrastructure, local economies, and community safety. Data-driven methods have been proposed to learn predictive patterns directly from historical PFDF observations. However, the current research landscape of data-driven PFDF prediction remains highly fragmented across feature spaces, model architectures, and evaluation protocols, making rigorous comparison and the derivation of scientific insights difficult. Moreover, existing studies lack a systematic investigation into the relative importance of heterogeneous factors (e.g., meteorological conditions, terrain characteristics, soil properties, and burn severity) in triggering PFDF. To address these limitations, we present a unified benchmark for data-driven PFDF prediction, enabling fair and comprehensive evaluation across diverse models and feature configurations. Furthermore, to better understand the underlying drivers of PFDF formation, we propose a reinforcement learning-based feature selection framework that identifies factors whose perturbations render positive and negative events indistinguishable, thereby discovering the regional underlying mechanisms of PFDF occurrence across regions. Our code and benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/KINDLab-Fly/PFDF-Benchmark.

</details>


### [69] [Dynamic Budget Allocation for LLM Evaluation under Hard Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07362)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARP，一种满足硬预算约束并自适应重分配未用预算的分配方法，用于多轮LLM交互的时间-事件校准，构建下预测界并估计越狱率等指标，具有有限样本覆盖保证且估计无偏，实验证明其覆盖接近名义水平且从不超预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM交互中，成功越狱或任务完成等事件的发生时间常因算力有限而部分观测（删失）。现有分配方法仅在期望上满足预算，单次评估可能超支，而轨迹成本事先未知，硬约束难以实现。

Method: 提出HARP（Hard-budget Allocation with Reflow for Predictive calibration）：一种满足硬资源约束并自适应重分配未使用预算的分配方法。用于构建时间-事件的下预测界（LPB），并估计固定基准上的越狱率等评估指标，同时处理其引入的获取决策间依赖。

Result: 理论上证明HARP从不超目标预算，其LPB具有有限样本覆盖保证，指标估计无偏。在智能体任务成功、LLM越狱、有害内容生成和RAG幻觉实验中，HARP覆盖接近名义水平且方差低，同时从不超预算。

Conclusion: HARP为多轮LLM评估提供了满足硬预算约束的时间-事件校准框架，兼具覆盖保证与无偏估计，适用于多种评估场景。

Abstract: We evaluate large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn interactions through their time-to-event: the number of interaction steps required to produce an event of interest, such as a successful jailbreak or agentic task completion. Under limited compute, interactions may be terminated before the event occurs, so that event times are only partially observed (censored). Existing allocation methods for calibrating time-to-event bounds satisfy the budget only in expectation and can exceed the available budget on a particular evaluation run.
