Enforcing a hard constraint is particularly challenging as the cost of a trajectory is initially unknown. We introduce Hard-budget Allocation with Reflow for Predictive calibration (HARP), a budget allocation that satisfies hard resource constraints and adaptively reallocates unused budget. We show how to use HARP to construct lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the time-to-event and to estimate evaluation metrics such as the jailbreak rate on a fixed benchmark. Although HARP induces dependence in acquisition decisions across different trajectories, we prove that HARP never exceeds the target budget, that its LPBs have finite-sample coverage guarantees, and that its metric estimates are unbiased. Experiments on agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations show that HARP achieves coverage close to the nominal level with low variance, while never exceeding the given budget.

</details>


### [70] [Multigroup Fairness and Omniprediction: Separations and Equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07374)
*Sílvia Casacuberta,Parikshit Gopalan,Varun Kanade,Omer Reingold,Konstantinos Stavropoulos,Pranay Tankala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨"全预测（omniprediction）"是否必须依赖多群体公平性（如多准确度、多校准）。结论：普通全预测不需要多群体公平性，而损失结果不可区分性（loss OI）等价于一种校准多准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数已知的全预测学习算法都依赖多群体公平性概念或其变体（如校准多准确度）。作者追问：这种依赖是本质性的吗？即全预测是否必然要求某种形式的多群体公平性？

Method: 通过理论证明建立两个方向的结果：(1) 证否方向——构造证明以说明普通全预测（针对恰当损失的）不蕴含期望精度，从而排除任何弱化的逆命题；(2) 证是方向——证明 loss OI 与某种校准多准确度等价。

Result: 第一，即便只要求"恰当损失"下的全预测，也不蕴含比校准、多准确度、多校准弱得多的"期望精度"，故多群体公平性并非全预测的必要条件。第二，loss OI 与一种形式的校准多准确度互相等价，即此前由校准与多准确度组合得到 loss OI 的构造具有逆命题。

Conclusion: 普通全预测不要求多群体公平性；但对更强的 loss OI 而言，多群体公平性（具体为校准多准确度）是必要且充分的刻画。这厘清了全预测与多群体公平性之间的本质关系。

Abstract: Omniprediction is a learning guarantee which requires a single predictor to be competitive relative to the best hypothesis from a benchmark class for any loss chosen from a family of loss functions. Loss Outcome Indistinguishability (loss OI for short) is a stronger notion that implies omniprediction. It requires the predicted distribution on labels to be indistinguishable from the true distribution to tests that depend on the loss functions and the benchmark class. Multiaccuracy and multicalibration are multigroup fairness notions that generalize classical notions of calibration and accuracy in expectation. Most known learning algorithms for omniprediction (both for the standard notion and for strengthenings like loss OI) rely on some version of these multigroup fairness notions, or on an intermediate notion called calibrated multiaccuracy. We ask if this is necessary: Does omniprediction require some form of multigroup fairness?
  We show that the answer is no for (plain) omniprediction, and yes for loss OI. First, a sequence of works shows that multicalibration or calibrated multiaccuracy imply omniprediction. We rule out even a weak converse, by showing that omniprediction for proper losses does not imply even accuracy in expectation, a much weaker notion than any of calibration, multiaccuracy, or multicalibration. Second, prior work showed how to achieve loss OI from a combination of calibration and multiaccuracy. We show a converse: loss OI is equivalent to a form of calibrated multiaccuracy.

</details>


### [71] [Inference and learning in sparse autoencoders as natural gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07389)
*Hadi Vafaii,Tejas Rao,David Chanin,Thomas Fel,Jacob L. Yates,Bruno Olshausen,David Klindt,Dileep George,Miguel Lázaro-Gredilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BeFOND，一个无编码器的稀疏编码模型，将推断与字典学习统一为共享变分自由能上的自然梯度流，用闭式动态改进重叠与稀有特征的恢复，并在合成数据与语言模型激活上优于摊销基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器在神经网络可解释性中常用，但当特征重叠或激活稀疏时，可靠恢复特征仍困难。这些挑战同时涉及推断（哪些特征解释输入）与字典学习（如何表示这些特征），而现有方法通常将两者分离处理。

Method: 作者将推断和字典学习统一为共享变分自由能上的自然梯度流，并实例化为BeFOND：一个无编码器稀疏编码模型，具有闭式推断与学习动态。其机制包括循环的"explaining away"以减少重叠特征间干扰，以及Fisher预条件以补偿稀有特征学习缓慢的问题。

Result: 在合成数据上，BeFOND改善了字典恢复与稀有特征检测，且随着叠加程度增加，相对摊销基线的优势扩大。在语言模型激活上，它提升了单特征概念检测与选择性干预效果，以更少训练数据超越预训练参考SAE；其特征质量随字典宽度持续提升，而基线大多趋于平台。

Conclusion: 在统一概率框架内改进推断与学习，可更好地利用数据与字典容量，从而解释并干预神经表征。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are widely used to uncover interpretable features in neural networks, yet reliable recovery remains difficult when features overlap or activate infrequently. These challenges involve both inferring which features explain an input and learning the dictionary that represents them. Here, we unify inference and dictionary learning as natural-gradient flows on a shared variational free energy. We instantiate this framework as BeFOND, an encoder-free sparse coding model with closed-form inference and learning dynamics. We show how recurrent explaining away reduces interference between overlapping features, while Fisher preconditioning can compensate for the slow learning of rare features. On synthetic data, BeFOND improves dictionary recovery and rare-feature detection, with a growing advantage over amortized baselines as superposition increases. On language-model activations, it improves single-feature concept detection and selective intervention, outperforming pretrained reference SAEs with substantially less training data. Its feature quality continues to improve with dictionary width, whereas the evaluated baselines largely plateau. Together, these results show how improving inference and learning within a unified probabilistic framework can make better use of data and dictionary capacity to interpret and intervene on neural representations.

</details>


### [72] [Fed-BRDECS: Privacy-Preserving and Heterogeneity-Aware Federated Deep Embedded Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07399)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-BRDECS：一种隐私保护且异质性感知的联邦深度嵌入聚类框架，用局部可算的样本稳定性损失替代全局软分配目标，并结合预测均衡采样与质心重启，在IID与非IID设定下均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦深度聚类希望从去中心化无标签数据中学习聚类友好表示并保护客户端隐私，但DEC式目标依赖全局软分配统计量，需要客户端暴露敏感信息；同时客户端数据存在非IID分布问题。

Method: 用局部可计算的样本稳定性损失替换全局归一化聚类目标，避免传输本地软分配分布；引入预测均衡采样，在没有真实标签情况下对本地稀有预测簇过采样；引入质心级重启，定期刷新有偏或失活质心，以应对非IID。

Result: 在图像和文本聚类基准上，Fed-BRDECS在IID与非IID划分下均稳定优于代表性联邦聚类与深度聚类基线；在联邦时间序列异常检测任务中，也能提升基于重建的检测器且不增加推理开销。

Conclusion: Fed-BRDECS通过局部化聚类目标与异质性处理机制，在保护隐私的同时有效提升联邦深度聚类性能，并具备向时间序列异常检测等任务的迁移适用性。

Abstract: Federated deep clustering seeks to learn clustering-friendly representations from decentralized unlabeled data while preserving client privacy. However, Deep Embedded Clustering (DEC)-style objectives depend on global soft-assignment statistics that require clients to reveal their sensitive information. We propose Fed-BRDECS, a privacy-preserving and heterogeneity-aware federated deep embedded clustering framework. Fed-BRDECS replaces the globally normalized clustering objective with a locally computable sample-stability loss, avoiding the transmission of local soft-assignment distributions. To tackle non-IID client distributions, we introduce prediction-balanced sampling, which oversamples locally rare predicted clusters without requiring ground-truth labels, and centroid-level restarting, which periodically refreshes biased or inactive centroids. Experiments on image and text clustering benchmarks show that Fed-BRDECS consistently outperforms representative federated clustering and deep clustering baselines under both IID and non-IID partitions. We further demonstrate its applicability to federated time-series anomaly detection, where it improves reconstruction-based detectors without adding inference-time cost.

</details>


### [73] [What pass@k Cannot Measure: Evaluating Diversity and Capability Retention after Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07405)
*Subham Rath,Raj Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pass@k只依赖正确采样的概率，无法反映解法的多样性分布；实验证明GRPO与RFT在多种多样性指标上方向相反，但pass@8/32无法区分二者，且在难题覆盖上均未超越起始模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域默认用pass@k判断RL后训练是否提升模型，但pass@k在总体层面仅取决于"答对的概率"，不含该概率如何分布于不同输出（即解的多样性）的项，作者要检验这一理论缺口是否会带来实际的评测误导。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上用GRPO与RFT（在模型自身最短且通过验证的rollout上训练）做小学数学任务后训练，每个arm跑三个随机种子；用三个互补多样性指标（token级熵、answer级熵、每prompt唯一答案数）衡量多样性，并做"仅限验证正确解"的受控比较与"仅看错误答案"的计数控制；在GSM8K与MATH-500困难子集上比较pass@1至pass@32。

Result: GRPO与RFT使三个多样性指标朝相反方向移动且三种子间零重叠；控制长度后GRPO正确解的词汇多样性低15%；即使只看错误答案，多样性差异仍存在，说明并非GRPO准确率更高所致。但pass@8与pass@32在GSM8K上无法给出一致赢家，MATH-500困难子集同样只在低k处有分离。相对起始checkpoint，无一训练arm显著提升难题覆盖：RFT显著更差，GRPO与Base统计上不可区分。GSM8K上只有pass@1能干净地区分两个arm，而pass@1按构造本无检测多样性之功能，反而奖励了正确解多样性最低的arm。

Conclusion: pass@k是标准评测协议缺失其所宣称衡量属性（多样性）的具体实例：GRPO相对RFT的pass@1优势反映的是"相对Base损失更小"而非能力提升，属缺失对照问题而非pass@k失效；该协议会奖励正确解多样性更差的模型。

Abstract: pass@$k$, the fraction of problems a model solves within $k$ sampled attempts, is the field's default protocol for deciding whether reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training on verifiable rewards improved a model. At the population level, pass@$k$ depends only on a problem's probability of a correct sample, with no term for how it is distributed across outputs. We show this gap is not academic. Training Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on grade-school math with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and with rejection-sampling fine-tuning (RFT, training on the model's own shortest verifier-passed rollout) moves three complementary diversity measures (token-level entropy, answer-level entropy, unique answers per prompt) in opposite directions, with zero overlap across three seeds per arm. The gap survives restricting to verifier-correct completions only (lexical diversity among correct solutions is 15% lower for GRPO, after controlling for length) and a count-controlled check isolating diversity among incorrect answers alone, ruling out that GRPO's higher accuracy alone explains it. Yet pass@8 and pass@32 show no consistent winner on GSM8K, and a hard MATH-500 subset shows the same pattern: separation only at low $k$. Compared against the starting checkpoint, no trained arm significantly improves hard-problem coverage: RFT is significantly worse, while GRPO is statistically indistinguishable from it - so GRPO's pass@1 edge over RFT reflects a smaller loss relative to Base, not a capability gain, a missing-control issue, not a failure of pass@$k$. On GSM8K, only pass@1, with no role in detecting diversity by construction, separates the arms cleanly, rewarding the arm whose correct solutions are least diverse. We argue this is a concrete instance of a standard evaluation protocol missing a property it is routinely used to certify.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluation of Active Feature Acquisition Policies with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07406)
*Yuta Kobayashi,Divyam Madaan,Shalmali Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用先验数据拟合网络（PFN）从有限离线数据中学习和评估主动特征获取策略，指出离线数据覆盖率不平衡时以总预测熵为奖励会产生认知偏差，并提出以后验期望（偶然）熵替代总预测熵来评估特征获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取需要按序采集特征以最大化关于目标变量的信息。从有限离线数据中学习和评估此类策略存在困难，而现成的PFN虽无需任务特定训练即可输出后验预测分布，但在离线数据覆盖不平衡时，已有的总预测熵奖励会把认知不确定性（源于离线数据不足）与偶然不确定性（源于特征本身无信息量）混为一谈，从而系统性地惩罚采集稀疏观测特征的行为，导致价值估计偏差。

Method: 作者利用先验数据拟合网络（PFN）作为现成模型输出后验预测分布，分析总预测熵作为奖励在离线数据覆盖不平衡时的偏差来源。为解决该偏差，提出以PFN输出所对应的后验期望（偶然）熵取代总预测熵，作为特征获取的评估准则，从而将认知不确定性从奖励中剥离。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法持续降低价值估计偏差，并产生具有良好经验覆盖率（empirical coverage）的可信区间，这进一步转化为更优的下游策略选择效果。

Conclusion: 以后验期望（偶然）熵替代总预测熵来评估特征获取，可以有效缓解离线数据覆盖不平衡带来的认知偏差，提升策略价值估计的准确性与可信区间的可靠性，从而改善最终策略选择。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition learns policies that sequentially acquire features to maximize information about a target variable. We study how to learn and evaluate such policies from finite offline data using prior-data fitted networks (PFNs), which are off-the-shelf models that output posterior predictive distributions without task-specific training. We show that under the imbalanced coverage of offline data, using total predictive entropy as a reward creates an epistemic bias that penalizes acquiring sparsely observed features. Specifically, this reward conflates epistemic uncertainty (arising from lack of offline data) with aleatoric uncertainty (arising from uninformative features). To address this, we target the posterior expected (aleatoric) entropy instead of the total predictive entropy output by a PFN for evaluating feature acquisitions. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently reduces value estimation bias and yields credible intervals with strong empirical coverage, which can translate to improved downstream policy selection.

</details>


### [75] [Learnable Spectral Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07419)
*Tamir Shor,Or Litany,Alex Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可学习谱激活（LSA），用残差截断傅里叶级数替代固定神经元级非线性，通过分离线权与激活系数的梯度更新来改变表示的因式分解，在多种任务上提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示（INR）方法主要通过坐标编码或周期性非线性改变网络可用频率来提升拟合能力，但频率可达性并非唯一瓶颈：具有局部或空间变化结构的信号还要求网络高效地将频率组合成多谐波内部响应，这一方面尚未被充分解决。

Method: LSA 将固定的神经元级非线性替换为残差截断傅里叶级数，其谐波振幅在训练中学习。它并不扩展渐近函数类，而是改变表示的因式分解：线性权重负责选择特征，激活系数负责谱整形，两者由各自的梯度分别更新。由于在给定预激活时激活输出对系数是仿射的，谱调优相比与特征选择纠缠的架构成为更直接的子问题。

Result: 该因式分解将更多目标信号能量集中到神经正切核（NTK）的主导特征模中，与优化行为的改善一致；在音频、图像、神经辐射场和神经声场任务上，LSA 均提升了重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过可学习谱激活实现特征选择与谱整形的解耦，是一种不扩展函数类即可改善 INR 拟合与优化的有效途径，并在多类任务中得到验证。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) are shaped by the spectral structure induced by their input encodings and activation functions. Existing methods improve fitting primarily by modifying which frequencies are available to the network, through coordinate encodings or periodic nonlinearities. However, frequency access is not the only bottleneck: signals with localized or spatially varying structure require the network to efficiently compose frequencies into multi-harmonic internal responses. We introduce learnable spectral activations (LSA), which replace fixed neuron-level nonlinearities with a residual truncated Fourier series whose harmonic amplitudes are learned during training. LSA does not expand the asymptotic function class. Instead, it changes the factorization of the representation: linear weights select features while activation coefficients control spectral shaping, and the two are updated by separate gradients. Because the activation output is affine in the coefficients given fixed pre-activations, spectral tuning becomes a more direct subproblem compared to architectures where it is entangled with feature selection. Empirically, this factorization concentrates more target-signal energy in the leading eigenmodes of the neural tangent kernel, consistent with improved optimization behavior. Across audio, image, neural radiance field, and neural acoustic field tasks, LSA also improves reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [76] [Benchmarking Label-Revealed Online Updates for EEG BCI Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07420)
*Bogdan Kozyrskiy,Artem Grachev,Abraham I. Camelo Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向脑机接口(BCI)的在线自适应基准，在时序前序（先测后训）评估下比较CSP与黎曼协方差方法，并研究标签揭示更新、受控遗忘及冷启动策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电(EEG)信号随时间漂移，导致静态BCI模型在实际应用中的性能下降，因此需要系统评估在线自适应方法的效果与机制。

Method: 构建在线自适应基准，在时序前序评估下比较两类常用流程：共空间模式(CSP)与基于黎曼协方差的方法。考察三点：(i)哪些流程最受益于标签揭示的更新；(ii)对旧数据进行受控遗忘是否提升鲁棒性；(iii)最小校准冷启动与预训练模型起始的比较。并在四个数据集（三个运动想象数据集和一个运动解码数据集）上进行实验，采用基于Shapley的数据估值分析时间块价值。

Result: 在四个数据集上，标签揭示的在线更新在两个最大数据流中使14个模型/数据集组合中的13个获得提升，相对冻结模型的准确率增益最高约18%。Shapley数据估值分析显示，在三个被分析的数据集中，最近时间块的平均价值最大，而较早的块仍保留正向价值。

Conclusion: 在线标签揭示更新对多数BCI流程有效，最新数据价值最高但旧数据并非无用，受控遗忘与冷启动策略对鲁棒性具有潜在影响。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) signals drift over time, which can cause static brain-computer interface (BCI) models to degrade in practice. We present a benchmark for online adaptation and compare two widely used pipeline families, Common Spatial Patterns (CSP) and Riemannian covariance-based methods, under time-ordered prequential (test-then-train) evaluation. We examine (i) which pipelines benefit most from label-revealed updates, (ii) whether controlled forgetting of older data improves robustness, and (iii) how a minimal-calibration cold start compares with starting from a pretrained model. Across four datasets (three motor-imagery datasets and one movement-decoding dataset), label-revealed online updates improve 13 of 14 model/dataset pairs on the two largest streams, with relative accuracy gains of up to about 18% over a frozen model. A Shapley-based data-valuation analysis over temporal blocks assigns the largest mean value to the most recent block in each of the three analyzed datasets, while older blocks retain positive value.

</details>


### [77] [StaFIR: Convex Learning of Stationarity-Aware Causal Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07430)
*Lorena Egger,Mathis Linger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 StaFIR——一种因果有限冲激响应滤波器，用指数滞后剖面的非负混合来学习差分强度，目标在平稳性与输入保真度之间取得平衡，在金融时间序列上验证其可自适应调节滤波强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳化持久时间序列需决定去除多少时间依赖。金融中常用分数差分，但多由 ADF 检验调节，限制在单参数滞后剖面族，且仅间接处理输入保真度问题。

Method: StaFIR 为因果 FIR 滤波器，由指数滞后剖面的可学习非负混合构成；其凸学习目标同时权衡经验平稳性与与输入的相似度。

Result: 在 ARFIMA–GARCH 受控设置与滚动金融序列（含已实现波动率预测）上评测：StaFIR 能随持久性调整滤波强度，在平稳区间限制不必要变换。下游预测精度与固定半阶差分无明显差异，但对原始信号相似度更高；直接预测实验显示输入相似度越高，预测惩罚越小，而原始表示仍更强。

Conclusion: 相比基于 ADF 的分数差分，StaFIR 提供了更灵活、兼顾平稳性与输入保真的自适应差分方案；虽未显著提升预测精度，但改善了表示相似性，为平稳化与信息保留的权衡提供新思路。

Abstract: Reducing nonstationarity in a persistent time series entails deciding how much of its temporal dependence to remove. In finance, fractional differencing is often tuned using the Augmented Dickey--Fuller (ADF) test, limiting the search to a one-parameter family of lag profiles and addressing input preservation only indirectly. We propose StaFIR, a causal finite-impulse-response filter with a learned nonnegative mixture of exponential lag profiles. Its convex learning objective balances empirical stationarity with similarity to the input. We evaluate StaFIR on ARFIMA--GARCH controlled settings and rolling financial series, including a realized-volatility forecasting task. The experiments show that StaFIR adjusts its filtering strength to persistence while limiting unnecessary transformation in stationary regimes. In downstream forecasting, there is no clear accuracy difference from fixed half-order differencing, while StaFIR achieves higher measured similarity to the raw signal. A complementary direct forecasting experiment finds that greater input similarity is associated with smaller forecasting penalties, although the raw representation remains stronger.

</details>


### [78] [Decoupling What from Where: How Should a Small GUI Grounding Model Receive the Action Type?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07444)
*Aadi Chauhan,Arthur Ilyasov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以Qwen2-VL-2B配合LoRA在AITW数据集上微调，系统比较了六种向小型GUI定位模型注入动作类型的方式。结果显示辅助损失、加性嵌入与提示词三种方法在混合流上提升5–7个hit@0.10点，但大部分增益源于对预处理缺陷的修复而非空间先验；去除问题类别后基线即追平。部署层面预测类型的优势尚未确立，错误类型会导致所有模型崩溃，并揭示inputs_embeds注入引发的1-D位置静默失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体需同时决定动作类型与执行位置，而小型定位模型如何接收动作类型信息尚不明确。作者希望在数据、算力与解码条件一致的前提下，公平比较多种类型条件注入机制，并检验其增益究竟来自真正的空间先验还是实验预处理带来的假象。

Method: 在Android in the Wild上以LoRA微调Qwen2-VL-2B，对比平铺基线与五种类型注入方式：辅助损失、硬路由动作词、加性学习嵌入、前置学习token、写入提示词。采用五种子、按episode聚类的bootstrap及种子级配对检验。另对序列化器将离屏触点钳制到原点的问题做消融，并检验预测类型部署与inputs_embeds注入的影响。

Result: 混合流上辅助损失、加性嵌入与提示词各比基线高5–7个hit@0.10点，硬路由与前置token与基线无显著差异。移除被钳制的类型事件后基线提升近7点，此后无任何机制命中率优于基线（区间排除2点效应），仅辅助损失缩短平均miss；在点击与滑动流上均无帮助。预测类型下流水线相对基线的裕度未确立（+0.016，95%区间[-0.017,+0.052]），错误类型使所有条件模型崩溃。前置token在共享学习率下几乎不动，提速十倍训练才能达到其他三种水平。另发现经inputs_embeds注入会使图像token退回1-D位置，损失9个点。

Conclusion: 动作类型注入的多数增益实为对特定序列化预处理缺陷的补偿，而非内在的空间先验；去除该缺陷后各机制优势消失，结论能否推广到其他序列化方式仍待验证。部署时预测类型收益未确立且对类型错误极度敏感，提示需谨慎评估条件注入路径与位置编码副作用。

Abstract: A GUI agent decides which action to take and where to take it; we ask how a small grounding model should receive the action type. Fine-tuning Qwen2-VL-2B with LoRA on Android in the Wild, we compare a flat baseline with five ways of supplying the type under matched data, compute, and decoding: an auxiliary loss, a hard-routed action word, an additive learned embedding, a prepended learned token, and the type written into the prompt. With five seeds, an episode-clustered bootstrap, and seed-level paired tests, the ranking on a mixed stream is clear: the auxiliary loss, the additive embedding, and the prompt word each gain five to seven hit@0.10 points over the baseline, while hard routing and the prepended token are not distinguishable from it. Much of that gain is protection from a preprocessing choice of ours rather than a spatial prior. Our serializer clamps the off-screen touch point AITW records for type events to the origin; that class degrades the baseline's click grounding, and removing it lifts the baseline by nearly seven points, after which no mechanism's hit rate beats it and the intervals exclude a two-point effect, though the auxiliary loss still shortens the average miss; on a stream of taps and swipes none helps. Whether this generalizes beyond one serialization is open. For deployment, the pipeline's margin over the baseline with predicted rather than gold types is not established (+0.016, 95% interval [-0.017, +0.052]), and a wrong type collapses every model conditioned at inference. The prepended token does not help at the shared learning rate, where its rows barely move from initialization; trained ten times faster it reaches the level of the other three, with a margin three seeds do not establish. We also document a silent failure: injecting conditioning through inputs_embeds makes Qwen2-VL fall back to 1-D positions for image tokens, costing nine points.

</details>


### [79] [Fork-and-Flush: Escaping Idea Basins in Autoresearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07447)
*Ziyang Cai,Christos Ziakas,Vasilis Kontonis,Tim Pearce,Siddhartha Sen,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Shivam Garg,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现自动研究智能体的独立运行常在不同分数上停滞，且其轨迹局限于解空间的"想法盆地"中；作者提出 fork-and-flush 干预方法，通过并行分叉并重置对话上下文来帮助智能体跳出这些盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动研究智能体通过反复提出、评估候选方案并利用反馈来迭代解决开放式问题。但作者观察到，即便在相同任务上运行同一个智能体，不同独立运行的结果往往差异巨大：它们会停滞在明显不同的分数上，而且即使投入大量额外计算资源，这种差距也难以消除。这一现象表明智能体陷入了某种局部解区域，限制了其探索能力和最终表现。

Method: 作者首先通过将候选产物按功能相似度进行嵌入，验证了轨迹确实长期停留在解空间的局部区域（称为"想法盆地"）。为帮助智能体逃离这些盆地，提出一种简单的周期性干预方法 fork-and-flush：将智能体分叉为多条并行轨迹，每条轨迹继承累积的工作区，但使用全新的对话上下文。每条轨迹运行固定时间跨度后，智能体从得分最高的那条轨迹继续。

Result: 在13个长周期研究和工程任务上（单次智能体运行可持续数天），在相同计算预算下，fork-and-flush 相比单次运行基线和 best-of-N 基线，在最小-最大归一化平均分数上分别取得了 66.0% 和 44.4% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 独立运行会因陷入想法盆地而停滞于不同分数，且额外计算难以弥合差距；fork-and-flush 通过并行分叉加上下文刷新有效帮助智能体跳出局部区域，在多个长周期任务上显著优于单次运行和 best-of-N 基线。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents tackle open-ended problems by repeatedly proposing candidate solutions, evaluating them, and using feedback to guide subsequent experiments. We show that independent runs of the same agent on the same task often plateau at substantially different scores, with gaps that persist even after considerable additional compute. Embedding their candidate artifacts by functional similarity provides further evidence that trajectories remain in localized regions of the solution space, which we call idea basins. To help agents escape these basins, we study a simple periodic intervention, fork-and-flush. Our method forks the agent into parallel trajectories, each inheriting the accumulated workspace but starting with a fresh chat context. After running each trajectory for a fixed horizon, the agent continues from the highest-scoring one. Across 13 long-horizon research and engineering tasks, with individual agent runs lasting up to several days, fork-and-flush outperformed the single-run and best-of-N baselines by a relative improvement of 66.0% and 44.4%, respectively, on the min-max normalized average score under an equal compute budget.

</details>


### [80] [Active Feature Acquisition for Cost-Efficient Temporal Prediction with Reduced Participant Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07452)
*Yunni Qu,Bing Cai Kok,Whitney Ringwald,Grant King,Aidan Wright,Kathleen Gates,Junier Oliva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种树蒸馏方法，从基于神经网络的纵向主动特征获取（LAFA）网络中学习可解释的策略，从而在保证预测精度的同时减少每次采集的题目数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学中准确预测病理结果需要密集纵向数据，但采集大量变量会增加参与者负担，导致无应答与流失。需在预测精度与采集负担之间取得平衡，而现有LAFA方法多基于难以解释的神经网络。

Method: 提出树蒸馏方法，从基于NN的LAFA网络中学习可解释的策略。该策略在每个时间点动态选择最优的条目子集进行采集，同时保持对特定结果的预测能力。通过模拟实验和一个关于预测每日酒精消费的经验EMA数据集进行验证。

Result: 在模拟和实证EMA数据集中，该方法均能显著减少每次采集的条目数量，且预测精度的损失极小。

Conclusion: 树蒸馏可为NN-based LAFA提供可解释策略，在几乎不损失精度的前提下有效降低采集负担，为心理学密集纵向研究提供实用方案。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of pathological outcomes is a central problem in psychology. To do so, psychologists often collect intensive longitudinal data. However, in such studies, the desire to acquire a large number of variables for the sake of accurate prediction is often counteracted by the need to minimize participant burden. Acquiring more variables per occasion can yield better predictions, but having too many acquisitions increase the risk of non-response and attrition. Longitudinal Active Feature Acquisition (LAFA) is a principled approach to resolve this conundrum. Instead of requiring responses to every item at every acquisition occasion, LAFA produces a policy that seeks to optimally select dynamic subsets of items to be acquired at each timepoint while preserving our ability to forecast a specific outcome. However, existing LAFA methods are mostly based on Neural Networks (NN) that are difficult to interpret in practice. In this work, we introduce a tree distillation method for learning an interpretable policy from NN-based LAFA networks. We validated our method through both a simulation and an empirical EMA dataset on forecasting daily alcohol consumption. In both cases, we find that we can meaningfully reduce the number of items acquired at each occasion with minimal loss in accuracy. Networks (NN) that are difficult to interpret in practice. In this work, we introduce a tree distillation method for learning an interpretable policy from NN-based LAFA networks. We validated our method through both a simulation and an empirical EMA dataset on forecasting daily alcohol consumption. In both cases, we find that we can meaningfully reduce the number of items acquired at each occasion with minimal loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [AlignQuant: Tile-Aligned Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07457)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlignQuant 提出一种后训练量化方法，以 GPU 兼容的二维权重 tile 作为精度分配、紧凑存储与执行的统一单元，在保持模型质量的同时实现最高 2.50 倍的生成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度混合精度量化虽能提升大语言模型推理效率，但局部精度选择与 GPU 规则化的存储和计算单元存在冲突，这种“精度边界不匹配”阻碍了压缩转化为实际加速。因此需要一种让精度灵活性与 GPU 常规执行兼容的方案。

Method: 以 GPU 兼容的二维权重 tile 作为精度分配、存储和执行的共同单元，使精度可在输出通道内跟随敏感度分布；采用联合 prefill/decode 校准，用量化激活下的投影输出扰动并结合语言模型损失梯度为精度削减打分；通过相位归一化分数，在全局权重存储预算下为任一阶段重要的 tile 优先分配更高精度；每个 tile 仅存储一种选定的表示形式，配合阶段专用 kernel 复用打包模型，将低位权重展开为 INT8 计算并使用 8 位激活。

Result: 在 4 个参数规模从 3B 到 14B 的 LLM 上，AlignQuant 相较 BF16 实现最高 2.50 倍的生成加速并保持模型质量；评估还覆盖 3 种 GPU 与最长 64K token 的上下文。

Conclusion: 实验表明，通过共享的 tile 单元，局部精度灵活性与规则的 GPU 执行可以共存。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-grained mixed-precision quantization promises efficient large language model inference, but local precision choices can conflict with regular GPU storage and computation units. This precision-boundary mismatch limits the translation of compression into practical acceleration. We introduce AlignQuant, a post-training quantization method that uses GPU-compatible two-dimensional weight tiles as the common unit of precision allocation, compact storage, and execution. This shared partition lets precision follow sensitivity within output channels. Joint prefill/decode calibration scores precision reductions using projection-output perturbations weighted by language-model loss gradients under quantized activations. Phase-normalized scores prioritize higher precision for tiles important to either phase under a model-wide weight-storage budget. Each tile stores one selected representation, while phase-specialized kernels reuse the packed model and expand lower-bit weights for INT8 computation with 8-bit activations. Across four LLMs spanning 3B to 14B parameters, AlignQuant achieves up to $2.50\times$ generation speedup over BF16 while preserving model quality. Evaluations further cover three GPUs and contexts up to 64K tokens. These results show that local precision flexibility and regular GPU execution can coexist through a shared tile unit. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/AlignQuant.

</details>


### [82] [Interpretable Hypergraph Learning via Neural Additive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07458)
*Shihan Feng,Xin Zheng,Shiyi Yang,Ren Wang,Chudi Zhong,Can Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出超图神经加性网络（HGNAN），将经典神经加性模型扩展到高阶关系数据，通过特征级非线性分解与超图结构聚合实现可解释学习，在大规模实验中性能与SOTA相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图学习方法（如超图神经网络）多为黑箱消息传递架构，难以区分节点属性与高阶结构信息各自的贡献，缺乏可解释性。

Method: HGNAN将经典神经加性模型扩展到高阶关系数据，融合特征维度的非线性分解与超图感知的结构聚合，支持节点级和超边级任务的透明预测。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HGNAN取得了与当前最优超图学习方法相当的性能，同时提供内在且有意义的可解释性。

Conclusion: HGNAN为超图结构数据提供了一种兼具竞争性性能与内在可解释性的学习框架。

Abstract: Hypergraphs offer a natural framework for modeling networked data, where dependencies among entities are governed by higher-order interactions. While hypergraph learning methods such as hypergraph neural networks have demonstrated remarkable predictive performance, most existing approaches rely on black-box message-passing architectures, making it difficult to disentangle the contributions of node attributes and higher-order structural information. To address this challenge, we introduce the hypergraph neural additive network (HGNAN), an inherently interpretable framework for learning on hypergraph-structured data. HGNAN extends classical neural additive models to higher-order relational data by integrating feature-wise nonlinear decomposition with hypergraph-aware structural aggregation, enabling transparent prediction for both node- and hyperedge-level tasks. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that HGNAN achieves performance comparable with state-of-the-art hypergraph learning methods while providing intrinsic and meaningful interpretability.

</details>


### [83] [Efficient Multimodal Inference through Adaptive Acquisition and Sequential Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07466)
*Payal Mohapatra,Haodong Yang,Yueyuan Sui,Stephen Xia,Benjamin Lundell,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemARC 通过序列模态聚合器与自适应运行时控制器，在编码前选择模态，实现高效多模态推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态系统通常编码所有输入，即使子集已足够预测；自适应获取虽可降本，但顺序融合使预测依赖顺序，决策需区分因式级多历史。

Method: SemARC 将 SeMA 与 ARC 耦合；SeMA 只执行被选编码器与融合分支，更新固定大小状态，每次获取后预测且不重算早期分支；随机模态子集与顺序监督各获取前缀；ARC 结合集合相关边际效用先验与残差 fitted-Q 学习选择下一模态或停止，不查看未获取输入、不保留获取顺序。

Result: 六个多模态分类数据集、十一个基线上，较各数据集最准基线平均宏F1高3.2%、总推断GFLOPs低61.4%；端到端延迟GPU/CPU降44.0%，Android INT8降47.2%；模态缺失下24种条件中21种匹配或超过最佳基线宏F1，平均GFLOPs低14.8%。

Conclusion: SemARC 为异构设备上的高效多模态推断提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Multimodal systems often encode every available input, even when a subset suffices for prediction. Adaptive acquisition can reduce this cost by using predictions from incrementally fused evidence to decide which modality to encode next and when to stop. However, sequential fusion makes these predictions order-dependent, so decisions based on them may need to distinguish factorially many histories of the same acquired set. We introduce SemARC, which couples a Sequential Modality Aggregator (SeMA) with an Adaptive Runtime Controller (ARC) and uses acquired evidence to select each modality before its encoder runs. SeMA executes only selected encoder and fusion branches, updates a fixed-size state, and predicts after each acquisition without recomputing earlier branches. We supervise every acquisition prefix under randomized modality subsets and orders to encourage consistent predictions across acquisition orders. ARC combines a set-dependent marginal-utility prior with residual fitted-Q learning to select the next available modality or stop, without inspecting unacquired inputs or retaining acquisition order. Across six multimodal classification datasets and eleven baselines, SemARC achieves 3.2% higher macro-F1 and 61.4% lower total inference GFLOPs on average relative to each dataset's most accurate baseline. End-to-end latency falls by 44.0% across GPU and CPU and by 47.2% on Android INT8 relative to the fastest measured baseline, on average. Under varying runtime modality missingness, SemARC still skips available modalities, matching or exceeding the best baseline macro-F1 in 21 of 24 conditions with 14.8% lower total GFLOPs on average. SemARC thus offers a practical path toward efficient multimodal inference across heterogeneous devices.

</details>


### [84] [Structure, Not Belief: Correlated Thompson Sampling from LLM-Derived Covariance in Combinatorial Semi-Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07470)
*Vikram Kakaria,Anish Kataria,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究对组合汤普森采样（CTS）动态的最小改动：用LLM一次性给出臂的分区，经RBF核构造正定相关矩阵Σ，使每轮后验采样具有相关性，但Beta后验仍只由真实奖励更新，即LLM只塑造采样器的移动方式而非其信念。论文给出理想化高斯采样器的贝叶斯遗憾界，并在合成与真实基准上验证相关性采样优于CTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS为每个臂独立抽取后验样本，其探索动态忽略了臂与臂之间的关系。作者希望引入一种外部结构信息（由LLM提供臂的分区）来改善探索效率，同时避免让LLM直接注入信念（如伪观测），因为后者可能带来偏差。因此动机是找到一种既能利用臂间关系、又不污染后验信念的最小改动方式。

Method: 方法上，查询一次LLM得到臂的分区，将分区通过基于簇排名的RBF核转换为正定相关矩阵Σ；每轮的后验样本以协方差Σ抽取，而Beta后验仅由真实奖励更新，从而LLM只影响采样器如何移动而非其信念。理论上为理想化高斯采样器给出自包含的贝叶斯遗憾界，其信息增益分解为来自K簇结构的K log T项和一个增长到d log T的岭项。实验对比CTS在16个合成伯努利族、Microsoft MIND-small新闻基准（d=200篇真实文章）以及无LLM的模拟oracle消融上进行。

Result: 在T=2500时，相关采样器在16个合成伯努利族上相较CTS降低遗憾19%（T=25000、使用数据自适应核时为6-7%）；在Microsoft MIND-small新闻基准上降低41%。伪观测热启动没有带来任何收益。无LLM消融显示，在无结构实例上收益来自核形状本身（随机分区或对采样噪声的简单退火即可复现），而在匹配oracle质量下注入信念从不带来帮助。理论表明√(d/K)的改进是有限时域的瞬态，只有在簇内相关性趋于1时才精确。

Conclusion: 结论是：对CTS动态的这一最小改动（相关性采样）能有效降低遗憾，其收益主要源于核形状而非LLM注入的信念；√(d/K)的改进只是有限时域内的瞬态效应；直接注入信念（如伪观测热启动）并无帮助。这提示在设计LLM辅助的bandit算法时，应关注其提供的结构形状对采样动态的影响，而非将其作为信念来源。

Abstract: Combinatorial Thompson sampling (CTS) draws independent posterior samples for every arm, so its exploration dynamics ignore any relation among arms. We study a minimal change to those dynamics: an LLM is queried once for a partition of the arms, the partition becomes a positive-definite correlation matrix $Σ$ through an RBF kernel on cluster ranks, and the per-round posterior sample is drawn with covariance $Σ$ while the Beta posteriors are updated from real rewards only, so the LLM shapes how the sampler moves, not what it believes. We give a self-contained Bayesian regret bound for the idealized Gaussian sampler whose information gain splits into a $K\log T$ term from the $K$-cluster structure and a ridge term that grows to $d\log T$: the $\sqrt{d/K}$ improvement over independent sampling is a finite-horizon transient, exact only as the within-cluster correlation tends to one. The correlated sampler reduces regret by 19% over CTS on 16 synthetic Bernoulli families at $T=2{,}500$ (6-7% at $T=25{,}000$ with data-adaptive kernels) and by 41% on the Microsoft MIND-small news benchmark ($d=200$ real articles), while pseudo-observation warm starts give nothing. An LLM-free ablation with a simulated oracle of controlled quality shows that on unstructured instances the gain is a property of the kernel shape (a random partition, or a plain tempering of the sampling noise, reproduces it), while belief injection at matched oracle quality never helps.

</details>


### [85] [Adapting to Changes in Agent Behavior via Finite-Depth Policy Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07475)
*Lan Shi,Daigo Shishika,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出有限深度框架，利用参考环境信息近似策略Hessian与混合导数，估计RL策略对他人行为参数的敏感度，并给出截断误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当其他智能体行为发生变化时，将强化学习策略适配到新行为通常需要大量新交互数据。策略敏感度可对局部最优策略随行为参数的变化作一阶预测，但其计算需要二阶导数，且影响会沿未来交互传播，计算困难。

Method: 构建有限深度框架：借助参考环境的信息近似策略Hessian与混合导数，用可调传播深度控制轨迹上导数传播的截断位置，并刻画被有限深度传播忽略的导数贡献，推导近似导数及策略敏感度的截断误差界。

Result: 误差界随传播深度非增，并在全时域传播时消失。以信念驱动的追逃博弈验证：随传播深度增加，导数估计误差总体降低；在估计精度与策略适配上优于基线方法。基于敏感度的初始化相比直接迁移提升了零样本回报，并在目标环境的后续微调中也有优势。

Conclusion: 有限深度传播可在精度与计算成本之间折中，为策略敏感度估计提供有误差保证的方法，并有效支持跨行为变化的策略迁移与微调。

Abstract: Adapting a reinforcement learning policy to changes in another agent's behavior typically requires a large amount of new interaction data. Policy sensitivity provides a first-order prediction of how a locally optimal policy changes with a behavioral parameter, but its computation requires second-order derivatives whose effects propagate across future interactions. We develop a finite-depth framework to estimate this sensitivity by approximating the policy Hessian and mixed derivative using information from a reference environment. The method features an adjustable propagation depth which determines where derivative propagation along the trajectory is truncated. We characterize the derivative contributions omitted by finite-depth propagation and derive truncation-error bounds for the approximated derivatives and resulting policy sensitivity. The bounds are nonincreasing with propagation depth and vanish at full-horizon propagation. Using a belief-driven pursuit-evasion game as a validation scenario, the proposed method generally achieves lower derivative-estimation errors as the propagation depth increases and outperforms the baseline methods in both estimation accuracy and policy adaptation. The sensitivity-based initialization improves zero-shot return over direct transfer, and also shows advantages for the subsequent fine-tuning in the target environment.

</details>


### [86] [SpecBraM: What Should an EEG Foundation Model Predict? Masked Band-Power Prediction versus Waveform Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07484)
*Peng Xie,Yequan Bie,Jianda Mao,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出掩码频带功率预测（MBP）作为自监督EEG预训练目标，在睡眠分期任务上优于波形重建，但增益不通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督EEG模型多采用掩码波形重建或离散码本预测，与下游任务（如睡眠分期）所依赖的节律功率存在错位，且波形目标对相位敏感、码本需学习。

Method: 提出掩码频带功率预测（MBP）：对掩码的通道-时间片段预测固定窄带对数谱能量。与波形目标在相同骨干、相同预训练数据（2388小时）和训练步数下对比，含2×2的tokenizer×target设计，用三个预训练种子。

Result: 在ISRUC和HMC睡眠分期上，MBP比原始及频带波形重建在全标签下高1.6-2.8个平衡准确率点，1%标签下高4.7-7.3点（严格线性探针）；目标效应大于tokenizer效应。冻结特征达0.7916/0.7425，优于匹配的手工谱基线（0.7636/0.7227），但1%标签时差距约1点；全量微调达0.8107/0.7669。增益不适用于所有含谱线索任务，如运动想象、抑郁筛查和警觉度回归。

Conclusion: 预训练目标应匹配下游标签相关的物理量及时空尺度；MBP对睡眠分期有效但增益并非普遍适用。

Abstract: Self-supervised EEG models often reconstruct masked waveforms or predict discrete codes. We study a task-aligned alternative: masked band-power prediction (MBP), which predicts fixed narrow-band log spectral energy for masked channel-time patches. This target retains rhythm power relevant to sleep staging while avoiding phase-sensitive waveform reconstruction and a learned codebook. Across three pretraining seeds, we compare band-power and waveform targets with matched backbones, pretraining data (2,388 hours), and training steps, including a 2x2 tokenizer-by-target design. On ISRUC and HMC sleep staging, MBP exceeds raw- and band-waveform reconstruction by 1.6-2.8 balanced-accuracy points with all labels and 4.7-7.3 points with 1% of labels under a strict linear probe; the target effect exceeds the tokenizer effect. Its frozen features reach 0.7916/0.7425 balanced accuracy, versus 0.7636/0.7227 for a matched rich handcrafted spectral baseline, although the gap is about one point with 1% of labels. Full fine-tuning reaches 0.8107/0.7669. The gains do not extend to every task with spectral cues, including motor imagery, depression screening, and vigilance regression. These results support choosing pretraining targets to match the physical quantities and spatial and temporal scales relevant to downstream labels.

</details>


### [87] [Robust Importance Sampling for Rare Events via Constrained Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07485)
*Paweł Lorek,Rafał Nowak,Rafał Topolnicki,Tomasz Trzciński,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于重要性采样的稀有事件概率估计框架，将问题分解为“覆盖”和“拟合”两阶段，并约束最终GMM提议以保证有限重要性采样方差，在多个基准和高维多模态场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件概率估计中，直接蒙特卡洛效率极低；现有方法如自适应交叉熵、变分推断方法（前向/反向KL）、Safe-ICE、子集模拟和序贯蒙特卡洛存在冷启动困难、效率与鲁棒性不足等问题，尤其在高维和多模态情形下易不稳定或失效。

Method: 通过重要性采样估计 I=P(g(X)>γ)，其中 X 服从高斯分布。框架结合稀有事件估计与交叉熵优化思想，分两步：一是“覆盖”阶段克服冷启动障碍；二是“拟合”阶段在获得有效信号后精炼提议分布。进一步对最终的GMM提议加以约束，确保其重要性采样方差有限，从而兼顾表达力与稳定性。

Result: 在大量基准实验中实现显著的方差缩减，表现出强鲁棒性和良好的成本—效率权衡；在高维与多模态设定下常优于粗蒙特卡洛、自适应交叉熵、变分推断类方法、Safe-ICE、子集模拟及序贯蒙特卡洛等基线，而竞争方法往往不稳定或失败。

Conclusion: 将稀有事件估计拆分为覆盖与拟合，并对GMM提议施加有限方差约束，能生成高表达力且实用的提议分布，显著提升稀有事件概率估计的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study estimating rare-event probabilities $I = \mathbb{P}(g(\mathbf{X}) > γ)$ with $\mathbf{X} \sim \mathcal{N}(\boldsymbolμ, \boldsymbolΣ)$ and general $g : \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$. We address this problem through importance sampling, and propose a framework that substantially improves efficiency and robustness over baselines such as crude Monte Carlo, adaptive cross-entropy, variational-inference-based methods (including reverse- and forward-KL approaches), as well as Safe-ICE, Subset Simulation, and Sequential Monte Carlo, drawing on ideas from both rare-event estimation and cross-entropy optimization. The key contribution has two parts: first, we separate the problem into coverage, to overcome the cold-start barrier, and fitting, to refine proposals once a meaningful signal is available; second, we constrain the final GMM proposal so that it has finite importance-sampling variance (since coverage alone is not sufficient -- without safeguards, importance sampling may still suffer from infinite variance). Together, these ingredients yield expressive proposals; finite variance does not by itself guarantee practical stability at a fixed sampling budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial variance reduction, strong robustness across diverse benchmarks, and favorable cost--efficiency trade-offs, with the proposed approach often outperforming these baselines, particularly in high-dimensional and multimodal settings where competing methods frequently become unstable or fail. Our code is available at https://github.com/lorek/robust-cfi-is.

</details>


### [88] [Who Bears the Burden? Learning Responsibility for Shared Constraints in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07491)
*Xiaoyang Cao,Jingqi Li,Zhe Fu,Alexandre M. Bayen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LiRA方法，通过为每个智能体学习一个共享拉格朗日乘子的“责任份额”，在有限训练周期内优化社会福利，实现预算约束下的公平与高效分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个智能体共享成本预算时，通用拉格朗日乘子只能保证总约束，却无法决定惩罚如何在智能体间分摊；统一惩罚忽视各智能体奖励牺牲的异质性，而智能体专属乘子仍可能依赖同一总成本信号。

Method: LiRA为每个智能体学习其在一个共享乘子中的责任份额，通过优化有限训练周期上的社会福利来分配乘子影响；乘子维持总预算，责任份额重新分配其影响而不改变原始奖励或约束。针对凸博弈，推导了考虑学习更新与数据分布变化的社会福利梯度，以便在收敛前优化责任份额。

Result: 在城市学习、MABIM、Harvest和MetaDrive四个环境中，智能体规模从3到400，LiRA相比统一乘子和智能体专属乘子基线，平均社会福利提升最多29%；电网和驾驶成本保持在预算内，库存违规减少，Harvest更有效利用可用预算。

Conclusion: LiRA通过责任份额分配共享拉格朗日乘子的影响，在不修改原始奖励与约束的前提下，为多智能体预算约束问题提供了兼顾约束满足与社会福利的可行方案。

Abstract: When multiple agents share a cost budget, a common Lagrange multiplier can enforce the aggregate constraint but does not determine how its penalty should be allocated across agents. Uniform penalties ignore heterogeneity in the rewards agents sacrifice, while agent-specific multipliers may still rely on the same aggregate cost signal. We introduce Lagrangian Responsibility Allocation (LiRA), which learns each agent's share of a common multiplier by optimizing social welfare over a finite training horizon. The multiplier enforces the aggregate budget, while responsibility shares redistribute its influence without modifying the original rewards or constraints. For convex games under standard regularity conditions, varying these shares induces a smooth family of normalized generalized Nash equilibria in which active constraints remain at their budgets while welfare varies. To optimize responsibility before convergence, we derive a welfare gradient that accounts for both learning updates and the induced change in data distribution. Across CityLearn, MABIM, Harvest, and MetaDrive, spanning 3 to 400 agents, LiRA improves average social welfare by up to 29% over uniform and agent-specific multiplier baselines. Grid and driving costs remain within budget, inventory violations decrease, and Harvest makes more effective use of available budget.

</details>


### [89] [Source-Learned Reliance for Selective Test-Time Adaptation of Multimodal Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07499)
*Payal Mohapatra,Yueyuan Sui,Haodong Yang,Benjamin Lundell,Stephen Xia,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARAT方法，将模型依赖度评估与运行时损坏检测解耦，用于多模态可穿戴系统的测试时自适应，在多个数据集上取得最优宏F1并降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态可穿戴系统在传感器流噪声或缺失时需保持可靠。现有测试时自适应方法在线评估可靠性，但跨模态一致性在传感器测量不同物理过程时会产生误导，且评估备选模态配置会增加推理成本。

Method: CARAT将模型依赖度与运行时损坏检测解耦，通过源训练摊销依赖度估计。采用非对称模态dropout课程训练缺失鲁棒的主干网络，并从窗口化输入投影梯度范数导出冻结的主干特定依赖度代理。部署时用轻量一分类检测器标记可疑流，代理指导在替换可疑集为缺失符号与衰减其表示之间联合选择，无需评估候选子集。

Result: 在四个可穿戴数据集、五种损坏类型、三个主干网络和八个TTA基线上，CARAT取得最高总体宏F1和最佳平均排名（2.42），在12个等权数据集-主干设置上比最强基线EATA高1.58个F1点。在五个评测配置下，CARAT比EATA少用9.49%的GFLOPs，参数更新少47.82%。

Conclusion: CARAT可作为可穿戴TTA的实用默认方案，在适度计算需求下提供有竞争力的鲁棒性，其收益随主干和数据集类型而变化；此外发现模态主导的单模态TTA方法在异构数据集上可媲美或超越多模态方法。

Abstract: Multimodal wearable systems must remain reliable when sensor streams become noisy or unavailable. Existing multimodal test-time adaptation (TTA) methods often assess reliability online, but cross-modal agreement can be misleading when sensors measure different physical processes, and evaluating alternative modality configurations adds inference cost. We propose CARAT, which decouples model reliance from runtime corruption detection to guide omission or attenuation, amortizing reliance estimation through source training. An asymmetric modality-dropout curriculum prepares a missingness-resilient backbone for omission and derives a frozen, backbone-specific reliance proxy from windowed input-projection gradient norms. At deployment, a lightweight one-class detector flags suspect streams, and the proxy guides a joint choice between replacing the suspect set with the backbone's trained missingness symbol and attenuating its representations before fusion, without candidate-subset evaluation. Across four wearable datasets, five corruption types, three backbones, and eight TTA baselines, CARAT achieves the highest overall macro-F1 and best mean rank (2.42), exceeding EATA, the strongest baseline, by 1.58 F1 points across 12 equally weighted dataset-backbone settings. Across five profiled configurations, CARAT uses 9.49% fewer GFLOPs and updates 47.82% fewer parameters than EATA. A pattern also emerges across sensing regimes: multimodal TTA methods such as PTA are competitive on IMU-dominated homogeneous datasets, whereas unimodal TTA methods like TENT and EATA match or exceed it on heterogeneous datasets. These results position CARAT as a practical default to wearable TTA, offering competitive robustness with modest computational requirements and benefits that vary across backbones and dataset regimes.

</details>


### [90] [Harmful SFT Leaves a Continuous Trace in LLM Checkpoint Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07518)
*Ziqun Bao,Xinyu Zhang,Yuchen Shao,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现SFT阶段优化有害遵从目标会在checkpoint更新空间中留下可读的、连续有序的痕迹，并提出仅基于权重的审计方法TRACE，无需查询模型或访问训练数据即可给出连续有害目标评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练大模型的安全审计主要依赖模型行为，需要执行模型且受限于可用评测的覆盖范围。作者希望探索一个不同的问题：SFT阶段优化的目标行为是否直接在checkpoint更新中留下可读证据，从而提供不依赖行为评测的审计信号。

Method: 作者利用由纯有害遵从、安全导向和良性效用SFT定义的参考几何，构建checkpoint级坐标s_H；通过匹配的遵从-拒绝对照、有害样本数量与训练强度等控制实验验证该痕迹反映SFT目标而非有害输入暴露。在此基础上提出TRACE：将未知checkpoint更新定位到冻结的有害与非有害参考原型之间，把该几何关系转化为连续有害目标分数，仅需权重更新，无需模型查询或未知SFT数据。

Result: 在四个7-8B骨干模型上，s_H与受控有害目标组成的Spearman相关系数为0.986-0.992，且该有序性在更大规模模型上依然存在。TRACE在分布偏移、未见数据、不同SFT配置、部分checkpoint访问以及LoRA/全参数微调下均保持稳定，并与独立测量的攻击成功率正相关；在低有害目标比例下仍具信息量。

Conclusion: checkpoint更新空间中的可读几何痕迹为安全审计提供了与行为评测互补的信号，TRACE可在行为评测不可用或不完整时使用。

Abstract: Safety auditing of post-trained large language models typically relies on model behavior, requiring model execution and depending on the coverage of available evaluations. This work asks a different question: Do the target behaviors optimized during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) leave readable evidence directly in checkpoint updates? We find that harmful-compliance SFT induces a continuous, objective-dependent ordering in checkpoint-update space. Using a reference geometry defined by pure harmful-compliance, safety-targeted, and benign-utility SFT, we find that a checkpoint-level coordinate s_H tracks controlled harmful-objective composition with Spearman correlations of 0.986-0.992 across four 7-8B backbones, with the same ordering persisting at larger model scales. Matched compliance-versus-refusal controls show that this checkpoint trace reflects the SFT objective rather than harmful-input exposure, while additional controls rule out simple explanations based on harmful-example count or generic training intensity. Building on this structure, we introduce TRACE, a weights-only auditing method that localizes an unknown checkpoint update relative to frozen harmful and non-harmful reference prototypes and converts this geometry into a continuous harmful-objective score. TRACE requires neither model queries nor access to the unknown SFT data, and can be evaluated directly from checkpoint updates. Across distribution shifts, unseen data, different SFT configurations, partial checkpoint access, and LoRA/full-parameter fine-tuning, the trace remains stable and is positively associated with independently measured attack success rates. TRACE remains informative even at low harmful-objective proportions, providing a complementary auditing signal when behavioral evaluation is unavailable or incomplete. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Code4TRACE-54D3.

</details>


### [91] [Activation Denoising: A Robustness View on Parallel vs Sequential LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07522)
*Yan Scholten,Rachel Lawrence,James Hensman,Stephan Günnemann,Alicia Curth,Riccardo Grazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"带激活去噪的并行量化"，将上游量化误差视为噪声并做鲁棒性正则化，在单次并行流程中接近序贯量化的效果且大幅省时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最可扩展的后训练量化方法并行地量化每一层，但量化误差会沿残差流累积，因为没有任何层去纠正前面层的误差；序贯量化通过在前序层已量化输出上逐层重新校准来应对误差累积，效果更好，但其串行调度在规模化时成为瓶颈。

Method: 从鲁棒性视角出发，把上游误差建模为噪声，通过预处理步骤加度量加权的舍入来正则化，使模型对该噪声鲁棒；该正则化在每层施加，形成随深度累积的平滑性惩罚，抑制量化误差在模型中放大的程度。同时用更一般的线性变换乘权重（不要求像正交旋转那样保持模型函数），并发现其与正交旋转互补、效果可叠加。

Result: 鲁棒性正则化在单次并行流程中恢复了序贯量化的相当一部分收益，而耗时仅为其一小部分；与正交旋转互补，二者效果累积。

Conclusion: 将累积量化误差视为鲁棒性问题，为规模化下更高效、更准确的LLM量化提供了有原则的基础。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is a powerful tool for compressing large language models. The most scalable methods quantize every layer in parallel, but quantization errors then compound through the residual stream, as no layer corrects for the errors of the layers before it. Sequential quantization accounts for this error compounding by re-calibrating each layer on the already-quantized outputs of its predecessors, yielding stronger results but at the cost of a serial schedule that becomes a bottleneck at scale. As a solution, we propose parallel quantization with activation denoising, which recovers much of the sequential benefit while keeping quantization fully parallel. Rather than re-calibrating layer-by-layer, we take a robustness perspective and model the upstream error as noise, regularizing to be robust to it through a preprocessing step followed by metric-weighted rounding. Applied at every layer, this regularization forms a depth-compounding smoothness penalty that dampens how strongly quantization errors amplify through the model. Unlike orthogonal rotations commonly used in quantization, which must preserve the model's function, we multiply the weights by a more general linear transformation. We find that the two are complementary and their effects compound. Empirically, our robustness regularization recovers a significant part of sequential quantization's benefit in a single parallel pass, at a fraction of its time. Overall, by treating compounding quantization errors as a robustness problem, we offer a principled foundation for more efficient and accurate LLM quantization at scale.

</details>


### [92] [Targeted search shows that random-device testing underestimates worst-case error in a simulated wave-based neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07529)
*Samrendra Roy,Jason Yoo,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数值案例研究，揭示随机采样器件评估波基傅里叶神经算子的局限：最坏器件搜索得到的误差可远超大量随机 Monte Carlo 器件的最大值，说明随机测试只能估计超阈值概率，而最坏器件搜索给出误差上界的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波基处理器有望为神经算子提供快速、节能的傅里叶层，但其通常仅在随机采样器件上验证。实际部署需了解在制造与对准变化下误差能有多大，而随机采样无法回答"误差能有多大"这一问题，因此需要引入最坏器件搜索的评估视角。

Method: 在程式化数值案例中，混合傅里叶神经算子将四个谱层运行于模拟相干4f处理器，含32个带容差旋钮（半宽为代表性而非标定值）。对120个模型（4任务×6训练方法×5随机种子），比较N个随机合规器件的最坏者与一个搜索得到的器件。实验涵盖单次冻结静态误差抽样与20次新抽样、均匀与Sobol'采样、共享旋钮、第二种串扰模型、0.25–2的盒尺度以及像素级器件模型。

Result: 在确定性模拟器上，搜索器件保留误差为200个Monte Carlo器件最大值的1.08–3.10倍，为1000个的1.06–2.71倍；20次新抽样下，仍超过200个随机器件最大值的模型占116/120。均匀采样下抽到此类器件的概率每模型至多0.37%。仅用随机静态误差训练的模型在搜索器件上误差达标称值的3.7–39.9倍；在随机与梯度搜索器件上微调可在全部20个任务-种子对中获得六种方法中最低的搜索误差。两个换热器量上，针对性搜索在全部39个模型中超过1000个随机器件的最坏者，因而超过Wilks 95/95限值；11个模型的平均压力上无随机器件超过1%误差阈值，而搜索器件超过了。

Conclusion: 随机测试估计的是误差超阈值的频率，而最坏器件搜索给出误差能有多大的下界。为可靠量化波基神经算子的失效风险，需在评估中纳入最坏器件搜索而非仅依赖随机采样。

Abstract: Wave-based processors promise fast, energy-efficient Fourier layers for neural operators. They are usually validated on randomly sampled devices, but using them requires knowing how large their error can become under fabrication and alignment variation. In a stylised numerical case study, a hybrid Fourier neural operator runs its four spectral layers on simulated coherent 4f processors with 32 toleranced knobs, whose half-widths are representative rather than calibrated. For 120 models (four tasks, six training methods, five seeds), we compared the worst of N random in-spec devices with a searched one. On a deterministic simulator with one frozen draw of the random static errors, the searched device's held-out error was 1.08-3.10 times the maximum over 200 Monte Carlo devices and 1.06-2.71 times that over 1000. With 20 fresh static draws, it still exceeded the maximum over 200 random devices in 116 of 120 models. Under uniform sampling, the probability of drawing such a device is at most 0.37% per model (two-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson), which says nothing about how large its error is. The gap persisted with uniform or Sobol' sampling at the search's budget, shared knobs, a second crosstalk model, box scales of 0.25-2 and a pixel-level device model. Models trained only with random static errors reached 3.7-39.9 times their nominal error on searched devices, and fine-tuning on random and gradient-searched devices gave the lowest searched error of the six in all 20 task-seed pairs. For two heat-exchanger quantities, a search targeted at each exceeded the worst of 1000 random devices in all 39 models, and hence the Wilks 95/95 limit (worst of 59). For the mean pressure of 11 models, no random device exceeded a 1% error threshold, but the searched device did. Random testing estimates how often errors exceed a threshold; worst-device search gives a lower bound on how large they can be.

</details>


### [93] [Preserving Unstable Modes Through Inverse Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07540)
*Leonardo F. Toso,Yann LeCun,James Anderson,Oumayma Bounou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在JEPA世界模型训练中加入逆动力学（动作重构）目标，可保证编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，从而保留可控不稳定模态，改善视觉控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统存在不稳定模态，小扰动若无反馈会导致无界增长；从高维视觉观测控制这类系统，需要能保留这些模态的表征。JEPA可从视觉数据学习表征与动力学，但本文发现"下一步预测+防坍缩正则"并不保证可控不稳定模态被保留——损失可被最小化而模态被坍缩，导致无法从学到的表征中稳定系统。

Method: 在世界模型训练中增加动作重构目标（逆动力学损失），促使表征具有控制感知性，即保留对控制关键的特征。理论分析在线性系统框架下进行：证明精确动作重构使编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，即H步内可由动作序列到达的任何状态方向都不会被丢弃；并证明随H增大，有限时域可控性格拉姆矩阵的主导特征子空间收敛到可控不稳定子空间。

Result: 在线性系统上建立了上述理论结果，并在非线性视觉控制任务（CartPole、Walker2D、PointMaze）上实证表明结论可推广，验证了控制感知表征学习的收益。

Conclusion: 仅有下一步预测与防坍缩正则不足以保留可控不稳定模态；引入逆动力学损失可实现控制感知的表征学习，理论上保证可达子空间信息不被丢弃，并在非线性视觉控制任务中显示出优势。

Abstract: Robotic systems often exhibit unstable modes, along which small perturbations and disturbances can cause unbounded growth unless corrected through feedback. Controlling such systems from high-dimensional visual observations requires representations that preserve these modes. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) provide a natural framework for learning such representations and their dynamics from visual data. However, we demonstrate that next step prediction combined with anti-collapse regularization does not guarantee that controllable unstable modes are preserved: the training loss can be minimized while these modes are collapsed, making stabilization from the learned representation impossible. To address this, we augment world-model training with an action reconstruction objective (i.e., an inverse dynamics loss) that encourages control-aware representations, namely, visual representations that preserve crucial features for control. We prove that exact action reconstruction makes the encoder injective on the finite-horizon reachable subspace. Thus, the encoder cannot discard any state direction reachable by an action sequence within $H$ steps. Moreover, we show that, as $H$ grows, the dominant eigenspace of the finite-horizon controllability Gramian converges to the controllable unstable subspace. We establish our theoretical results for linear systems and demonstrate empirically that our findings extend to nonlinear visual control tasks (CartPole, Walker2D, and PointMaze), highlighting the benefits of control-aware representation learning.

</details>


### [94] [Foundation Model-Aided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Wireless Random Access Network Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07550)
*Myeung Suk Oh,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基础模型（FM）提升多智能体强化学习（MARL）在随机接入（RA）网络优化中的效率，设计了基于共识去中心化架构的FM辅助Actor-Critic算法，并给出收敛性分析，证明其收敛阶与使用评论家模型交换和线性逼近的传统MARL相同，数值结果验证其显著加速了RA网络优化的MARL训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机接入（RA）是处理多终端不可预测数据流量的基础MAC层调度方案。虽然MARL已被用于优化RA无线网络，但其依赖经验驱动的分布式策略学习，每个优化任务都需大量训练开销，限制了实际应用可行性。因此需要提升MARL效率。

Method: 在基于共识的去中心化MARL架构中，设计了一个基础模型（FM）辅助的Actor-Critic算法；并提供其在局部奖励交换和非线性价值函数逼近下的收敛性分析。

Result: 理论分析表明，该算法达到与采用评论家模型交换和线性逼近的传统MARL相同的收敛阶；数值结果显示，基于FM的方法显著提升了RA网络优化中MARL的训练速度。

Conclusion: 利用基础模型可有效提高MARL在多样化RA网络优化任务中的效率，为RA网络的强化学习优化提供了更可行的实际应用途径。

Abstract: Random access (RA) is one of the most foundational medium access control (MAC) layer scheduling schemes for handling unpredictable data traffic from multiple terminals. While multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) has been explored to optimize RA-based wireless networks, its reliance on experience-driven, distributed policy learning incurs significant training overhead for each optimization task, limiting its feasibility in real-world applications. In this work, we propose to leverage a foundation model (FM) to improve MARL efficiency across diverse RA network optimization tasks. Specifically, we design an FM-aided actor-critic algorithm within a consensus-based decentralized MARL architecture and provide its convergence analysis under local reward exchanges and nonlinear value function approximations to show that our algorithm achieves the same convergence order as the conventional MARL with critic model exchanges and linear approximations. Our numerical results show that our FM-based approach significantly enhances MARL speed for RA network optimization.

</details>


### [95] [Which and When to Admit: Gradient Admission for Data-Centric Small Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07553)
*Hongyu Cao,Yanchi Liu,Kunpeng Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Yanjie Fu,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRADE数据中心框架，通过状态感知样本选择与步级门控控制进入LoRA子空间的梯度，缓解冲突、静态选择与饱和覆盖问题，在三个骨干、七个数据集上一致优于LoRA及基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调在低秩子空间中适配小语言模型时面临三个结构性问题：冲突梯度相互抵消；静态数据选择无法跟踪动态学习过程；子空间饱和导致后续更新覆盖有用方向。作者认为有效适配需控制哪些数据诱导的梯度进入LoRA子空间以及何时进入。

Method: 提出GRADE框架，包含两个机制：一是状态感知选择器，持续接纳与演化中的多任务梯度场对齐的样本；二是自校准的步级门控，拒绝在饱和附近可能造成破坏性覆盖的更新。

Result: 在三个当代骨干模型和七个异构指令数据集池上，GRADE在准确率和鲁棒性上优于强数据选择与PEFT稳定化基线；是唯一在所有架构上持续优于标准LoRA的方法，并产生更连贯的梯度轨迹与更少的破坏性覆盖。

Conclusion: SLM成功适配不仅取决于选择哪些数据，还取决于允许哪些梯度进入并留存于受限更新子空间。GRADE通过梯度对齐的数据中心配方验证了这一观点。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning adapts small language models (SLMs) to heterogeneous instruction data within a low-rank update subspace, making it vulnerable to three structural problems: conflicting gradients that cancel, static data selection that cannot track evolving learning dynamics, and subspace saturation that causes later updates to overwrite useful directions. We argue that effective adaptation therefore requires controlling which data-induced gradients enter the LoRA subspace and when. We propose GRADE (GRadient-Aligned Data-centric rEcipe), a data-centric framework combining two mechanisms: a state-aware selector that continually admits samples aligned with the evolving multi-task gradient field, and a self-calibrating step-level gate that rejects updates likely to cause destructive overwrite near saturation. Across three current-generation backbones and a heterogeneous seven-dataset instruction pool, GRADE outperforms strong data-selection and PEFT-stabilization baselines in accuracy and robustness. It is the only method to improve consistently over standard LoRA on every architecture, while producing more coherent gradient trajectories and less destructive overwrite. These results show that successful SLM adaptation depends not only on which data are selected, but also on which gradients are allowed to enter and persist in the constrained update subspace.

</details>


### [96] [Global Transport Couplings for Classifier-Free Guided Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07555)
*Katarina Petrović,Zander W. Blasingame,Danyal Rehman,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Michael Bronstein,Stephen Y. Zhang,Lazar Atanackovic,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Global Transport (GT)，一种不依赖类别标签的全局类无关最优传输耦合，用于条件流模型训练。GT 单独使用会因将不同条件关联到源噪声不同区域而损害性能，但与 CFG 结合后在多种条件生成任务、模型规模和采样预算下持续提升生成质量，表明条件流的耦合设计应在推理所用的引导流下评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输耦合已被证明能降低无条件流模型的训练方差，但其在条件生成中的作用尚不明确。为每个条件单独构建耦合在现代图像基础模型的大规模或连续条件空间中不可行，因此需要一种通用的、不依赖标签的耦合方案。

Method: 提出 Global Transport (GT)，一种全局类无关的最优传输耦合，计算时不使用类别标签。研究在离散类别条件与连续文本条件的图像生成任务中，跨模型规模评估 GT，并结合 classifier-free guidance (CFG) 分析耦合选择如何改变引导轨迹，同时从经验与理论两方面在引导流下考察耦合。

Result: GT 单独使用时性能变差，因为不同条件被关联到源噪声的不同区域；但与 CFG 结合后，GT 在跨领域、跨模型规模和跨采样预算的条件下一致地改善生成质量。这种反转现象表明引导下的表现与未引导时相反。

Conclusion: 条件流的耦合设计应在推理时所用引导流下进行评估，而非仅看未引导生成。耦合设计是引导流设定下一个简单的训练时改进轴，可在不修改现有架构、采样器或引导机制的情况下提升性能。

Abstract: Optimal-transport couplings have been shown to reduce training variance in unconditional flow models, but their role in conditional generation remains unclear. A natural approach constructs separate couplings for each condition, but this is impractical for large or continuous conditioning spaces found in modern image foundation models. We introduce Global Transport (GT), a global class-agnostic optimal-transport coupling, computed without class labels. GT can associate different conditions with different regions of the source noise, and consequently worsens performance without guidance. However, when combined with classifier-free guidance (CFG), GT consistently improves generation across domains, model scales, and sampling budgets. This reversal suggests that couplings for conditional flows should be evaluated both empirically and theoretically under the guided flow used at inference, rather than on unguided generation. We evaluate GT over both discrete class and continuous text conditioned image generation across model scales, and investigate how coupling choice alters guided trajectories. These results identify coupling design in the guided flow setting as a simple training time axis to improve performance without modifying existing architectures, samplers, or guidance mechanisms.

</details>


### [97] [TAFFY: A Task-Adaptive Tabular Foundation Model with In-Context Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07559)
*Zijian Li,Xiangchen Song,Gongxu Luo,Jie Qiao,Ruichu Cai,Zhenhao Chen,Xinshuai Dong,Fan Feng,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAFFY表格基础模型，通过上下文多样性先验与任务条件循环Transformer增强从上下文推断任务特定预测关系的能力，在六个分类和五个回归基准上取得最低平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的研究表明，合成任务预训练可显著提升上下文学习能力，而整体性能主要取决于模型在推理时能否从可用上下文中推断出任务特定的预测关系。因此需要强化这一能力。

Method: 引入两个组件：1）上下文多样性先验（In-Context Diversity Prior），在共享因果过程上通过受控干预和分布偏移生成多个相关环境来构建每个合成预训练上下文，鼓励模型学习更全面、任务特定的表示；2）任务条件循环Transformer（Task-Conditioned Looped Transformer），迭代且选择性地应用一组共享Transformer块以细化上下文表示，并用任务条件门控调节最终隐状态更新，实现任务自适应的迭代细化。

Result: 在六个分类和五个回归基准数据集上，TAFFY取得最低平均排名。

Conclusion: 上述组件共同促使模型在预训练中通过上下文对比识别预测关系，并为每个任务动态调制上下文整合，从而提升表格基础模型的上下文学习性能。

Abstract: Recent progress in tabular foundation models suggests that training on synthetic tasks can substantially improve in-context learning capabilities, with overall performance largely depending on how well models can infer task-specific predictive relationships from the available context during inference. In this paper, we introduce TAFFY, a tabular foundation model with an In-Context Diversity Prior and a Task-Conditioned Looped Transformer that strengthen this ability. Specifically, to construct each synthetic pretraining context, the In-Context Diversity Prior samples from multiple related environments derived via controlled interventions and distribution shifts on a shared causal process. This in-context diversity encourages the model to learn a more comprehensive and task-specific representation. Moreover, the Task-Conditioned Looped Transformer iteratively and selectively applies a shared group of Transformer blocks to refine contextual representations, with a task-conditioned gate modulating the final hidden-state update. This enables task-adaptive iterative refinement. Together, these components encourage the model to identify predictive relationships from contextual contrasts during pretraining and dynamically modulate context integration for each task. Across six classification and five regression benchmark datasets, TAFFY attains the lowest average rank.

</details>


### [98] [Learning a Mixture of GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07562)
*Tiago da Silva,Amauri H. Souza,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出描述GFlowNet混合模型的通用理论框架，分为连续索引（CI）和离散索引（DI）两类，并据此提出Stratum-Conditioned（SC）GFlowNet，实现无额外计算开销的并行训练加速与更好的模式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet集成虽能改善状态空间探索与收敛，但带来显著的运行时开销，且各方法间的概念联系尚不清晰。

Method: 建立描述GFlowNet混合模型的通用理论框架，细分为连续索引（CI）与离散索引（DI）集合；CI可从随机特征展开角度解释，DI则统一了既有训练方法；基于马尔可夫链Doob h-transform提出Stratum-Conditioned（SC）GFlowNet，按模块化函数分解状态空间并限制各组件采样特定子集，支持集中式与组件级并行训练。

Result: CI GFlowNet被证明可提升图结构任务中的采样器表达能力，并缓解学习不稳定；SC GFlowNet在不引入显著额外计算的情况下，显著加快学习收敛并提升模式覆盖。

Conclusion: 该框架厘清了GFlowNet集成的理论联系，SC GFlowNet为高效、可并行的采样训练提供了新方案。

Abstract: Learning an ensemble of GFlowNets to sample from a discrete target distribution has become a common approach for achieving better state space exploration and convergence than that of a monolithic sampler. However, these methods often add a substantial runtime overhead to the base model, and their conceptual connection remains elusive. To address this, we first propose a general-purpose theoretical framework for describing a mixture of GFlowNets, which we specialize into continuously (CI) and discretely indexed (DI) collections. On the one hand, we show CI GFlowNets can be interpreted through the lens of a random features expansion, provably boosting the sampler's expressivity in graph-structured tasks and reducing learning instability via spectral shifting. On the other hand, we demonstrate DI GFlowNets encompass prior approaches for GFlowNet training and provide the foundation for the newly proposed Stratum-Conditioned (SC) GFlowNets. This method, which is inspired by the Doob's h-transform of Markov chains, decomposes the state space according to a prescribed modular function and restricts each component to sample from a distinct subset of it. Importantly, SC GFlowNets support centralized and component-wise embarrassingly parallel training, and we show both of them significantly speed up learning convergence and mode coverage without introducing any non-negligible extra computation.

</details>


### [99] [Complementary Feature Domains: Information Preservation Does Not Imply Predictive-Contribution Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07565)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu-Adewumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出互补特征域(CFD)理论，将预测价值刻画为由表示及其实现族共同诱导、以上下文为索引的贡献系统，并证明Shannon信息保持并不意味着该贡献系统被保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息论视角通常以信息保持（如可逆变换）作为表示等价或预测价值不变的依据，但预测价值实际上依赖于具体的决策族与实现方式。作者希望区分"信息保持"与"实现依赖的预测贡献"，并刻画两者之间的定量转变规律，为多表示预测提供理论依据。

Method: 作者形式化定义了CFD贡献缺陷，用以度量受控重编码下上下文贡献的变化。针对有界Lipschitz效用，证明每个联盟效用变化受重编码前后可达动作集之间行为距离的约束，从而每个上下文贡献缺陷受相应联盟不相容性之和的界约束。理论上区分了精确行为闭合（导致不变性）与逐步精确补偿（导致恢复）两种情形。并通过一个受控ECG实验验证机制：使用非线性双射重编码在冻结的时频表示下改变固定仿射学习器的准确率，再施加精确逆变换以检验恢复。

Result: 理论上证明Shannon信息保持不蕴含CFD贡献系统保持：可逆表示变换可在目标信息不变的情况下改变受限决策族下的预测贡献；给出了基于行为距离与联盟不相容性的贡献缺陷上界。ECG实验显示，非线性双射重编码保持冻结时频表示中的信息，却改变了固定仿射学习器的准确率，而施加精确逆变换后所有测试联盟的准确率均得到恢复。

Conclusion: 研究将"信息保持"与"实现依赖的贡献"明确分离，并给出多表示预测中贡献转变的定量规律；表明预测价值需在表示与实现族联合的语境中理解，精确行为闭合可保证不变性，而充分补偿可恢复贡献。

Abstract: Complementary Feature Domains (CFD) theory characterizes predictive value as a context-indexed contribution system induced jointly by representations and their realization family. We show that Shannon-information preservation does not imply preservation of this contribution system: an invertible representation transformation can leave target information unchanged while altering predictive contribution under a restricted decision family. We formalize the resulting transition through a CFD contribution defect that measures how contextual contributions change under controlled recoding. For bounded Lipschitz utility, we show that each coalition utility shift is bounded by the behavioral distance between the attainable action sets before and after recoding; consequently, every contextual contribution defect is bounded by the sum of the corresponding coalition incompatibilities. Exact behavioral closure yields invariance, while increasingly accurate compensation yields restoration. A controlled ECG experiment illustrates the mechanism: a nonlinear bijective recoding preserves the information in a frozen time-frequency representation but changes accuracy under a fixed affine learner; applying the exact inverse restores all tested coalition accuracies. The result separates information preservation from realization-dependent contribution and provides a quantitative transition law for multi-representation prediction.

</details>


### [100] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Atmospheric Rivers in GraphCast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07583)
*Madelyn Mathai,Timothy B. Higgins,Kevin M. Grise,Chirag Agarwal,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用稀疏自编码器（SAE）解析GraphCast的内部表征，发现其自发计算了"大气河"强度（IVT）这一稳定内部变量，且Matryoshka SAE能按重要性排序概念并揭示其关系，干预实验证实其因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI天气模型虽预报能力媲美业务预报，但其内部如何表征大气仍是开放问题；特征归因只能揭示哪些输入重要，无法说明模型内部计算了什么、如何组合信息，因此需要新方法探究模型学到的"概念"。

Method: 在GraphCast上训练稀疏自编码器（标准SAE与Matryoshka SAE），以大气河为研究对象，探测其内部概念；通过Matryoshka SAE按重要性排序概念并揭示概念间关系，并用定向干预检验因果性。

Result: 标准与Matryoshka SAE均显示GraphCast把大气河强度（以IVT衡量）计算为一个稳定内部变量，尽管IVT既非输入也非目标；Matryoshka SAE相较标准SAE的非结构化概念检索，能按重要性排序概念并暴露其关系；大气河概念在各深度层持续存在，干预实验证实其因果作用。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种寻找模型内部变量并判断模型实际依赖哪些变量的途径，这也是进一步追问这些变量在气候变暖导致现象变化时是否仍具意义的前提。

Abstract: While AI weather models now rival operational forecasts, how they represent the atmosphere internally remains an open question: feature attribution reveals which input patterns matter, not what the model computes or how it combines information internally. We train sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on GraphCast to uncover its learned concepts, using atmospheric rivers as our phenomenon of focus. Both standard and Matryoshka SAEs show GraphCast computes atmospheric river intensity, measured by integrated vapor transport (IVT), as a stable internal variable, despite IVT being neither an input nor a target. In contrast to the unstructured concept retrieval of the standard SAE, the Matryoshka SAE orders concepts by importance and exposes their relations. Atmospheric river concepts persist across depth and direct interventions confirm causality. This method offers a way to find internal variables and determine which of them the model actually relies on, which is a prerequisite for asking whether those variables remain meaningful as the phenomenon changes under a warming climate.

</details>


### [101] [Hub for Outliers, Spokes for Inliers: Uniform Latent Space Construction for Dual-Mismatched Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07610)
*Li Yuan,Yaxin Hou,Jiawei Tang,Yongbiao Gao,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"轮毂-辐条"（hub-spoke）潜空间几何结构，用于解决半监督学习中因无标注数据类别不均衡且含有未知类而导致的类别分布与标签空间双重不匹配问题，在多种设置下性能提升最高达3.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习假设有标注与无标注数据共享相同的类别分布和标签空间，但现实中无标注数据往往类别不均衡并包含未知类样本。这种双重不匹配会导致多数类主导潜空间、未知类样本被过度自信地误分类，从而降低特征判别性和伪标签质量。

Method: 提出hub-spoke潜空间几何：已知类均匀分布在中心hub周围，每个类围绕其原型形成紧凑簇；hub为高不确定性的未知类样本提供一个"低证据区域"作为锚点。该几何与基于证据（evidence-based）的分类器结合，通过结构化特征组织缓解多数类主导，并引导高不确定性未知类样本向hub聚集。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法优于当前最先进方法，在各种设置下最高提升3.25%。

Conclusion: 通过结构化潜空间几何与证据分类器的结合，能够同时缓解多数类主导并提升特征判别性与不确定性分离能力，有效应对类别分布与标签空间的双重不匹配。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning typically assumes that labeled and unlabeled data share an identical class distribution and label space. However, this setting is often violated: unlabeled data may be imbalanced and contain unknown class samples, causing mismatches in both class distribution and label space. Such dual mismatch leads to majority classes dominating the latent space and unknown class samples being overconfidently misclassified, degrading feature discriminability and pseudo-label quality. To address this, we propose a hub-spoke latent geometry, where known classes are uniformly distributed around a central hub and each class forms compact clusters around its prototype, while the hub provides an anchor for a low-evidence region specifically designed for high-uncertainty unknown class samples. Integrated with an evidence-based classifier, this geometry ultimately enhances feature discriminability and uncertainty separation by mitigating majority-class domination through structured feature organization and guiding high-uncertainty unknown class samples toward the hub. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with a maximum improvement of 3.25% across various settings.

</details>


### [102] [AFA-BANDIT: Provably Near-Optimal Online Multi-Feature Classification Under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07615)
*AbdAlRahman Odeh,Teng-Hui Huang,Hesham El Gamal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将在线主动特征获取(AFA)建模为组合式带背包的赌博机(BwK)问题，提出了一种新的算法LP-Chain，并在理论上给出了改进的遗憾上界，实验上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线主动特征获取(Online AFA)需要在样本到达时决定获取哪些昂贵特征并进行预测，现有方法要么使用深度强化学习但缺乏性能保证，要么只优化成本调整后的奖励而无法强制全局预算约束。因此需要一种既有理论保证又能处理全局预算、组合复杂性和演化奖励的在线AFA框架。

Method: 作者将在线AFA形式化为组合式带背包的赌博机(Combinatorial BwK)问题，统一了特征获取与预测决策。利用基数感知的置信界和子集更新结构推导出改进的遗憾上界。为避免指数级动作空间，提出LP-Chain算法，通过搜索随特征数线性增长的代价感知特征子集链来高效行动。

Result: 在理论上，论文在组合BwK框架下获得了相比标准BwK更优的遗憾上界。在合成数据实验中，LP-Chain的性能优于基于HEDGE的BwK方法和基于深度强化学习的在线AFA基线，并且在特征数量增加时具有良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作为在线主动特征获取提供了新的组合式带背包赌博机建模，兼顾了理论保证与计算可行性。LP-Chain在保持与现有方法相当的预测性能的同时，提升了效率和扩展性，为在线AFA问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) is a classification problem in which an agent decides which costly features to acquire before predicting each sample's label. Unlike batch AFA, which trains a fixed policy and classifier offline on fully observed data, online AFA updates its predictor from revealed labels as samples arrive. Existing online methods either use deep reinforcement learning (RL) without performance guarantees or maximize cost-adjusted reward rather than enforce a global budget. We formulate online AFA as a combinatorial Bandits with Knapsacks (BwK) problem that couples acquisition and prediction. Unlike prior bandit-based AFA and classical BwK, our setting has combinatorial complexity, evolving rewards, a global budget, and structured side information. We obtain an improved regret upper bound over standard BwK bounds in this framework, leveraging a cardinality-aware confidence bound and the subset update structure. To avoid an exponentially large action space, we propose \emph{LP-Chain}, a variant that searches a cost-aware chain of feature subsets with a size that grows linearly with the number of features. While the regret upper bound is specific to the combinatorial framework, \emph{LP-Chain} empirically achieves comparable predictive performance. On synthetic data, \emph{LP-Chain} outperforms HEDGE-based BwK and deep RL-based online AFA baselines and scales favorably to more features.

</details>


### [103] [Stateless Language Agents: Scaling Long-Horizon Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07625)
*Qizheng Zhang,Changxiu Ji,Isaac Sun,Yuetai Li,Shubhangi Upasani,Sherry Ruan,Boyuan Ma,Fenglu Hong,Vamsidhar Kamanuru,Yoonho Lee,Yuzhen Mao,Genghan Zhang,Rulin Shao,Qiuyang Mang,Andy Dimnaku,Changran Hu,Radha Poovendran,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无状态语言智能体（SLA）框架，主张将持久研究状态从智能体会话中移出，由外部harness管理状态并为每次调用重构角色专属的聚焦上下文，从而在长周期自动化研究中实现更高效的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统让大模型智能体在长周期上运行，但更多推理并不等于更多进展：智能体会重放不断增长的历史、重复彼此的工作，或在token持续消耗时停止实验。而现有评测多用短预算或早期就饱和的基准，未能检验这些失败模式。作者将失败归因于两个设计选择：研究状态存放在哪里，以及由谁决定下一步尝试什么。

Method: 提出"有状态搜索 + 无状态智能体"原则并实现为SLA框架。任何智能体都不跨调用携带对话；harness拥有研究状态（候选解与实测结果），并为每次调用重建全新的、角色专属的上下文，使智能体所见成为显式设计选择而非随运行增长的历史。框架中，无状态Advisor读取harness汇总的跨搜索方向证据并分配具体实验，由并行Worker执行。

Result: 在软件工程、内核优化与算法设计三类任务、最高十亿token预算下，与三个近期框架对比，SLA在每项任务上都取得最佳最终结果，并以减少超过84%的token达到最强内核基线的最终性能。共享检查点消融表明，聚焦上下文与显式任务分配各自都有贡献，且效果可在完整运行中累积；Advisor消耗的token不足0.6%。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用SLA——将持久研究状态排除在智能体对话之外，同时说明短评测周期可能误判研究系统及其组件。建议以长预算和不易饱和的基准来评测自动化研究系统。

Abstract: Automated research systems increasingly run LLM agents over long horizons, but more inference does not by itself produce more progress: agents replay growing histories, duplicate one another's work, or stop experimenting while token consumption continues. Yet most evaluations use short budgets or benchmarks that saturate early, leaving these failure modes untested. We trace these failures to two choices: where research state lives and who decides what to try next. We introduce Stateless Language Agents (SLAs), built on the principle of stateful search with stateless agents: no agent carries its conversation across invocations; instead, the harness owns the research state (candidate solutions and measured outcomes) and reconstructs a fresh and role-specific context for every invocation. What each agent sees becomes an explicit design choice rather than a history that grows with the run. We implement this principle in the SLA framework, where a stateless Advisor reads harness-summarized evidence across search directions and assigns concrete experiments to parallel Workers. We evaluate SLA against three recent frameworks on software engineering, kernel optimization, and algorithm design at budgets of up to one billion tokens. SLA achieves the best final result on every task and reaches the strongest kernel baseline's final performance with over 84% fewer tokens. Ablations from shared checkpoints show that focused contexts and explicit assignments each contribute to SLA's progress, with effects that can compound over full runs, while the Advisor consumes less than 0.6% of tokens.
