Method: 构建 CheckerBench 可执行基准，包含 300 个任务，源自 297 个 CVE，覆盖 167 个代码库、85 个 CWE 和五种语言生态；每个任务包含易受攻击和修复后的版本、固定分析环境及检查器脚手架。并提出 CheckerLab 通用评测框架，独立重建提交的检查器，并测量易受攻击-修复诊断对比、补丁定位、误报和工具使用等指标。

Result: 在 21 种模型-框架配置及每种配置三次独立重复实验中，平均 Pass@1 为 32.30%，最佳达到 45.33%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可靠且可复用的检查器开发对当前编码智能体仍具挑战性。

Abstract: Static-analysis checker synthesis requires agents to interpret a defect specification, inspect a repository, implement analyzer-specific logic, and refine the checker through repeated compilation and analysis feedback. Existing coding-agent benchmarks focus on tasks such as patch generation or vulnerability detection and rarely assess whether an agent can develop a working checker in a repository from start to finish. We introduce CheckerBench, an executable benchmark of 300 tasks derived from 297 CVEs across 167 repositories, 85 CWEs, and five language ecosystems. Each task includes vulnerable and fixed revisions, a pinned analysis environment, and a checker scaffold. We further introduce CheckerLab, a common evaluation framework that independently rebuilds submitted checkers and measures vulnerable-fixed diagnostic contrast, patch localization, false positives, and tool use. Across 21 model-harness configurations and three independent repeats per configuration, mean Pass@1 is 32.30%, while the best reaches 45.33%. These results show that reliable, reusable checker development remains challenging for current coding agents.

</details>


### [210] [When Old Facts Return: Re-Reads, Reverts, and the Limits of Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07715)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 记忆系统会因同一旧语句重现而恢复已废弃的值，但"重读旧源"与"真正回退"产生相同观测序列却要求相反的当前答案；作者在130个原子转换上研究该歧义，发现防复活的守卫可将重读情形下的准确率从10.8%恢复到97.7%，但无法识别合法回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于身份的时间记忆存在歧义：重新读取旧源代码与真正的版本回退会产生相同的观测值序列，却要求相反的当前答案，导致系统无法区分"观测到某值"与"该值确实发生变化"这两类情形。

Method: 从软件修复中提取130个"提取器选定的原子转换"作为研究对象；设置普通转换条件作为基线，再追加对旧语句的逐字重读构造歧义条件；并引入一个拒绝重新激活已废弃值的守卫机制，辅以两项支持性研究（向答案模型暴露废弃历史、为变更行为提供当前源码），另对707个软件修复做探索性提取以界定范围。

Result: 普通转换条件下，基于身份的时间记忆达到98.5%的模型判定准确率，在字面陈旧值代理下零观测错误；追加逐字重读后准确率降至10.8%，陈旧值率升至88.5%；守卫机制在该重读条件下将准确率恢复至97.7%、陈旧值率降至0.8%，但无法在缺少变更溯源时识别合法回退。

Conclusion: 设计启示是必须区分"对某值的观测"与"该值已发生变化的证据"；结论仅限所保留的实验，受限于选定输入、仅聚合的答案记录、同族判定器及失败后守卫评估。

Abstract: A memory system can retire an obsolete value and later restore it merely because the same old statement appears again. A re-read of an old source and a genuine revert can produce the same observed sequence of values while requiring opposite current answers. We study this ambiguity on 130 extractor-selected atomic transitions derived from software fixes. In the ordinary transition condition, identity-based temporal memory reaches 98.5% model-judged accuracy with zero observed errors under a literal stale-value proxy. Appending a verbatim re-read of the old statement reduces accuracy to 10.8% and raises the stale-value rate to 88.5%. A guard that refuses to reactivate a previously retired value restores accuracy to 97.7% and reduces that rate to 0.8% in this constructed re-read condition. The guard cannot also recognize a legitimate revert without additional change provenance. Two supporting studies examine exposing retired history to the answer model and supplying current source for changed behavior. An exploratory extraction study over 707 software fixes provides scope context, not a universal coverage estimate. The design implication is to distinguish an observation of a value from evidence that the value changed. Selected inputs, aggregate-only answer records, related-family judges and a post-failure guard evaluation limit the conclusions to the retained experiments.

</details>


### [211] [Acquiring and Verifying Repository Norms for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07757)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhenxi Chen,Christoph Treude,Mingwei Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 RepoNorm，独立于编码任务地获取仓库显式与隐式规范，并交付给现有编码智能体，显著提升规范遵循率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体生成的改动虽能通过功能测试，却未满足仓库的贡献要求。遵循仓库特定规范需从分散的仓库来源中识别指导信息，并解读其条件与例外；检索与文档方法仅提供通用上下文，智能体仍需自行判断哪些规范适用。

Method: RepoNorm 独立于编码任务获取显式与隐式仓库规范，利用仓库证据检查规范内容与适用性，必要时查询 Git 历史，并将规范包交付给现有编码智能体。评估使用三个编码模型与 RepoNormBench 的 121 个任务。

Result: 相对无额外生成指导的 Raw 基线，整体规范遵循率（NCR）提升 7.42–10.77%，贡献 NCR 提升 31.64–45.44%，提示省略 NCR 提升 11.34–17.69%；三个模型在 RepoNorm 下的各项 NCR 均高于 CodeWiki 文档。功能成功率较 Raw 提升 5.79–9.09 个百分点，但配对比较未达显著性阈值。默认配置下抽样精确率为 86%。

Conclusion: RepoNorm 能有效提升编码智能体对仓库规范的遵循程度，尤其显著改善贡献相关规范，且优于文档式方法，但功能成功率提升尚不显著。

Abstract: Changes produced by coding agents can pass functional tests while leaving repository contribution requirements unmet. Following repository-specific norms requires identifying guidance dispersed across repository sources and interpreting its conditions and exceptions. Retrieval and documentation approaches supply general context, but agents must still determine which norms apply. We introduce RepoNorm to acquire explicit and implicit repository norms independently of coding tasks. It checks norm content and applicability using repository evidence, consults Git history when needed, and delivers norm packages to existing coding agents. Our evaluation uses three coding models and 121 tasks from RepoNormBench. Against the baseline with no additional generated guidance (Raw), relative improvements are 7.42-10.77% for Overall Norm Compliance Rate (NCR), 31.64-45.44% for Contribution NCR, and 11.34-17.69% for Prompt-omitted NCR. All three coding models also obtain higher values on these NCR measures with RepoNorm than with CodeWiki documentation. Functional success rates show observed gains of 5.79-9.09 percentage points over Raw; the paired comparisons do not reach the significance threshold. Sampled precision is 86% with RepoNorm's default configuration.

</details>


### [212] [ES-Trace: Auditing Ethical-Sourcing Disclosure of Code Generation Models Beyond Model Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07762)
*Zhuolin Xu,Haibo Wang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ES-Trace框架，借助模型文档溯源图（MDTG）审计代码生成模型的伦理溯源披露，发现模型卡单独审计低估披露水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型在软件开发中应用渐广，但其开发涉及知识产权、隐私、公平性、劳动实践与环境影响等伦理溯源问题。已有工作虽定义了伦理溯源标准，但不清楚现有模型披露了多少证据、证据位于何处。

Method: 提出ES-Trace伦理溯源披露审计框架，通过模型文档溯源图（MDTG）表示模型、版本与文档工件间的关系，从而追溯模型卡之外的披露证据。应用于10个发布者的26个模型，覆盖77份文档与20个ES-CodeGen方面。

Result: 仅审计模型卡的平均得分为1.77/5；解析所声明的引用后升至2.82/5，且增幅主要来自发布者结构化元数据中声明的文档。

Conclusion: 主要发现：(1) 社会与劳动相关方面即使扩大文档审计范围仍记录不足；(2) 解析文档引用显著提高观察到的披露程度（1.77→2.82/5）；(3) 仅模型卡审计会误判发布级别的披露变化；(4) 文档不匹配会将证据关联到错误的模型或版本。研究呼吁采用引用感知的审计、显式的模型—版本绑定与文档一致性，并加强社会与劳动方面的记录。

Abstract: Code generation models have been increasingly used in software development, but their development raises ethical-sourcing concerns involving intellectual property, privacy, fairness, labour practices, and environmental impact. Although prior work has defined ethical-sourcing criteria for code generation, it remains unclear how much evidence existing models disclose and where that evidence can be found. We introduce ES-Trace, a framework for ethical-sourcing disclosure audits that traces disclosed evidence beyond model cards using the Model Documentation Traceability Graph (MDTG), which represents relationships among models, versions, and documentation artifacts. We apply ES-Trace to 26 models from 10 publishers across 77 documents and 20 ES-CodeGen aspects. Model-card-only auditing yields a mean score of 1.77/5, while resolving the declared references increases it to 2.82/5, with most of the increase arising from documents that the publisher declares in structured metadata. The key findings of our study include: (1) social and labour-related aspects remain poorly documented, even when expanding the audit to the full documentation scope, (2) resolving documentation references substantially increases observed disclosure, raising the mean score from 1.77/5 to 2.82/5, (3) model-card-only audits can mischaracterize release-level disclosure changes, and (4) documentation mismatches can associate evidence with the wrong model or version, highlighting the need for explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts. Our study calls for reference-aware ethical-sourcing disclosure audits, explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts, and stronger documentation of currently underreported social and labour-related aspects.

</details>


### [213] [Harness Engineering for Software Engineering via Modular Executable Dev-Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07832)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Peng Kuang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dev-Primitives（将仓库组件与其常驻 LLM 配对，形成可执行、可自修改的抽象单元）和基于此的 HERMES 框架，通过依赖感知动态激活与缺陷诊断机制在仓库规模上实例化这些原语。在四个软件工程基准上平均超越匹配基线 12.4%，并以更低推理成本接近强模型配置的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备终端访问能力的大语言模型智能体在长周期软件工程工作流中表现脆弱：程序状态分散于源文件、配置、测试、依赖和运行时行为中，需要反复重建，导致交互历史过长、上下文爆炸和语义漂移；大型仓库也使任务相关组件的识别更加困难。

Method: 提出 Dev-Primitives，将仓库组件从被动的软件制品转变为软件工程中的主动参与者。每个 Dev-Primitive 将一个仓库制品与一个常驻 LLM 配对，为制品提供基于其自身实现和依赖的智能体原生接口，支持自然语言推理、组件间通信和局部自我修改。在此基础上提出 HERMES 框架，通过依赖感知的动态激活机制和将执行证据映射回需修改组件的缺陷诊断机制，在仓库规模上实例化这些原语。

Result: 在四个软件工程基准上的实验表明，HERMES 平均超越匹配的基线 harness 12.4%。当搭配强激活与诊断模型时，即使使用 Qwen3-8B 作为 Dev-Primitives，HERMES 在全部四个基准上与同构的 GPT-5.6 Sol 配置的差距也在 4.5% 以内，同时在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上降低推理成本 26.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，harness 设计对软件工程智能体至关重要：通过模块化、可执行的 Dev-Primitives 与有效的激活和诊断机制，可以在显著降低成本的同时接近强模型配置的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) equipped with terminal access have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating software engineering tasks. However, existing agents remain brittle on long-horizon workflows, where they must repeatedly reconstruct program state scattered across source files, configurations, tests, dependencies, and runtime behavior, leading to increasingly long interaction histories, context explosion, and semantic drift. Large repositories further complicate the identification of task-relevant components. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Dev-Primitives} (\emph{Development Primitives}), a modular and executable abstraction that transforms repository components from passive software artifacts into active participants in software engineering. Each Dev-Primitive pairs a repository artifact with a resident LLM, which gives the artifact an agent-native interface grounded in its own implementation and dependencies, enabling natural-language reasoning, inter-component communication, and localized self-modification. Building on Dev-Primitives, we propose \textbf{HERMES}, a Harness Engineering framework for software engineeRing via Modular Executable Dev-PrimitiveS, which instantiates these primitives at repository scale through a dependency-aware dynamic activation mechanism and a bug diagnosis mechanism that maps execution evidence back to the components that must be revised. Extensive experiments on four software engineering benchmarks demonstrate that HERMES outperforms matched baseline harnesses by 12.4\% on average. Moreover, when paired with strong activation and diagnosis models, HERMES, even with Qwen3-8B Dev-Primitives, remains within 4.5\% of the homogeneous GPT-5.6 Sol configuration across all four benchmarks, while reducing inference cost by 26.2\% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, highlighting the importance of harness design in software engineering agents.

</details>


### [214] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Dense and Mixture-of-Experts Models for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08173)
*Anvi Kalpesh Shah,Umamaheswara Sharma B*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出加权质量指数（QI），融合功能正确性、可维护性、安全性与生成效率，用可配置权重评估自动程序修复模型，发现不同权重下模型排名会变化，且MoE模型以更少激活参数达到接近大稠密模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统APR评估仅以生成补丁是否通过测试套件为标准，会掩盖模型在可维护性、安全性和计算成本上的差异，可能导致对模型能力的片面甚至误导性判断，因此需要一个多维、可配置权重的综合评价框架。

Method: 受ISO/IEC 25010软件质量模型启发，提出加权质量指数（QI），将功能正确性、可维护性、安全性与生成效率按可配置权重组合；在相同本地硬件上评估三个稠密模型Qwen2.5-Coder（3B、7B、14B）以及160亿参数、24亿激活参数的MoE模型DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite，数据集为40个QuixBugs和90个Defects4J缺陷。

Result: 模型排名随权重方案变化，说明单一指标评估会掩盖权衡关系；MoE模型与7B、14B稠密模型在正确性上几乎无统计显著差异（McNemar精确检验），但激活参数少3-6倍；而在三个稠密模型规模间，正确性随规模显著提升。

Conclusion: 对于稀疏代码模型，激活参数数量可能比总参数数量更能反映其真实能力，APR评估应采用多维加权框架而非仅依赖测试通过率。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) with language models is usually evaluated by whether a generated patch passes the test suite, which can hide differences in maintainability, security, and computational cost. We propose a Weighted Quality Index (QI), inspired by the ISO/IEC 25010 software quality model, that combines functional correctness, maintainability, security, and generation efficiency under configurable weighting schemes. We evaluate three dense Qwen2.5-Coder models (3B, 7B, 14B) and the 16B-parameter DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model (2.4B active parameters) on 40 QuixBugs and 90 Defects4J bugs, all run locally on identical hardware to control for infrastructure effects. Model rankings change with the weighting scheme, showing that single-metric evaluation can hide trade-offs. The MoE model shows almost no statistically significant difference in correctness from the 7B and 14B dense models (McNemar's exact test) while using 3-6 times fewer active parameters, whereas correctness increases significantly across the three dense scales. These results suggest that active parameter count can be a more informative lens than total parameter count for sparse code models.

</details>


### [215] [Newer and Bigger, but Safer? A Longitudinal Study of the Functionality-Security Gap in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08240)
*Thiago Santos de Moura,Fynn Matuschek,Flavio Toffalini,Yannic Noller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对七个模型家族（五个开源权重）共32个LLM进行纵向研究，评估其"功能-安全差距"，发现新模型确实更安全但无人消除差距，且开源并不必然更不安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码常通过功能测试却存在安全漏洞，此即"功能-安全差距"。此前一项长期研究基于三个模型家族得出结论：模型变聪明但不变安全，且唯一考察的开源权重家族停滞。该结论能否推广到其他家族，尤其是紧凑模型，尚不明确。

Method: 采用CWEval基准，涵盖五种编程语言、119个任务与31类CWE，对七个模型家族（五个开源权重）各三代旗舰与紧凑版本共32个LLM，跨家族、模型规模和语言比较差距变化轨迹。

Result: 新模型在绝对意义上更安全，但没有家族消除差距；与既有工作不同，开放性并不区分家族——所有开源权重家族均显著缩小差距，而某旗舰闭源模型仍保持与Llama同样宽的差距。紧凑模型通常不如旗舰安全，但有例外。CWE层面确认日志注入（CWE-117）、HTTP响应拆分（CWE-113）等长期弱点，最新闭源模型在内存与整数类弱点上出现回退，同一CWE在不同语言中风险差异很大。

Conclusion: 开发者不应假定升级即提升安全性，也不应假定闭源模型更安全；每次更换模型后都应重跑安全检查，并在模型上下文中提供安全API。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used to generate code. Although their functional plausibility keeps improving, the generated code often contains security vulnerabilities. The functionality-security gap captures code that passes functional tests but fails security tests. A recent longitudinal study of three model families concluded that LLMs become smarter but not safer, with the only considered open-weight family stagnating. Whether this holds for other (open-weight) families and particularly for compact models remains open. We present a longitudinal study of the gap across 32 LLMs from seven model families (five open-weight), covering three successive releases per family in flagship and compact variants. Using CWEval with 119 tasks in five programming languages and 31 CWEs, we compare trajectories across families, model sizes, and languages. Newer models do become safer in absolute terms, although no family closes the gap. Unlike prior work, we find that openness does not separate the families: every considered open-weight family narrows the gap significantly, while Gemini 3.1 Pro keeps a gap as wide as the one reported for Llama. Compact models usually produce less secure code than their flagship counterparts, with notable exceptions (e.g., Gemini 3.7 Flash). At the CWE level, we confirm persistent weaknesses such as log injection (CWE-117) and HTTP response splitting (CWE-113) and regressions in the newest proprietary models on memory and integer weaknesses, and show that the same CWE carries very different risk across languages. From these results, we derive implications for LLM vendors, researchers, and developers. In particular, developers should assume neither that upgrades improve security nor that proprietary models are more secure; they should rerun security checks after each model change and provide secure APIs in the model's context.

</details>


### [216] [Learning from Failures: A Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement for LLM-Based Vulnerability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08405)
*Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出失败驱动提示精炼(FDPR)方法，通过分析LLM漏洞分析中的反复失败模式来系统改进提示设计，实验表明该方法提升了可靠性并产出可复用的提示设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件漏洞分析中虽有前景，但效果高度依赖提示设计。现有研究多用聚合性能指标比较提示策略，难以揭示模型失败的深层原因，也无法系统性地改进提示。

Method: 提出FDPR方法：在Damn Vulnerable Java Application(DVJA)上识别反复出现的失败模式（假阳性、假阴性、无依据推理、CWE错误分类），将其转化为针对性的提示精炼；随后在Juliet测试套件上评估新提示，并进行跨模型验证以检验泛化性。

Result: 失败驱动的精炼提升了LLM漏洞分析的可靠性，并提炼出可复用的提示设计原则。

Conclusion: 反复出现的模型失败为提示工程提供了有原则的基础，可支撑更可靠的LLM漏洞分析系统的系统性开发。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as promising tools for software vulnerability analysis, but their effectiveness depends heavily on prompt design. Existing research primarily compares prompting strategies using aggregate performance metrics, providing limited insight into why models fail or how prompts can be improved systematically. We propose Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement (FDPR), a methodology that analyzes recurring model failures to guide evidence-based prompt refinement. Using the Damn Vulnerable Java Application (DVJA), we identify recurring failure modes, including false positives, false negatives, unsupported reasoning, and CWE misclassification, and translate them into targeted prompt refinements. We then evaluate the resulting prompt on the Juliet Test Suite and perform cross-model validation to assess generalizability. The results show that failure-driven refinement improves the reliability of LLM-based vulnerability analysis while yielding reusable prompt design principles. More broadly, this work demonstrates that recurring model failures provide a principled foundation for prompt engineering, enabling the systematic development of more reliable LLM-based vulnerability analysis systems.

</details>


### [217] [RAPO-Sol: Retrieval-Augmented Preference Optimization for Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08429)
*Rongcun Wang,Shi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPO-Sol，一个两阶段训练框架（RAFT + DPO），用于仓库级Solidity智能合约代码生成，在推理时无需检索即可提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solidity智能合约管理资产、权限和不可逆状态变更，代码生成既有用又对安全至关重要。仓库级生成要求模型在状态变量、修饰符、事件、继承、外部调用和访问控制逻辑间保持一致性，难度大。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段采用检索增强微调（RAFT），为每个训练输入附加相似Solidity样例以学习合约级模式，但推理时无需检索；第二阶段采用直接偏好优化（DPO），通过Solidity语义锚点扰动（SAP）构造被拒样本，扰动验证语句、可见性修饰符、数据位置关键字、上下文变量、支付操作和低级调用，使模型偏好参考合约而非语义有缺陷的近似样本。

Result: 在SolidityBench上，使用CodeLlama-7B-Instruct、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct测试，RAFT持续优于监督微调，基于SAP的DPO在BLEU和SolidityScore上进一步提升；完整RAFT+DPO流程在三个模型上均取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 训练时的检索增强与Solidity感知的偏好优化具有互补收益，且不增加推理阶段的检索成本。

Abstract: Smart contracts written in Solidity manage assets, permissions, and irreversible state changes, making code generation both useful and security-critical. Repository-level Solidity generation is challenging because models must synthesize complete contracts or libraries while preserving consistency across state variables, modifiers, events, inheritance, external calls, and access-control logic. We present RAPO-Sol, a two-stage training framework for repository-level Solidity code generation. First, Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (RAFT) augments each training input with similar Solidity examples, helping the model learn recurring contract-level patterns while remaining retrieval-free at inference time. Second, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trains the model to prefer reference contracts over close but semantically flawed alternatives. We construct rejected samples using Solidity Semantic-Anchor Perturbation (SAP), which perturbs validation statements, visibility modifiers, data-location keywords, context variables, payment operations, and low-level calls. Experiments on SolidityBench with CodeLlama-7B-Instruct, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct show that RAFT consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning, while SAP-based DPO provides further gains in BLEU and SolidityScore. The full RAFT+DPO pipeline achieves the best performance across all three models, demonstrating complementary benefits from retrieval during training and Solidity-aware preference optimization without adding retrieval cost at inference.

</details>


### [218] [A Case Study in Assuring AI-Written Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08651)
*Lindsey Ferris,Sierra Bonilla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本案例研究描述了一个由无正式软件工程训练的操作者通过编码智能体构建的生产级医疗平台，其工作流演化为"人类主导的元智能体系统"。研究表明，当代码量与复杂度超出人工审查能力时，测试、监控与审查智能体均存在可失败性，人类控制的关键在于让预期结果、判定证据、智能体权限与人类最终决策共同锚定于同一目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体既能让缺乏正式训练的人构建原本无法实现的系统，也会产出超出专家有效审查能力的代码量。因此，穷尽式代码审查无法作为人类控制的唯一可靠基础，需要探讨在这种情况下人类如何保持对系统的控制。

Method: 采用案例研究法，考察一个通过编码智能体构建、由无正式软件工程训练的操作者治理的生产级医疗平台。随时间推移，该工作流发展为人类主导的元智能体系统：一个智能体编写代码，其他智能体进行监督与审查，项目规则负责传递经验教训。

Result: 操作者发现用于监督系统的测试、监控与审查智能体均具有可失败性：部分监控测量的是代理指标而非实际结果；部分审计静默失败；缺失的检查从报告结果中消失；一次自动修复还引发了运营中断。

Conclusion: 在此案例中，人类控制依赖于将预期结果、用于判断结果的证据、智能体的权限以及人类的最终决策，全部绑定到同一个底层目标之上。

Abstract: Software-engineering agents can enable people without formal software training to build systems they could not otherwise implement and simultaneously can produce more code than even experts can meaningfully inspect. In both cases, exhaustive code review is not reliable as the sole basis for human control. We report a case study of a production healthcare platform built through coding agents and governed by an operator without formal software-engineering training. Over time, its workflow grew into a human-led meta-agent system where one agent wrote code, other agents supervised and reviewed it, and project rules carried lessons forward. The operator found that tests, monitors and reviewing agents used to supervise the system were fallible. Some monitors measured proxies rather than outcomes, some audits failed silently, missing checks disappeared from reported results and one automated repair caused operational disruption. In this case, human control depended on keeping the intended outcome, the evidence used to judge it, the agents' permissions and the final human decision were all tied to the same underlying objective.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [When Robots Crash: Optimal Asynchronous Gathering at Weber Meeting Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06939)
*Animesh Maiti,Prakhar Shukla,Abhinav Chakraborty,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究无限网格上异步、匿名、遗忘型移动机器人在崩溃故障下的最优聚集问题，提出无需指定领导者的CrashTolerantWeberGathering()算法，证明强多重检测下部分全对称配置不可解，其余配置可实现有限时间最优聚集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常依赖指定机器人打破对称性，一旦该机器人崩溃会无限阻塞其余机器人，因此需要一种每个机器人都能独立选出同一目标、且不依赖任何特定机器人存活的容错聚集方案。

Method: 在无限网格上，机器人具备全局可见性与强多重检测但无坐标系与手性；允许最多n-2个机器人永久崩溃且崩溃与延迟不可区分。算法让每个机器人从自身快照独立选出相同的Weber会合节点作为唯一共同目标，并采用目标依赖的受限最短路径，保持目标的最优性并绕开崩溃机器人。

Result: 证明在强多重检测下，某些完全对称配置上最优聚集是不可能的；对剩余所有配置，算法能选出唯一共同目标、在执行过程中始终保持其最优性，并允许正常机器人不等待崩溃机器人持续推进，从而保证有限时间内完成聚集。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了崩溃故障下无需领导者、可独立决策的最优聚集算法，同时刻画了强多重检测下最优聚集的不可解边界，提升了移动机器人聚集问题的容错性与最优性保证。

Abstract: We study the \textit{optimal gathering} problem over a finite set of designated \textit{meeting nodes} for \textit{asynchronous, anonymous,} and \textit{oblivious} mobile robots on an infinite grid under crash faults. The robots have global visibility and strong multiplicity detection, but share neither a coordinate system nor chirality. The objective is to gather all non-faulty robots at a \textsc{Weber Meeting Node}, minimizing the total Manhattan distance from their initial positions. Up to $n-2$ robots may crash permanently, and such crashes are indistinguishable from arbitrary delays. Existing approaches often rely on a designated robot to break symmetry, whose crash may block the remaining robots indefinitely. Instead, our approach enables every robot to independently select the same target from its snapshot, while target-dependent restricted shortest paths preserve the target as a \textsc{Weber Meeting Node}. We prove that, under strong multiplicity detection, optimal gathering is impossible from certain fully symmetric configurations. For all remaining configurations, our algorithm \textsc{CrashTolerantWeberGathering()} selects a unique common target, preserves its optimality throughout the execution, and allows non-faulty robots to progress without waiting for crashed robots, thereby guaranteeing gathering in finite time.

</details>


### [220] [LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration for Constrained Multi-Objective Container Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07030)
*Suvarthi Sarkar,Abhishek Hazra,Akshat Mittal,Aryabartta Sahu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM驱动多智能体协作的自适应容器放置框架，在满足多资源容量与反亲和硬约束前提下，同时优化能耗与亲和性，实验显示较能耗贪心基线降耗8.36%、亲和性提升39.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心容器放置需同时最小化能耗、最大化亲和偏好，并满足多资源容量与反亲和约束。传统固定规则方法缺乏对动态集群状态的自适应性且难以扩展；元启发式方法虽灵活，但计算开销大、速度慢且易陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的多智能体协作框架：四个专用智能体在每个放置步骤中运行闭环ReAct循环。能耗智能体与亲和智能体就冲突目标进行辩论，放置智能体通过容忍门控仲裁（TGA）解决冲突；重排智能体在放置后精化阶段通过单调改进规则（MIR）进一步优化决策。所有智能体均基于环境提供的确定性、可行性过滤后的候选表进行推理，保证动作天然满足硬约束。

Result: 基于Google Cluster Trace（100个应用、25台机器配置）的实验表明，该框架比能耗贪心基线降低8.36%能耗，亲和性提升39.30%。消融实验确认多轮辩论机制、放置智能体中的TGA、重排智能体中的MIR各组件均有实质贡献，移除任一组件都会同时降低能耗与亲和性表现。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的多智能体协作框架可自适应且高效地解决容器放置的多目标冲突问题，在保证硬约束满足的同时显著优于传统基线，且各架构组件均不可或缺。

Abstract: Container placement in data centers must simultaneously minimize power consumption and maximize affinity preferences, while satisfying multi-resource capacity and anti-affinity constraints. Traditional approaches typically rely on fixed rules, which lack adaptability to dynamic cluster states and are difficult to extend for adaptive decision-making. On the other hand, meta-heuristic methods, although more flexible, are often computationally expensive, slower and prone to getting trapped in local optima. In this work, we propose an adaptive and efficient approach based on an LLM-driven multi-agent collaboration framework, where four specialized agents operate in a closed-loop ReAct cycle at each placement step. A Power Consumption Agent and an Affinity Agent debate over competing objectives, while a Placement Agent resolves conflicts using Tolerance-Gated Arbitration (TGA). A Rearrangement Agent further refines decisions through the Monotone Improvement Rule (MIR) in a post-placement refinement phase. All the agents reason over deterministic, feasibility-filtered candidate tables provided by the environment, ensuring that every proposed action inherently satisfies hard constraints. Based on experimental evaluation using the Google Cluster Trace with a configuration of 100 applications and 25 machines, the proposed framework consumes 8.36% less power and achieves 39.30% higher affinity than the power-greedy baseline. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component, the multi-round debate mechanism, the TGA in the Placement Agent, and the MIR in the Rearrangement Agent, contributes meaningfully to the overall performance, with removal of any single component degrading both power consumption and affinity satisfaction.

</details>


### [221] [Repository-Scale Performance Characterization of the IO500 Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07035)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Anila Ghazanfar,Sepehr Mahmoodianhamedani,Sascha Safenreider,Julian Kunkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对2019—2025年间116个站点提交的294份post-reset IO500基准测试记录进行仓库级特征分析，揭示各基准阶段性能与综合排名之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IO500是评估HPC存储系统的通用基准，其不断增长的提交仓库为研究跨系统、跨时间的性能行为提供了机会。但以往研究多局限于排行榜排名，未能充分挖掘仓库数据的价值，因此需要对IO500结果进行超越排行榜的系统性分析。

Method: 结合描述性分析与时间分析，并采用阶段级相关性分析、规模敏感性分析以及基准日志检查，考察单个基准阶段所代表的性能特征与综合排名之间的关系。

Result: 测量相似I/O行为的各阶段之间关系强，而带宽与元数据性能之间的关系较弱且对规模敏感；时间分析和日志级分析还揭示出仅凭汇总分数无法显现的信息。

Conclusion: 分析IO500结果在排行榜排名之外具有重要价值，并指出在使用社区基准数据进行比较和纵向HPC性能分析时，需重视系统规模、基准执行和仓库来源等关键因素。

Abstract: The IO500 benchmark provides a common basis for evaluating high-performance computing (HPC) storage systems, while its growing submission repository also offers an opportunity to study performance behavior across systems and time. This work presents a repository-scale characterization of 294 post-reset IO500 submissions from 116 sites spanning 2019--2025. We combine descriptive and temporal analysis with phase-level correlation, scale-sensitivity analysis, and benchmark-log examination to investigate how performance characteristics represented by individual benchmark phases relate to composite rankings. The results show strong relationships among phases measuring similar I/O behavior, but weaker and scale-sensitive relationships between bandwidth and metadata performance. Temporal and log-level analyses further reveal information that is not apparent from aggregate scores alone. These findings demonstrate the value of analyzing IO500 results beyond leader-board rankings and identify important considerations concerning system scale, benchmark execution, and repository provenance when using community benchmark data for comparative and longitudinal HPC performance analysis.

</details>


### [222] [T-CCL: Resource Efficient and Performant Collective Communication using Tensor Memory Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07098)
*Keyvan Dadashzadeh,Yuehong Zhou,Minyu Cui,Miquel Pericas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 T-CCL，一种基于 TMA 的资源高效集合通信库，通过将数据搬运与规约卸载到 TMA 并以异步流水线方式执行，在保持高带宽的同时大幅降低 SM 资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 Transformer 模型依赖多 GPU 执行，需要频繁的集合通信。现有通信库为达到高带宽或低延迟往往占用大量 GPU 线程，导致 SM 侧资源占用过高，尤其在通信与计算并发时挤压其他 GPU 工作的可用资源。因此高效集合通信不仅要有高性能，还应降低 SM 侧资源使用。

Method: T-CCL 面向节点内通信，基于 Tensor Memory Accelerator（TMA）实现。它将数据移动与规约操作均卸载到 TMA，并把每个集合通信执行为一系列异步 TMA 操作的流水线，从而在维持高带宽的同时减少所需的 SM 资源。

Result: 在 AllReduce、AllGather、ReduceScatter 上，T-CCL 在资源不受限时比 NCCL 最多快 2.4x，在资源受限预算下最多快 3.42x，与 NCCL 最新的对称内存内核性能相当，且在实测案例中占用相同或更少的 SM。在 GEMM-通信重叠案例中，将后端从 NCCL 换成 T-CCL 后，相对串行基线的算子级平均加速在两 GPU 上从 1.12x 提升到 1.25x，四 GPU 上从 1.04x 提升到 1.14x。集成到 vLLM 作为通信后端后，端到端推理吞吐相比 vLLM 自动后端调度最多提升 1.31x，并在所有评估的 batch size 上均优于其表现。

Conclusion: T-CCL 证明通过 TMA 卸载与流水化设计可同时兼顾集合通信的高带宽与低 SM 资源占用，在重叠计算场景和真实推理系统中均带来显著收益，是一种资源高效的节点内集合通信方案。

Abstract: Large transformer-based models increasingly depend on multi-GPU execution, which requires frequent collective communication among GPUs. Existing communication libraries often rely on many GPU threads to achieve high bandwidth or low latency, resulting in a large streaming multiprocessor (SM)-side resource footprint. This footprint can limit the resources available to other GPU work, particularly when communication and computation execute concurrently. Thus, efficient collective communication should not only achieve high collective performance but also reduce its SM-side resource usage. This paper presents T-CCL, a resource-efficient collective communication library based on the Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) for intra-node communication. T-CCL offloads both data movement and reduction operations to TMA and executes each collective as a pipelined series of asynchronous TMA operations, reducing the SM resources required for collective communication while maintaining high bandwidth. Evaluated across AllReduce, AllGather, and ReduceScatter collectives, T-CCL outperforms NCCL by up to 2.4x with unrestricted communication resources and up to 3.42x under restricted resource budgets, remains competitive with NCCL's recent symmetric-memory kernels, and occupies the same or fewer SMs in profiled cases. In a GEMM-collective overlap case study, switching the communication backend from NCCL to T-CCL raises the average operator-level speedup over a sequential baseline from 1.12x to 1.25x on two GPUs and from 1.04x to 1.14x on four GPUs, as T-CCL uses fewer SMs for communication, leaving more SMs available to the overlapped GEMM. Integrated into vLLM as a communication backend, T-CCL improves end-to-end inference throughput over vLLM's automatic backend dispatch by up to 1.31x, outperforming it at every evaluated batch size on both the conversation and decode-heavy workloads.

</details>


### [223] [Memory-Efficient Expert Routing for Distributed MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07333)
*Arnab Kanti Tarafder,Jaume Guasch-Martí,Gokcen Kestor,Jie Ren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RelayMoE：基于环的MoE执行模型，避免全量top-k扩展分发缓冲，实现内存高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型向数百专家和高top-k路由扩展时，分布式训练的内存效率成为关键瓶颈。峰值内存由MoE块主导，其分发流水线中每个中间缓冲都随top-k路由线性放大。标准all-to-all分发器需一次性构造完整top-k扩展缓冲，内存开销大。

Method: 提出RelayMoE环式执行模型，在专家权重或token循环流动时本地计算，避免全量top-k扩展分发缓冲；根据通信量在专家路由与token路由间选择，并重叠传输与计算；环结构天然支持反向传播中的内存高效重计算，每次跳步重建专家中间激活、计算梯度后立即释放。

Result: 在30B-57B生产MoE模型及多种专家配置下评估：单层MoE实验较Megatron-LM平均提速2倍；相同GPU内存预算下全模型训练吞吐提升最高2.02倍，最大可训练序列长度延长最高2.85倍。

Conclusion: RelayMoE通过环式执行与重计算显著降低MoE训练内存瓶颈，节省的内存可支持更长序列、更大批次或保留更多注意力激活以减少重计算，提升训练吞吐。

Abstract: As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale toward hundreds of experts and higher top-$k$ routing, memory efficiency in distributed training becomes a critical bottleneck. Peak memory is dominated by the MoE block, not attention: every intermediate buffer in the MoE dispatch pipeline is individually scaled by top-k routing. The standard all-to-all dispatcher sends all routed tokens in a single collective step, requiring the full top-$k$-expanded buffer to be constructed at once. In this work, we propose RelayMoE, a ring-based MoE execution model that computes locally as expert weights or tokens circulate, avoiding full top-$k$-expanded dispatch buffers. RelayMoE selects between expert and token routing according to communication volume and overlaps transfers with computation. The ring structure naturally supports memory-efficient MoE recomputation during backward: each hop reconstructs expert intermediates, uses them to compute gradients, and releases them before the next hop. The saved memory supports longer sequences and larger batches, or retains more attention activations to reduce attention recomputation and improve training throughput. We evaluate RelayMoE on 30B$-$57B production MoE models and varied expert configurations. In single-layer MoE experiments, RelayMoE achieves a $2\times$ average speedup over Megatron-LM. In full-model training under the same GPU memory budget, it improves throughput by up to $2.02\times$ and extends the largest tested trainable sequence length by up to $2.85\times$.

</details>


### [224] [Byzantine-Tolerant Causal Unicast with Constant Message Space Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07368)
*Purv Patel,Ajay D. Kshemkalyani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拜占庭容错的因果消息排序算法，将单播应用消息空间开销降至最优的O(1)，通过“发送许可(SPS)”不变式与“隔离缓冲乐观模型”实现，并形式化证明了系统级活性与一种弱化的因果安全抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步系统中的因果消息排序对分布式应用至关重要，但在拜占庭故障下同时保证强安全性与活性本质困难。现有拜占庭容错方案要么依赖同步假设，要么存在O(n)消息空间开销与O(n^2)消息复杂度，要么使用密码学却无法保证强安全性。因此需要一种兼具低开销、无需密码学且能提供可量化安全保证的方案。

Method: 提出新的拜占庭容错因果排序算法：采用“发送许可(SPS)”不变式；设计“隔离缓冲乐观模型”作为内存管理架构；摒弃统一因果缓冲区，改用隔离的按对等节点队列，并配备事件驱动的“级联空间驱逐”机制，以规避拜占庭状态钉住与队头阻塞攻击。

Result: 实现了针对点对点消息的最优O(1)应用消息空间开销；形式化证明了算法保证系统级活性，并满足称为“拥塞松弛因果投递”的弱化因果安全抽象；在诚实节点间通信中严格保持弱因果安全，除非极端网络延迟或拜占庭扣留攻击超出可量化的本地缓冲容量而触发乐观驱逐。

Conclusion: 该算法在因果排序与高吞吐、常数消息空间开销、低计算开销及严格有界本地空间之间取得平衡，为拜占庭环境下的因果排序提供了理论与实践兼顾的可行方案。

Abstract: Causal message ordering provides essential semantics for distributed applications, yet ensuring it within an asynchronous system subject to Byzantine failures presents fundamental theoretical and practical challenges. Prior research establishes that algorithms cannot guarantee both strong safety and liveness without using cryptography under these conditions. Existing Byzantine-tolerant solutions make synchrony assumptions or suffer from $O(n)$ message space overheads and $O(n^2)$ message complexity, where $n$ is the number of processes in the system, or use cryptography, but may not guarantee strong safety. In this paper, we present a novel Byzantine-tolerant causal ordering algorithm that achieves an optimal $O(1)$ application message space overhead for point-to-point messages. Our approach uses a Sender Permission to Send (SPS) invariant and an \textit{Isolated-Buffer Optimistic Model} for the memory management architecture. To circumvent Byzantine state-pinning and head-of-line blocking attacks, instead of unified causal buffers, we use isolated, per-peer queues equipped with event-driven \textit{Cascading Space Evictions}. We formally prove that our algorithm guarantees system-wide liveness and satisfies a weakened causal safety abstraction called \textit{Congestion-Relaxed Causal Delivery}. Under this model, weak causal safety is strictly preserved for all honest-to-honest communications unless extreme network latency or Byzantine withholding attacks exceed quantifiable local buffer capacities, forcing optimistic queue evictions. This formal guarantee successfully balances causal ordering with high throughput, constant message space overhead, low computational overhead, and strictly bounded local space.

</details>


### [225] [Productivity in HPC: A Critical Review from SLOC and Performance Portability to AI-Assisted Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07495)
*Ami Marowka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对高性能计算（HPC）生产力测量方法进行了批判性综述，梳理了从代码行数、静态复杂度、可用性研究到开发时间模型与语义方法的发展脉络，指出AI辅助编程等新趋势下代码转换与人力投入的对应关系正在弱化，并总结了量化评估的主要局限与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产力一直是HPC领域的核心关切，但始终缺乏可行的形式化定义和广泛接受的量化度量指标。近期在性能可移植性分析中引入的代码分歧、收敛性和语义度量，未能建立生产力与性能可移植性之间的可信形式关系；而JACC、Mojo、JLIR、Accl++等语言与编译系统虽在工程上取得进展，却多以可编程性、生态复用、代码规模或实现负担来间接表示生产力，而非直接测量人力投入。因此有必要对HPC生产力测量进行系统性批判回顾。

Method: 采用批判性文献综述方法，追溯HPC生产力测量从源代码行数（SLOC）、静态复杂度、实证可用性研究，到开发时间模型和语义方法的发展。同时考察自动化迁移与AI辅助编程如何削弱代码转换与人力投入之间的对应关系，使投入转向验证、纠错和性能调优。并从测量内容、直接性、客观性、可复现性、实用性及其与性能和可移植性的关系等维度对各类方法进行比较。

Result: 综述表明，现有生产力度量方法在直接性、客观性和可复现性等方面各有不足；AI与自动化迁移的兴起使得传统以代码转换量衡量人力投入的方式逐渐失效，生产力评估的重心正转向验证、纠错和调优等环节。

Conclusion: 本文识别并总结了HPC生产力量化评估的主要局限与开放挑战，呼吁建立更可行、更直接的度量框架，以应对自动化与AI辅助编程带来的新变化。

Abstract: Productivity has long been recognized as a central concern in high-performance computing (HPC), yet it still lacks a workable formal definition and a broadly accepted metric for quantified assessment. Recent attempts to incorporate productivity into performance-portability analysis have introduced code-divergence, convergence, and semantic measures, but have not established a convincing formal relationship between productivity and performance portability. More recent language and compiler systems, including JACC, Mojo, JLIR, and Accl++, pursue productive performance portability through higher-level abstractions, multi-level intermediate representations, automatic kernel generation, and runtime compilation. These systems demonstrate substantial engineering progress, while productivity is still commonly represented through programmability, ecosystem reuse, code size, or reduced implementation burden rather than direct measurement of human effort. This paper presents a critical review of HPC productivity measurement, tracing its development from source lines of code (SLOC), static complexity, and empirical usability studies to development-time models and semantic approaches. We also examine how automated migration and artificial-intelligence(AI)-assisted programming weaken the correspondence between code transformation and human effort, shifting effort toward verification, correction, and performance tuning. The reviewed methods are compared in terms of what they measure, directness, objectivity, reproducibility, practicality, and their relationship to performance and portability. We conclude by identifying the principal limitations and open challenges for quantified HPC productivity assessment.

</details>


### [226] [Mosaic: GPU Sharing with Latency Guarantees through Kernel-Level Interference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07504)
*Foteini Strati,Ethan Graham,Leo Stephan,Paul Elvinger,Ana Klimovic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mosaic，一种显式建模 GPU 干扰机制的核级干扰预测器，并基于它构建调度器 MosaicSched，在保证延迟 SLO 的同时最大化尽力而为吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 在 AI 负载下常被低效利用，工作负载共置可提升利用率，但会引入干扰，损害延迟敏感型负载。现有方法要么用粗粒度启发式调度，要么依赖模拟器特定或同样粗粒度的预测器，无法捕捉线程块放置、内存层次争用、SM 内部资源争用等多种干扰机制。

Method: 提出 Mosaic，一个核级干扰预测器，结合解析模型与轻量学习模型显式建模多种干扰机制；并将其集成到调度器 MosaicSched 中，进行在线核准入控制，在整卡共置与 SM 分区之间做选择。

Result: 在四种 GPU 架构上，Mosaic 将预测误差相比先前预测器降低最多一个数量级；MosaicSched 在所有工作负载下将 p99 延迟保持在目标 SLO 以下或非常接近。

Conclusion: 显式建模 GPU 干扰机制的核级预测能显著提升精度，并支撑在线调度在满足延迟 SLO 的前提下提升尽力而为吞吐。

Abstract: GPUs are increasingly in demand for AI workloads, yet often remain substantially underutilized, motivating workload colocation. However, colocation introduces interference that can degrade latency-critical workloads. Existing approaches mitigate interference using either heuristic-based scheduling or interference predictors. Heuristics rely on coarse-grained metrics that overlook important interference sources, while predictors often depend on simulator-specific or similarly coarse metrics. However, GPU interference is complex and arises from multiple mechanisms, including thread-block placement, memory hierarchy contention, and intra-SM resource contention. We present Mosaic, a kernel-level interference predictor that explicitly models these mechanisms using a combination of analytical and lightweight learned models. Across four GPU architectures, Mosaic reduces prediction error by up to an order of magnitude compared to prior predictors. We integrate Mosaic into a scheduler, MosaicSched, that performs online kernel admission control and selects between full-GPU colocation and SM partitioning to maximize best-effort throughput while satisfying latency SLOs. Across all workloads, MosaicSched keeps the p99 latency below or very close to the target SLO.

</details>


### [227] [NCCL M2N: A Layout- and Topology-Aware Collective for Distributed Tensor Resharding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07516)
*Kaushik Kandadi,Youngeun Kwon,Sreeram Potluri,Ching-Hsiang Chu,Ke Wen,Pouya Kousha,Sangkug Lym,Nitin Nitin,Manjunath Gorentla Venkata*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出NCCL M2N，一种布局与拓扑感知的集合通信原语，用于分布式张量重分片，在256 GPU上实现最高7.9倍传输加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练与rollout生成的张量布局不同，需在进程组间重分片权重，但标准集合通信无法直接表达这种M-to-N重分布。现有flat direct会向目的副本重复发包，gather-then-broadcast则集中网络注入并传输冗余数据，导致通信开销巨大。

Method: NCCL M2N根据源/目的mesh与placement推导传输区域与全局通信调度。其分层路由将源贡献均衡分配到可用目的rank，在目的NVLink域间只转发一份拷贝，再经NVLink完成本地复制；网络传输与本地复制重叠，避免副本倍增的源出口流量。并建立聚合数据移动模型刻画两阶段极限。

Result: 在NVL72集群最多256块GB200 GPU、NDR InfiniBand上测试：单FFN-MoE层传输达9.8 ms，比flat direct（77.3 ms）提速7.9倍；256 GPU DeepSeek-V3 NeMo-RL实验中，权重同步时间从5.78 s降至2.77 s，相对all-gather+broadcast提速2.09倍，步时减少12.7%。

Conclusion: NCCL M2N作为布局与拓扑感知的集合通信原语，有效解决了M-to-N重分片中的冗余流量问题，显著降低权重同步与step时间开销。

Abstract: Distributed training and rollout generation often use different tensor layouts, requiring model weights to be resharded across distinct process groups. This M-to-N redistribution is not directly expressed by standard collectives. Flat direct sends duplicate traffic across destination replicas, while gather-then-broadcast concentrates network injection at one root and transfers data that destinations do not need. For DeepSeek-V3 on 256 GPUs, legacy all-gather plus broadcast accounts for 29.4% of the reported reinforcement-learning step time. We present NCCL M2N, a layout- and topology-aware collective primitive for distributed tensor resharding. Given source and destination meshes and placements, it derives the required transfer regions and a global communication schedule. Its hierarchical route balances source contributions across eligible destination ranks, forwards one copy between destination NVLink domains, and completes local replication over NVLink. Network transfer and local replication overlap, avoiding replica-multiplied source egress. An aggregate data-movement model captures the limits of both stages. We evaluate NCCL M2N on up to 256 GB200 GPUs in an NVL72 cluster with NDR InfiniBand. A single FFN-MoE layer transfer achieves up to 7.9x speedup over flat direct sends (9.8 ms versus 77.3 ms). In a separate 256-GPU DeepSeek-V3 NeMo-RL experiment, NCCL M2N reduces reported weight-sync time from 5.78 s to 2.77 s, a 2.09x speedup over legacy all-gather plus broadcast, and reduces step time by 12.7%.

</details>


### [228] [TRANSIT: Transparent Scale-in for Multi-Node LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07593)
*Hyungyo Kim,Nicholas Satchanov,Hrishi Shah,Gaohan Ye,Jiaqi Lou,Robert Walkup,Shweta Salaria,I-Hsin Chung,Hubertus Franke,Seetharami Seelam,Apoorve Mohan,Nam Sung Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TRANSIT 是一个透明的 scale-in 框架，将 CPU DRAM 作为 GPU 内存扩展，使多节点模型训练可在更少 GPU 上保持效率，无需修改应用、框架、调度器、驱动或操作系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式训练中，GPU 显存容量限制了模型规模与并行配置。现有框架管理的卸载（offloading）方案效率不足，且往往需要修改软件栈，难以在不牺牲训练效率的前提下减少 GPU 用量。

Method: TRANSIT 通过用户空间插桩（interposition）层，在分布式训练期间透明地将 CPU DRAM 作为 GPU 显存的延伸，无需修改应用、训练框架、集群调度器、设备驱动或操作系统；同时采用零拷贝数据路径进行 CPU-GPU 传输以提升效率。

Result: 在最多 64 块 NVIDIA H100 GPU、RoCE 网络上，对稠密与 MoE 模型及多种并行配置评估显示：相比 TorchTitan、ZeRO-Offload、ZeRO-Infinity，每 GPU 吞吐分别最高提升 68%、59%、42%；使用减少 50% 的 GPU 仍保持基线 90% 以上吞吐；每节点网络流量最多降低 33%；通信受限场景下每 GPU 吞吐最多提升 35%。

Conclusion: TRANSIT 提供了一种透明、易部署的显存扩展方案，在减少 GPU 用量的同时维持甚至提升训练效率，并降低网络开销，适用于稠密与 MoE 大模型训练。

Abstract: TRANSIT is a transparent scale-in framework to enable multi-node model training on fewer GPUs while maintaining training efficiency by transparently leveraging CPU DRAM as an extension of GPU memory during distributed training. It achieves this through a user-space interposition layer, requiring no modifications to the application, training framework, cluster scheduler, device driver, or operating system. Furthermore, TRANSIT achieves higher efficiency by leveraging a zero-copy data path for CPU-GPU transfers. We evaluate TRANSIT on dense and MoE models across scales up to 64 NVIDIA H100 GPUs and multiple parallelism configurations over a RoCE network. Our evaluation shows that TRANSIT can: (a) outperform state-of-the-art framework-managed offloading techniques, achieving up to 68%, 59%, and 42% higher per-GPU throughput than TorchTitan, ZeRO-Offload, and ZeRO-Infinity, respectively, (b) enables training with 50% fewer GPUs while maintaining over 90% of baseline per-GPU throughput, (c) lower per-node network traffic by up to 33%, and (d) improve per-GPU throughput by up to 35% in communication-bound settings.

</details>


### [229] [FailBench: Evaluating Fault Tolerance Across Distributed Training Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07688)
*Khaled Aljbab,Amine Barrak*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 FailBench——一个覆盖七种分布式训练架构、八种崩溃容错机制及无容错基线的统一评测框架，在142个组合上系统评估后给出容错机制选择决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错机制通常只在自身设计的架构上被评估，跨架构缺乏可比性，导致实践者在选择容错机制时缺少指导依据。

Method: 构建统一评测框架 FailBench，覆盖7种分布式训练架构、8种崩溃容错机制与1个无容错基线，考察单点、并发与级联fail-stop故障；在8xV100集群上评估142个（架构, 机制, trace）组合，单rank检查点规模205 MB至2.7 GB。

Result: 1) 无普适最优机制：A2上磁盘检查点稳态开销最低（0.5%），内存复制恢复最快（约17 ms），即时检查点避免周期性稳态开销但故障时需约0.9 s；当进程组重构耗时达秒级时，机制差异更多体现在运行时开销而非恢复速度。2) gossip训练在丢失worker后样本吞吐提升17.9%，但相较匹配的无故障基线在损失进展上无可检测改善。3) 机制开销强依赖架构：内存复制开销从3.7%到176%不等。

Conclusion: 作者将上述发现提炼为容错机制选择的决策框架，并将 FailBench 作为开放工件发布，为跨架构容错机制选型提供实证依据。

Abstract: Distributed deep learning relies on data, pipeline, tensor, and hybrid parallelism, yet fault-tolerance mechanisms are typically evaluated only on the architecture for which they were designed. This leaves practitioners with little guidance when choosing mechanisms across architectures. FailBench provides a unified evaluation harness covering seven distributed training architectures, eight crash-fault-tolerance mechanisms and a no-FT baseline, and single, concurrent, and cascading fail-stop failures. We evaluate 142 (architecture, mechanism, trace) combinations on an 8xV100 cluster, with per-rank checkpoint states ranging from 205 MB to 2.7 GB. Three findings emerge. First, no mechanism is universally best: on A2, disk checkpointing has the lowest steady-state overhead (0.5%), in-memory replication restores fastest (~17 ms), and just-in-time checkpointing avoids periodic steady-state checkpoint cost but incurs ~0.9 s upon failure. When process-group re-formation takes seconds, mechanisms differ more in runtime overhead than restore speed. Second, gossip training increases sample throughput by 17.9% after losing a worker, yet shows no detectable improvement in loss progress over a matched no-fault baseline. Third, mechanism cost depends strongly on architecture: in-memory replication overhead ranges from 3.7% to 176%. We translate these findings into a decision framework for selecting fault-tolerance mechanisms and release FailBench as an open artifact.

</details>


### [230] [When Can Stateless Recovery Defeat Byzantine Quorum Safety? A Tight Normal Form for Single-Step BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07759)
*Arnab Mallick,Indraveni Chebolu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究拜占庭法定人数安全中副本恢复丢失协议状态却保留身份与签名密钥时可能遗忘先前投票的问题，分析了由至少 q 个副本匹配签名投票构成的证书，给出了冲突证书重叠规模界，并将松弛量分解为四个非负计数，刻画了零松弛的精确签名者划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拜占庭法定人数安全依赖正确副本拒绝签署冲突值。然而，副本在恢复过程中丢失协议状态但仍保留身份和签名密钥时，可能遗忘之前的投票，从而破坏安全性假设。论文旨在形式化并量化这种恢复导致的冲突证书风险。

Method: 论文研究由 n 个副本中至少 q 个副本的匹配签名投票构成的证书，假设每个正确副本在两次恢复之间避免冲突投票。分析两个冲突证书的重叠规模，其大小介于 2q-n 与 b+c 之间（b 为拜占庭副本数，c 为执行期间恢复的正确身份数）。主结果将松弛量 b+c-(2q-n) 分解为四个非负计数：第一证书中的额外签名者、第二证书中的额外签名者、两个证书之外的标识、以及重叠之外的拜占庭或恢复标识。并区分证书形成与接受，给出基于配置的故障与恢复上限的充分检查，并解释为何在签名释放前写入持久化投票记录可防止冲突。

Result: 零松弛会强制形成精确的签名者划分。在 n=3f+1 副本、阈值 q=2f+1、至多 f 个拜占庭副本且恰好一次正确恢复事件的设定下，任何冲突对都恰好强制 f 个拜占庭副本全部位于重叠区并与已恢复副本一起；每个证书都有一个由 f 个正确副本构成的互不相交的侧面。一个最小协议可达到这种形式。

Conclusion: 论文通过松弛量分解精确刻画了恢复事件下冲突证书的结构，表明在标准参数下冲突必然要求最大数量的拜占庭副本且它们与恢复副本共同位于重叠区。同时强调持久化投票记录在签名释放前写入是防止此类冲突的关键机制。

Abstract: Byzantine quorum safety relies on correct replicas refusing to sign conflicting values. A replica that loses its protocol state during recovery but retains its identity and signing key may forget an earlier vote. We study certificates formed by matching signed votes from at least $q$ of $n$ replicas, assuming that each correct replica avoids conflicting votes between recoveries. If two conflicting certificates form, their overlap has size between $2q-n$ and $b+c$, where $b$ counts Byzantine replicas and $c$ counts correct identities that recovered during the execution considered. Our main result decomposes the slack $b+c-(2q-n)$ into four nonnegative counts: extra signers in the first certificate, extra signers in the second, identities in neither certificate, and Byzantine or recovering identities outside their overlap. Zero slack forces an exact signer partition. With $n=3f+1$ replicas, threshold $q=2f+1$, at most $f$ Byzantine replicas, and exactly one correct recovery event, any conflicting pair forces exactly $f$ Byzantine replicas, all in the overlap together with the recovered replica; each certificate has a disjoint side of $f$ correct replicas. A minimal protocol attains this form. We distinguish certificate formation from acceptance, give a sufficient check using configured fault and recovery caps, and explain why durable vote records written before signature release prevent the conflict.

</details>


### [231] [A Private IPFS Data Sanctuary for Verifiable Digital Collection Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07970)
*Joachim Snellings,Jasper Funk-Smit,Margot Belot,Eran Wolff,Sebastian Baldauf,Lisa Pfeiffer,Frederik Berger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文评估了柏林自然博物馆的七节点私有IPFS部署，用于GLAM机构藏品数据的分布式暂存与内容寻址存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆、图书馆、档案馆与美术馆（GLAM）产生的科学藏品数据在采集后需保持可识别、可恢复、可验证，而单一工作站或存储路径存在依赖风险，因此需要具备冗余能力的本地暂存架构。

Method: 在柏林自然博物馆部署七节点私有IPFS：5个Raspberry Pi 5边缘节点和2个归档级节点，均配备NVMe存储，分布于两个有线区域并通过共享无线链路连接。测试内容包括：按内容标识（CID）恢复两个真实原始采集文件夹、摄入一个大型机器学习模型权重文件夹，并比较两个边缘节点和两个归档节点的持续遥测数据。

Result: 原始采集A（6.20 GB）在34.0秒内完成（有效逻辑速率182 MB/s），采集B（5.19 GB）用时14.092秒（368 MB/s）。156.1 GB模型权重文件夹递归添加约耗时300秒（逻辑摄入率520 MB/s）。四组持续遥测共记录857.3节点小时和27,616次有效块状态探测，全部报告本地块访问成功。

Conclusion: 该原型展示了一种实用的机构本地分布式文件系统（DFS），用于内容寻址的采集暂存。博物馆特有对象标识可在藏品对象层面保持稳定，而每个导入测量自动获得基于内容的状态标识。复制将相同块分布于不同故障域，内容寻址支持仓库内未变更块的复用，从而支持可验证的数字藏品对象以及GLAM机构间协作的联邦存储模型。

Abstract: Background: Galleries, libraries, archives and museums (GLAM) increasingly produce scientific collection data whose digital state must remain identifiable, recoverable, and verifiable after acquisition. A staging architecture should provide redundancy without requiring every measurement to depend on one workstation or storage path. Methods: We evaluated a private seven-node InterPlanetary File System (IPFS) deployment at the Museum für Naturkunde Berlin. Five Raspberry Pi 5 edge peers and two archive-class peers contributed NVMe-backed repositories across two wired zones linked by a shared wireless path. Two real raw-acquisition folders were recovered by content identifier (CID), a large machine-learning model-weight folder was ingested, and sustained telemetry from two edge and two archive nodes was compared. Results: Raw acquisition A (6.20 GB) materialized in 34.0 s (182 MB/s effective logical rate) and raw acquisition B (5.19 GB) in 14.092 s (368 MB/s). A 156.1 GB model-weight folder was recursively added in approximately 300 s (520 MB/s logical ingest). Four sustained telemetry series comprised 857.3 logged node-hours and 27,616 valid block-stat probes, all reporting successful local block access. Conclusion: The prototype demonstrates a practical institution-local distributed file system (DFS) for content-addressed acquisition staging. Museum-specific object identifiers can remain stable at the collection-object level while each imported measurement automatically receives a content-derived state identity. Replication intentionally distributes identical blocks across failure domains, while content addressing enables reuse of unchanged blocks within repositories. This combination supports verifiable digital collection objects and a federated storage model for collaborating GLAM institutions.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond Marginal Monitoring: Distributed Joint-Distribution Testing for Data Concept Drift in Large Scale E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08132)
*Cagdas Pullu,Mahmut Emir Arslan,Bugra Balkac,Aylin Ondersev Balta,Cihangir Celal Palaci,Fikri Cem Yilmaz,Altan Cakir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种多变量双样本漂移检测器在大规模工业数据集上的表现，发现基于Spark的分布式MMD-RFF在强漂移下表现稳健，而逐维KS检验因统计量饱和失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念漂移威胁生产环境机器学习，但多变量双样本漂移检测器在大规模场景下的实证行为尚未被充分刻画。现有基准很少涉及工业运营数据中典型的上亿行、高基数特征，缺乏对检测器可扩展性和可靠性的系统评估。

Method: 在三个互补环境（Harvard Dataverse、经校验的Failing Loudly复现、基于1.375亿行Trendyol排序特征表的新型合成注入基准）中评估五类多列双样本检验（边缘、投影和核嵌入方法），覆盖四种漂移类型和两种严重程度范围。

Result: 基于Apache Spark的分布式最大均值差异（随机傅里叶特征）表现稳健：在强范围内按四种漂移类型平均，达到r=0.940的漂移幅度相关性、80.4%真阳率和3.2%假阳率。逐维KS检验因类ID列在非对称采样下导致统计量饱和而失效。弱配置下（实际翻转比例至多0.57%）检测器难以可靠区分。

Conclusion: 分布式MMD-RFF是可扩展的稳健方案；逐维KS在大规模采样设计中存在关键约束；弱漂移配置需未来进行强度网格功效分析，以区分基本灵敏度下限与可扩展的阈值偏移。

Abstract: Concept drift threatens production machine learning, yet the empirical behavior of multivariate two-sample drift detectors at scale remains under-characterized. Existing benchmarks rarely address the hundreds of millions of rows and high-cardinality features typical of industrial-operational datasets. We evaluate five multi-column two-sample tests (marginal, projection-based, and kernel embedding methods) across three complementary environments: the Harvard Dataverse, a validated Failing Loudly reproduction (mean absolute error between 0.030 and 0.053), and a novel synthetic-injection benchmark on the 137.5-million-row Trendyol collection-ranking feature table. Testing four drift types across two severity-scope regimes, we demonstrate that distributed Maximum Mean Discrepancy with Random Fourier Features on Apache Spark scales robustly. Averaged over the four drift types in the strong regime and under a calibrated threshold, it achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.940 with expected drift magnitude, an 80.4% true positive rate, and a 3.2% false positive rate. Conversely, the per-dimension Kolmogorov-Smirnov test failed due to statistic saturation from ID-like columns under asymmetric sampling, establishing a critical constraint for large-scale sampling design. At weak configurations (realized-flip fractions of at most 0.57%), detectors struggled to reliably discriminate, highlighting the need for future intensity-grid power analyses to distinguish fundamental sensitivity bounds from scalable threshold shifts.

</details>


### [233] [GPU Acceleration of Awkward Arrays: Using Python cuda.compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08238)
*Maksym Naumchyk,Ianna Osborne*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍基于 Python CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL) 的 Awkward Array CUDA 执行模型，通过 lazy execution 与操作融合减少 kernel 启动开销，提升 HEP 中不规则嵌套数据的 GPU 分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Awkward Array 是高能物理 (HEP) 中处理嵌套、变长数据的常用 Python 库。此前基于 CUDA 的 GPU 加速虽已验证可行性，但存在不规则数据访问、细粒度 kernel 启动以及操作可组合性差等局限，因此需要一种更可组合、可持续的 GPU 后端方案。

Method: 基于 Python CCCL 构建 CUDA 执行模型，无需自定义 CUDA kernel，改用高层 Python 接口；采用 lazy execution 构建并优化表达式图，将多个 Awkward 操作融合为更少的 CUDA kernel；设计上强调可扩展性，允许用户自定义 Python 代码以极少样板代码接入 GPU 执行路径，同时保持现有分析语义不变。

Result: 针对代表性 HEP 分析模式的性能研究表明，该方案相较此前已报道的 eager GPU 执行策略取得改进。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Awkward Array 的 GPU 能力推进为更可组合、可持续的后端，契合 HL-LHC 及未来基于 Python 的分析需求。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a widely used library in high-energy physics (HEP) for representing and manipulating nested, variable-length data in Python. Previous CHEP contributions have explored GPU acceleration for Awkward Array, demonstrating the feasibility and performance benefits of CUDA-based backend while also identifying limitations related to irregular data access, fine-grained kernel launches, and composability of operations. In this contribution, we present recent developments that build directly on these earlier efforts by introducing a CUDA execution model for Awkward Array based on the Python CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL).
  Using CCCL, we eliminate the need for custom CUDA kernels and can instead use a high-level Python interface. The CCCL-based approach also enables fusion of multiple Awkward operations into a reduced number of CUDA kernels, addressing kernel launch overhead observed in earlier GPU implementations. Lazy execution allows expression graphs to be constructed and optimized prior to kernel generation, improving performance for analysis workflows involving jagged arrays, combinatorial operations, and reductions. In contrast to earlier approaches, this design also emphasizes extensibility, allowing user-defined Python code to be incorporated into GPU execution paths with minimal boilerplate and without breaking existing analysis semantics.
  We present performance studies that demonstrate improvements over previously reported eager GPU execution strategies for representative HEP analysis patterns. These developments extend the GPU capabilities of Awkward Array toward a more composable and sustainable backend, aligned with the needs of Python-based analysis at the HL-LHC and beyond.

</details>


### [234] [DySCo: Dynamic Sharding for Collaborative Edge-Cloud LLM Inference with Depth-Synchronized Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08268)
*Jingpo Xu,Paul Joe Maliakel,Ivona Brandic,Shashikant Ilager*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DySCo：面向层划分边缘-云协同推理的运行时系统，通过本地KV缓存与按层范围执行器（dyForward）结合深度同步批处理（DSB），消除异构划分带来的空闲间隙并支持异构请求共享后缀批处理，吞吐提升最高达275%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐渐支撑移动端与IoT边缘应用，但资源需求高，通常只能部署在云端。层划分的边缘-云协同推理让资源受限的边缘设备参与计算。然而异构划分点带来两种耦合的低效：一是边缘执行与通信在云调用之间造成空闲间隙；二是到达模型不同深度的请求无法按常规方式批处理。

Method: 提出 DySCo 协同运行时：保持 KV 缓存本地化；引入 dyForward，一种模型感知的层范围执行器，可从常驻模型分片中运行可配置的连续层范围而无需重新加载权重。针对多边缘服务场景，提出深度同步批处理（DSB），将异构请求推进到最深切分点，并对其公共后缀进行批处理。

Result: 在异构设备、两个模型家族以及本地和广域链路上实验：即使排除等待时间，空闲间隙仍会增加后续GPU前向调用的延迟，每解码步最多增加25 ms云端后缀延迟。平均并发为8时，DSB相比FIFO吞吐提升275%，相比精确匹配批处理提升48%，相比轮询交错提升79%，同时降低每会话平均延迟。

Conclusion: 具有不同边缘-云切分点的请求可以复用常驻云端权重并共享批处理后缀计算，验证了DySCo在层划分协同推理中的有效性。工件仓库已公开。

Abstract: Pervasive intelligent applications are increasingly deployed on mobile and Internet of Things (IoT) edge devices. Consequently, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support these applications. Yet, due to their high resource demands, LLMs are mostly deployed in the cloud. Layer-wise edge-cloud inference lets resource-constrained edge devices contribute computation to LLMs they cannot host in full. However, heterogeneous split points introduce two coupled inefficiencies. First, edge execution and communication create idle gaps between cloud invocations. Second, requests arriving at different model depths cannot be conventionally batched. We present DySCo, a collaborative runtime that keeps KV caches local and introduces dyForward, a model-aware layer-range executor that runs configurable contiguous layer ranges from resident model shards without reloading weights. For multi-edge serving settings, we introduce depth-synchronized batching (DSB), which advances heterogeneous requests to the deepest cut and batches their common suffix. Experiments across heterogeneous devices, two model families, and local and wide-area links show that idle gaps increase the latency of subsequent GPU forward calls even when waiting time is excluded, adding up to 25 ms of additional cloud-side suffix latency per decoding step in our measurements. At an average concurrency of eight, DSB improves throughput by 275% over FIFO, 48% over exact-match batching, and 79% over round-robin interleaving while reducing mean per-session latency. Together, these results show that requests with different edge-cloud splits can reuse resident cloud weights and share batched suffix computation. The artifact repository for this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/Large-scale-Sustainable-Computing-LSC/dysco-artifact

</details>


### [235] [vTen: Tensor-Centric Verification Framework for Domain-Specific Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08372)
*Chanmin Baek,Keehyuk Lee,Mincheol Cha,Somi Hong,Xuan Truong Nguyen,Hyuk-Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: vTen 是一个以数据为中心的 DSA 验证框架，通过声明式 DSL 与内核粒度批处理，将验证意图与执行机制解耦，在 3D U-Net 加速器上实现 2 倍仿真加速与 60.3% 代码复杂度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量为中心的软件模型与信号级硬件测试平台之间存在语义鸿沟，造成 DSA 验证的生产力瓶颈；现有 Cocotb 等框架因细粒度交互导致同步开销过高。

Method: 提出 vTen 数据为中心框架，严格将验证意图与执行机制解耦，采用声明式 DSL 和内核粒度批处理，最小化主机与仿真器的交互频率。

Result: 在生产级 3D U-Net 加速器上评估，相比 Cocotb，仿真延迟性能提升 2 倍，代码复杂度降低 60.3%。

Conclusion: vTen 有效缓解了验证过程中的同步开销与复杂度问题，为 DSA 验证提供了更高效的数据中心化方案。

Abstract: The semantic gap between tensor-centric software models and signal-level hardware testbenches creates significant productivity bottlenecks in verifying Domain-Specific Accelerators (DSAs). Existing frameworks like Cocotb suffer from prohibitive synchronization overheads due to fine-grained interactions. To address this, we propose vTen, a data-centric framework that strictly decouples verification intent from execution mechanics. By leveraging a declarative DSL and kernel-granular batching, vTen minimizes host-simulator interaction frequency. Evaluation on a production-scale 3D U-Net accelerator demonstrates that vTen achieves a 2x performance improvement in simulation latency and a 60.3% reduction in code complexity compared to Cocotb.

</details>


### [236] [NeMo-DCR: Bit-Exact Delta-Compressed Refit for Scalable Agentic RL at Trillion-Parameter Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08430)
*Songlin Jiang,Zhiyu Li,Terry Kong,Yu Yao,Youngeun Kwon,Bernard Nguyen,Ashwath Aithal,Mario Di Francesco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NeMo-DCR 通过只传输权重变化（delta）实现位精确的跨集群权重同步，在 30B-1T 模型上比全量 checkpoint 传输快 12-40 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RL 将训练与 rollout 分离，每次策略更新都需要把权重同步到 rollout 集群。传输完整 1T checkpoint 耗时 87.5 分钟，成为瓶颈。现有稀疏性利用系统在布局映射、精确性和效率上均有不足，且无法从 refit 中途失败中完全恢复。

Method: 提出 NeMo-DCR（增量压缩 refit），仅发送变化但保持位精确。布局上，用固定仿射映射将变化从训练分片投影到 checkpoint 规范坐标，残余转换覆盖其余变化，服务运行时原生加载器完成放置。精确性上，用可压缩 XOR 掩码承载仿射变化（投影与加载器保留存储位），用覆写承载其余变化；接收端原位应用，重试覆写部分写入，联合提交绑定策略与基线。效率上，通过对象存储或中继树在 delta 构建期间流式传输，无需跨集群集合通信。

Result: 即使在 3% 和 5% 的变化率下，30B-1T 模型的 NeMo-DCR refit 也比仅传输的全量 checkpoint 参考快 12-40 倍。1T 中继树 refit 在 3% 变化率下仅需 150 秒，而非 87.5 分钟。

Conclusion: NeMo-DCR 使跨集群 agentic RL 在万亿参数规模下的 refit 变得实用。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) disaggregates training from rollout, so each policy update must reach the rollout clusters before the next batch. Transferring a full 1T checkpoint for such weight synchronization (refit) takes 87.5 min between two AWS regions. Measurements of BF16 training show that about 1% of weights change their stored values per step. Recent systems exploit this sparsity but fall short on placement, exactness, or efficiency: they reimplement placement rules, assemble full tensors, rebuild values arithmetically, or use a cross-cluster collective, and none fully recovers from mid-refit failures.
  We present NeMo-DCR (Delta-Compressed Refit), which sends only changes yet is bit-exact: receivers obtain the same parameter and buffer bits as a dense refit. For placement, fixed affine mappings project changes from training shards into the checkpoint's canonical coordinates, residual conversion covers the other changes, and the serving runtime's native loader places all changes in receiver storage. For exactness, compressible XOR masks carry affine changes whose projection and loader preserve stored bits, and overwrites carry the others. Receivers apply both in place, retries overwrite partial writes, and a joint commit binds the policy to the baseline for the next delta. For efficiency, object storage or a relay tree streams payloads during delta construction, without a cross-cluster collective. Even at 3% and 5% change rates, NeMo-DCR refits of 30B-1T models are 12-40$\times$ faster than a transport-only full-checkpoint reference. A 1T relay-tree refit at 3% takes 150 s instead of 87.5 min, making refits practical for cross-cluster agentic RL at trillion-parameter scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Beyond Successor Accuracy: State Retention for Recursive Self-Improvement in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07105)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Zheng Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Shujie Li,Edith Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"分布式进展"(distributed progress)概念与跨代优势(CGA)指标，揭示推荐递归自我改进(Rec-RSI)中进步可能存在于代际关系而非仅最新模型，并提出无标签的秩分离统计量来选择保留哪一代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rec-RSI将推荐输出反馈到后续训练中。仅以最新模型评估每轮训练，默认后继模型能完全巩固更新，但更新前后的模型可能保留互补的排序决策，因此需要新的评估与保留视角。

Method: 提出"分布式进展"概念及跨代优势(CGA)——一种跨代与代内模型对的边际匹配对比；引入秩分离统计量，在选型时无需标签即可预测应保留哪个模型族。

Result: 在4个数据集和3种序列推荐编码器上，偏好保留机制随架构变化：GRU4Rec与SASRec受益于跨代配对，FMLP初始偏向代内配对，第二次更新后转向跨代。秩分离在首轮12/12、次轮5/6的设置中选出更优族；留出测试中，所选族在34/36轨迹上优于直接后继。五种迁移机制无法在单一模型中稳定复现这些收益。

Conclusion: 状态保留是Rec-RSI的一个独立问题：进展可能存在于代际关系之间，而不只是最新模型中。

Abstract: Recommendation recursive self-improvement (Rec-RSI) feeds recommender outputs into subsequent training. Evaluating each round solely through its latest model assumes that the successor consolidates the update, although pre- and post-update models may retain complementary ranking decisions. We term this \emph{distributed progress} and quantify it using cross-generation advantage (CGA), a marginally matched contrast between cross- and within-generation model pairs. A rank-separation statistic, label-free at selection time, predicts which family to retain. Across four datasets and three sequential recommendation encoders, the preferred retention regime varies by architecture: cross-generation pairing benefits GRU4Rec and SASRec, whereas FMLP initially favors within-generation pairing and shifts toward cross-generation pairing after a second update. Rank separation selects the stronger family in 12/12 first-update and 5/6 second-update dataset-encoder settings; on held-out tests, the selected family outperforms the direct successor in 34/36 trajectories. Five transfer mechanisms do not consistently reproduce these gains in one model. These findings establish state retention as a distinct Rec-RSI problem: progress may reside in relations between generations as well as in the latest model. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Jinfeng-Xu/RecRSI}{https://github.com/Jinfeng-Xu/RecRSI}.

</details>


### [238] [RAGFlip: Measuring Query-Level Negative Flips in Retriever Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07266)
*Elyas Irankhah,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究检索器升级中的"负翻转"问题：即BM25原本能正确检索到相关段落的查询，在替换为更强检索器后反而检索失败。作者在三个BEIR数据集上评估了BGE-large、E5-large-v2和SPLADE，发现负翻转在所有设置中都存在且因语料、检索器和深度而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索器升级通常用聚合指标评估，这会掩盖那些此前已被正确服务的查询上的性能退化。聚合指标的提升可能掩盖查询级别的兼容性损失，因此需要专门研究负翻转现象来揭示这一问题。

Method: 作者定义"负翻转"为：BM25能检索到已判定的相关段落而替换检索器不能的查询。在三个BEIR数据集（Natural Questions、HotpotQA、FiQA）上，跨五个检索深度，评估BGE-large、E5-large-v2和SPLADE三种替换检索器。使用"任意相关"支持标签，并在HotpotQA上测试了要求所有正qrel段落更严格的标签版本，还进行了固定预算的BM25组合实验和HotpotQA阅读器下游验证实验。

Result: 所有替换检索器都提升了整体检索覆盖率，但负翻转在每个设置中都出现，且随语料、检索器和深度显著变化。在k=1时，8.6%–37.5%的BM25成功案例被丢失。较大深度设置下负翻转率较低。在HotpotQA的k=10上，要求所有正qrel段落时负翻转率升至12.3%–17.3%。简单的BM25固定预算组合能减少这些退化，HotpotQA阅读器实验显示部分检索翻转伴随答案退化。

Conclusion: 更强的检索器虽提升整体指标，却会在查询级别丢失此前正确的检索结果，这种现象普遍存在且被聚合指标掩盖。因此，评估检索器更新时应同时考虑查询级兼容性与聚合检索质量。

Abstract: Retriever upgrades are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics, which can hide regressions on queries the previous retriever already served correctly. We study these regressions as negative flips: queries for which BM25 retrieves a judged relevant passage and the replacement does not. We evaluate BGE-large, E5-large-v2, and SPLADE on three BEIR collections: Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and FiQA, across five retrieval depths. All replacements improve overall retrieval coverage. Negative flips occur in every setting and vary substantially by corpus, retriever, and depth. At k=1, 8.6-37.5% of BM25 successes are lost across the evaluated settings. Negative-flip rates are lower in the larger-depth settings, where the BM25-supported cohort is defined separately at each depth. These rates use the any-relevant support label. On HotpotQA at k=10, requiring every positive qrel passage raises the negative-flip rate to 12.3-17.3%. Simple fixed-budget combinations with BM25 reduce these regressions, and a HotpotQA reader experiment provides a limited downstream check in which some retrieval flips are accompanied by answer regressions. These results motivate evaluating retriever updates using query-level compatibility alongside aggregate retrieval quality.

</details>


### [239] [Rethinking Semantic ID Construction for Generative Recommendation: SimHash with Parallel Decoding and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07402)
*Yuqing Liu,Huiyuan Chen,Yibo Wang,Wooseong Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"哈希式语义ID必然劣于学习式量化"的共识，提出免训练的FLASH框架，通过并行解码与显式语义对齐让SimHash达到SOTA，证明简单高效的分词器在生成式推荐中可与复杂学习方案媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中，语义ID需兼顾语义表达力与计算效率。学术界普遍认为基于哈希（如SimHash）的简单方法本质上不如复杂的学习式量化方法。作者对此共识提出质疑，认为性能差距并非源于哈希本身的局限，而是源于与自回归解码的结构性不匹配，以及刚性离散化造成的信息损失。

Method: 提出FLASH两阶段框架：一是采用并行解码（parallel decoding）替代自回归解码，解决哈希tokenization与解码方式的结构性错配；二是引入显式语义对齐（explicit semantic alignment）机制以缓解刚性离散化带来的信息损失。整体方案无需任何tokenizer训练，属于training-free的SimHash分词方法。

Result: FLASH在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能，且无需训练tokenizer，并在冷启动场景下展现出更强的泛化能力。实验还表明语义对齐是一种跨多种范式的通用有效机制。

Conclusion: 哈希式简单分词器的性能劣势并非固有，而是源于解码兼容性与语义基础缺失。在匹配的解码方式与语义对齐下，简单高效的分词器可在生成式推荐中达到与复杂学习方案相当的性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Semantic ID-based generative recommendation represents each item as a sequence of discrete tokens, enabling structured modeling of item semantics. A critical challenge is constructing semantic IDs that are both semantically expressive and computationally efficient. While recent approaches favor complex learned quantization, simple hashing-based methods such as SimHash are widely regarded as fundamentally inferior. In this work, we challenge this consensus by showing that the apparent performance gap does not stem from inherent limitations of hashing, but rather from a structural mismatch with autoregressive decoding, coupled with the inevitable information loss during rigid discretization. Based on this insight, we propose FLASH, a two-stage framework that revitalizes training-free SimHash tokenization through parallel decoding and explicit semantic alignment. Despite its simplicity, FLASH achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets without requiring any tokenizer training, while exhibiting stronger generalization in cold-start scenarios. Notably, we demonstrate that semantic alignment acts as a universally effective mechanism across diverse paradigms. Our findings suggest that, with compatible decoding and semantic grounding, simple and efficient tokenizers can achieve performance comparable to complex learned counterparts in generative recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/KevinC2015/Flash.

</details>


### [240] [A Study of Prior Case Retrieval Using Lexical, Semantic, and Rhetorical Role Information in Indian Legal Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07437)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu,Aniket Mani,Rishu Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对印度法律判决的先例检索（IL PCR）任务，评估了三种连续设计的检索系统。研究发现：结合多种修辞角色（事实、判决理由、先例、论证、法条）优于单一角色或全文检索；法条相似度不具区分性；基于LLM训练数据的法律蕴含重排序器在官方评测中远差于内部验证分数。作者提出带查询不相交划分和冻结验证融合的新设计，最终四阶段流水线达到Micro F1 0.2549、MRR 0.6081、nDCG@10 0.4187。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度法律先例检索的关键难点在于区分真正相关的法律事实与仅表面相似的词汇。与查询判决共享法条的先前案例未必相关，因此需要超越单纯的词汇相似度。本文旨在通过实证评估解决这一问题，并探索更可靠的检索系统设计。

Method: 作者对IL PCR任务的三代连续检索系统设计进行了实证评估：比较了修辞角色（事实、判决理由、先例、论证、法条）组合与单一角色及全文检索的效果；检验法条相似度的区分能力；评估基于LLM生成训练数据的法律蕴含重排序器。最后提出包含查询不相交划分（query disjoint splitting）与冻结验证融合（frozen validation fusion）的新设计，构建四阶段流水线。

Result: 实验表明：修辞角色组合优于单一角色和全文检索；法条相似度不具区分性；LLM训练的法律蕴含重排序器官方评测表现远差于内部验证分数。两个内部MRR高达0.97的重排序器官方MRR低至0.14。最终四阶段流水线取得Micro F1 0.2549、MRR 0.6081、nDCG@10 0.4187。

Conclusion: 内部验证分数与官方评测结果之间存在显著差距，仅依赖内部验证不可靠。采用查询不相交划分与冻结验证融合的新设计能有效缓解该问题，四阶段流水线在官方指标上取得相对稳定的表现，为印度法律先例检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: For retrieving prior cases in Indian legal judgments, the problem involves distinguishing relevant legal facts from mere lexical similarities because a prior case that shares a statute with the query judgment is not necessarily relevant. In this paper, we describe an empirical evaluation of three consecutive designs of retrieval systems for the IL PCR(Indian Legal Prior Case Retrieval) task. We demonstrate that the combination of the rhetorical roles (Fact, Ratio Of The Decision, Precedent, Argument, Statute) is better than either using individual roles or performing full text retrieval, that statute similarity is not discriminative, and that a legal entailment reranker with training data produced by an LLM is much worse in terms of official evaluation than its internal validation score. Two rankers, with excellent internal MRR up to 0.97, performed poorly in terms of official evaluation (MRR as low as 0.14). Thus, we propose a new design with query disjoint splitting and frozen validation fusion. The result is a four stage pipeline with Micro F1 0.2549, MRR 0.6081, and nDCG@10 0.4187.

</details>


### [241] [DBRAG: Multi-Table Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Complex Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07622)
*Prince Larbi Ampofo,Ryoji Kubo,Djellel Difallah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DBRAG，一个面向多表问答的检索增强生成框架，先离线索引检索候选表、用查询相关行丰富摘要并由LLM重排，再由程序辅助推理器选表并执行操作，在Spider、GeoQuery、ATIS数据集上提升了表检索与多表问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的表格推理方法多局限于单表场景，或假设相关表已被提供；而实际用户常对整个数据库发出复杂数据探索查询，相关信息可能分散于多个关系之中，因此需要支持多表问答的框架。

Method: DBRAG包含几个步骤：(1) 使用离线表索引检索候选表；(2) 用查询相关的行丰富候选表的摘要；(3) 用LLM对候选表重排；(4) 程序辅助推理器选择所需表并在其完整内容上执行操作，从而保持初始提示上下文紧凑。

Result: 在Spider、GeoQuery和ATIS数据集上的实验表明，DBRAG在表检索和多表问答两方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: DBRAG通过检索增强与程序辅助推理相结合，有效应对多表问答中相关表分散的问题，并保持提示上下文紧凑，验证了该框架在多表场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have introduced new capabilities for reasoning over structured data, particularly through program-aided tools that can analyze tables. However, many existing methods address single-table scenarios or assume that the relevant tables are already provided. In practice, users often issue complex data exploration queries over entire databases, where relevant information may be distributed across multiple relations. In this work, we introduce DBRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework tailored for multi-table question answering. DBRAG first retrieves candidate tables using an offline table index, enriches their summaries with query-relevant rows, and uses an LLM to rerank the candidates. A program-aided reasoner then selects the required tables and executes operations over their full contents, keeping the initial prompt context compact. Experiments on the Spider, GeoQuery, and ATIS datasets used in this study demonstrate improvements in table retrieval and multi-table question answering.

</details>


### [242] [Learning to Retrieve via Reinforcement Learning in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07731)
*Qi Liu,Fengming Liang,Yiqun Chen,Erhan Zhang,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RELER，一个用强化学习直接优化检索/下游任务奖励的框架，使现有嵌入模型能直接在嵌入空间学习检索，并在BRIGHT及RAG任务上优于InfoNCE与LambdaLoss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索模型通常用对比目标训练，虽能学到有效表示，但并不直接优化检索指标或下游任务表现，因此存在目标不匹配的问题。

Method: 从以归一化编码器输出为中心的von Mises-Fisher分布采样单位长度的查询与文档嵌入动作，以检索或下游结果为奖励，用REINFORCE结合留一法基线（RLOO）更新编码器；并提出条件均值投影（CMP），将采样嵌入投影到由其编码器输出与对比候选嵌入张成的低维子空间，在保持期望的同时降低策略梯度噪声。

Result: 在BRIGHT基准上，RELER在后训练BGE-M3与Qwen3-Embedding骨干时，平均nDCG@10持续优于InfoNCE和LambdaLoss；在RAG设置下（仅适配查询编码器，固定文档索引与生成器），于七个QA数据集上联合优化检索与答案奖励，同时提升了平均检索性能和答案质量。

Conclusion: 用强化学习直接优化检索与下游奖励是可行且有效的，CMP能缓解高维嵌入空间探索的采样噪声，为对齐任务特定目标提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models are typically trained with contrastive objectives that learn effective representations but do not directly optimize retrieval metrics or downstream task performance. To address this problem, we introduce RELER (REinforcement LEarning for Retrieval), a reinforcement learning framework that enables existing embedding models to learn to retrieve directly in embedding space and align to task-specific rewards. We train RELER by sampling unit-length query and document embedding actions from von Mises-Fisher (vMF) distributions centered on normalized encoder outputs, scoring the resulting retrieval or downstream outcomes as rewards, and updating the encoder with REINFORCE using a leave-one-out baseline (RLOO). As exploration in the high-dimensional embedding space is prone to sampling noise, we further propose conditional-mean projection (CMP), which projects each sampled embedding onto the low-dimensional subspace spanned by its encoder output and the candidate embeddings it is compared against, reducing noise in the policy gradient while preserving its expectation. We evaluate RELER on BRIGHT, a benchmark with reasoning-intensive queries that remain challenging for existing embedding models. RELER consistently outperforms InfoNCE and LambdaLoss in average nDCG@10 when post-training BGE-M3 and Qwen3-Embedding backbones. We further evaluate downstream utility through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where we adapt only the query encoder while keeping the document index and generator fixed. Across seven QA datasets, jointly optimizing retrieval and answer rewards improves both average retrieval performance and answer quality in RAG.

</details>


### [243] [Token-Budgeted Escalation for Financial Document QA: Cost Is Predictable, Benefit Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07760)
*Junru Zhu,Yixin Yang,Xiaoqing Ding,Ruoyu Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将金融文档问答中的"选择性升级"建模为有限批次预算分配问题：对150个FinanceBench问题先做top-1检索，再由预测器估计top-5额外调用的收益与token成本，并按"预测收益/token"排序分配调用。在10%预算下，该策略比仅按收益排序提升裁定质量0.034，同时比一次性top-5检索节省46.6% token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
